<file path="automation/meeting_scheduler.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Meeting Scheduler Module
Handles meeting schedule persistence, CRUD operations, background trigger engine,
and conversational intent parsing for voice/chat-based schedule management.
"""

import json
import os
import re
import uuid
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, date
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List

# APScheduler for background trigger engine
try:
    from apscheduler.schedulers.background import BackgroundScheduler
    from apscheduler.triggers.date import DateTrigger
    APSCHEDULER_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    APSCHEDULER_AVAILABLE = False
    print("[!] APScheduler not installed. Meeting auto-join disabled.")


# ─── Schema ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Each meeting entry:
# {
#   "id": "uuid",
#   "title": "Team Standup",
#   "platform": "google_meet" | "teams",
#   "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij",
#   "start_time": "2026-10-12T14:00:00",   (ISO 8601 local)
#   "end_time":   "2026-10-12T15:00:00",
#   "auto_leave": true,
#   "status": "pending" | "active" | "completed" | "cancelled"
# }
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Paths
BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
SCHEDULE_FILE = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "config", "meetings.json")


class MeetingScheduler:
    """Manages meeting schedules with JSON persistence and APScheduler triggers."""

    def __init__(self, meeting_bot=None):
        """
        Args:
            meeting_bot: Optional MeetingBot instance. When provided the scheduler
                         auto-triggers bot.join_meeting() ahead of each event.
        """
        self.meeting_bot = meeting_bot
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._meetings: list[dict] = []

        # Ensure config dir exists
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(SCHEDULE_FILE), exist_ok=True)

        # Load persisted schedule
        self._load()

        # Background scheduler
        self.scheduler = None
        if APSCHEDULER_AVAILABLE:
            self.scheduler = BackgroundScheduler(daemon=True)
            self.scheduler.start()
            # Register periodic poller to catch imminent/due meetings every 10s
            self.scheduler.add_job(
                func=self._poll_and_trigger_due_meetings,
                trigger="interval",
                seconds=10,
                id="meeting_poll_daemon",
                replace_existing=True,
            )
            # Re-schedule all future pending meetings that were persisted
            self._reschedule_all_pending()

    # ─── Persistence ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _load(self):
        """Load meetings from JSON file."""
        if os.path.exists(SCHEDULE_FILE):
            try:
                with open(SCHEDULE_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._meetings = json.load(f)
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError):
                self._meetings = []
        else:
            self._meetings = []

    def _save(self):
        """Persist meetings to JSON file."""
        with open(SCHEDULE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(self._meetings, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    # ─── CRUD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def add_meeting(self, title: str, meeting_url: str,
                    start_time: datetime, end_time: datetime = None,
                    auto_leave: bool = True) -> dict:
        """Add a new meeting and schedule its trigger."""
        platform = self._detect_platform(meeting_url)
        if end_time is None or end_time <= start_time:
            end_time = start_time + timedelta(hours=1)

        meeting = {
            "id": str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
            "title": title,
            "platform": platform,
            "meeting_url": meeting_url,
            "start_time": start_time.isoformat(),
            "end_time": end_time.isoformat(),
            "auto_leave": auto_leave,
            "status": "pending",
        }

        with self._lock:
            self._meetings.append(meeting)
            self._save()

        # Schedule the trigger
        self._schedule_trigger(meeting)
        return meeting

    def cancel_meeting(self, meeting_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mark a meeting as cancelled and remove its trigger."""
        with self._lock:
            for m in self._meetings:
                if m["id"] == meeting_id:
                    m["status"] = "cancelled"
                    self._save()
                    # Remove APScheduler job
                    if self.scheduler:
                        try:
                            self.scheduler.remove_job(f"meeting_{meeting_id}")
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    return True
        return False

    def cancel_next_pending(self) -> dict | None:
        """Cancel the nearest pending meeting. Returns cancelled entry or None."""
        pending = self.get_pending()
        if not pending:
            return None
        target = pending[0]
        self.cancel_meeting(target["id"])
        return target

    def mark_active(self, meeting_id: str):
        """Set status to active."""
        with self._lock:
            for m in self._meetings:
                if m["id"] == meeting_id:
                    m["status"] = "active"
                    self._save()
                    return

    def mark_completed(self, meeting_id: str):
        """Set status to completed."""
        with self._lock:
            for m in self._meetings:
                if m["id"] == meeting_id:
                    m["status"] = "completed"
                    self._save()
                    return

    def get_all(self) -> list[dict]:
        return list(self._meetings)

    def get_pending(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Return pending meetings sorted by start_time (soonest first)."""
        now = datetime.now().isoformat()
        return sorted(
            [m for m in self._meetings if m["status"] == "pending" and m["start_time"] >= now],
            key=lambda m: m["start_time"],
        )

    def get_today(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Return today's meetings (all statuses)."""
        today = datetime.now().date().isoformat()
        return [
            m for m in self._meetings
            if m["start_time"].startswith(today)
        ]

    def get_active(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Return currently active meetings."""
        return [m for m in self._meetings if m["status"] == "active"]

    def get_by_id(self, meeting_id: str) -> dict | None:
        for m in self._meetings:
            if m["id"] == meeting_id:
                return m
        return None

    def bulk_import(self, entries: list[dict]) -> int:
        """Import a list of meeting dicts. Returns count of successfully imported."""
        count = 0
        for entry in entries:
            try:
                st_raw = entry["start_time"]
                if isinstance(st_raw, str):
                    start = datetime.fromisoformat(st_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                elif isinstance(st_raw, datetime):
                    start = st_raw
                else:
                    continue

                if start.tzinfo is not None:
                    start = start.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)

                et_raw = entry.get("end_time")
                end = None
                if isinstance(et_raw, str):
                    end = datetime.fromisoformat(et_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                elif isinstance(et_raw, datetime):
                    end = et_raw

                if end is not None and end.tzinfo is not None:
                    end = end.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)

                self.add_meeting(
                    title=entry.get("title", "Untitled Meeting"),
                    meeting_url=entry.get("meeting_url", ""),
                    start_time=start,
                    end_time=end,
                    auto_leave=entry.get("auto_leave", True),
                )
                count += 1
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Meeting Import] Skipping invalid entry: {e}")
        return count

    # ─── Platform Detection ───────────────────────────────────────

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_platform(url: str) -> str:
        if "meet.google.com" in url:
            return "google_meet"
        elif "teams.microsoft.com" in url or "teams.live.com" in url:
            return "teams"
        return "unknown"

    # ─── APScheduler Trigger Engine ───────────────────────────────

    def _schedule_trigger(self, meeting: dict):
        """Schedule the bot to join 60 seconds before start_time."""
        if not self.scheduler or not self.meeting_bot:
            return

        start = datetime.fromisoformat(meeting["start_time"])
        end = datetime.fromisoformat(meeting["end_time"]) if meeting.get("end_time") else start + timedelta(hours=1)
        now = datetime.now()
        trigger_time = start - timedelta(seconds=60)

        # Don't schedule past events that are completely over
        if now >= end:
            return

        # If meeting is starting now, imminent, or already in progress: trigger immediately
        if trigger_time <= now < end:
            if meeting.get("status") == "pending":
                print(f"[Scheduler] Meeting '{meeting['title']}' is starting now or in progress -- auto-joining immediately!")
                threading.Thread(target=self._trigger_join, args=[meeting], daemon=True).start()
            return

        self.scheduler.add_job(
            func=self._trigger_join,
            trigger=DateTrigger(run_date=trigger_time),
            id=f"meeting_{meeting['id']}",
            args=[meeting],
            replace_existing=True,
            misfire_grace_time=300,
        )
        print(f"[Scheduler] Trigger set for '{meeting['title']}' at {trigger_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}")

    def _poll_and_trigger_due_meetings(self):
        """Periodic poller running every 10s ensuring imminent or due meetings are never missed."""
        if not self.meeting_bot:
            return
        now = datetime.now()
        with self._lock:
            # Refresh from disk
            self._load()
            for m in list(self._meetings):
                if m.get("status") == "pending":
                    try:
                        start = datetime.fromisoformat(m["start_time"])
                        end = datetime.fromisoformat(m["end_time"]) if m.get("end_time") else start + timedelta(hours=1)
                        if now >= end:
                            m["status"] = "completed"
                            self._save()
                        elif (start - timedelta(seconds=60)) <= now < end:
                            if self.meeting_bot.is_in_meeting():
                                continue
                            print(f"[Scheduler Poller] Auto-joining due meeting: '{m['title']}'")
                            threading.Thread(target=self._trigger_join, args=[m], daemon=True).start()
                    except Exception as e:
                        pass

    def _reschedule_all_pending(self):
        """Re-register triggers for all future pending meetings (on startup)."""
        for m in self.get_pending():
            self._schedule_trigger(m)

    def _trigger_join(self, meeting: dict):
        """Callback fired by APScheduler or poller — launches the Playwright bot."""
        with self._lock:
            if meeting.get("status") != "pending":
                return
            meeting["status"] = "active"
            self._save()

        print(f"\n[Scheduler] [>] Auto-joining '{meeting['title']}'")
        try:
            end_time = datetime.fromisoformat(meeting["end_time"]) if meeting.get("end_time") else None
            self.meeting_bot.join_meeting(
                url=meeting["meeting_url"],
                end_time=end_time,
                auto_leave=meeting.get("auto_leave", True),
                title=meeting.get("title", "Online Meeting"),
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Scheduler] Join failed: {e}")
        finally:
            self.mark_completed(meeting["id"])

    # ─── Conversational Intent Parsing ────────────────────────────

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_target_date(text: str, ref_date) -> Optional[date]:
        """Parse natural end date like '13th nov', 'nov 13', '2026-11-13'."""
        text = text.strip().rstrip('.,;)"\'')
        # ISO / standard date formats
        for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d", "%Y/%m/%d", "%d-%m-%Y", "%d/%m/%Y"):
            try:
                return datetime.strptime(text, fmt).date()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Strip ordinals: 13th -> 13
        cleaned = re.sub(r'(\d+)(st|nd|rd|th)', r'\1', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()
        month_map = {
            "jan": 1, "january": 1, "feb": 2, "february": 2, "mar": 3, "march": 3,
            "apr": 4, "april": 4, "may": 5, "jun": 6, "june": 6, "jul": 7, "july": 7,
            "aug": 8, "august": 8, "sep": 9, "september": 9, "oct": 10, "october": 10,
            "nov": 11, "november": 11, "dec": 12, "december": 12,
        }

        # Pattern: "13 nov" or "13 november"
        m1 = re.search(r'(\d{1,2})\s*([a-zA-Z]+)', cleaned)
        if m1:
            day = int(m1.group(1))
            m_str = m1.group(2).lower()
            m_num = month_map.get(m_str[:3]) or month_map.get(m_str)
            if m_num:
                y = ref_date.year
                try:
                    d = datetime(y, m_num, day).date()
                    if d < ref_date:
                        d = datetime(y + 1, m_num, day).date()
                    return d
                except Exception:
                    pass

        # Pattern: "nov 13" or "november 13"
        m2 = re.search(r'([a-zA-Z]+)\s*(\d{1,2})', cleaned)
        if m2:
            m_str = m2.group(1).lower()
            day = int(m2.group(2))
            m_num = month_map.get(m_str[:3]) or month_map.get(m_str)
            if m_num:
                y = ref_date.year
                try:
                    d = datetime(y, m_num, day).date()
                    if d < ref_date:
                        d = datetime(y + 1, m_num, day).date()
                    return d
                except Exception:
                    pass

        return None

    def parse_schedule_command(self, command: str) -> dict | None:
        """
        Parse a natural-language scheduling command and return meeting dict.
        Supports:
            - URLs with quotes or punctuation stripped
            - Quoted titles and smart title extraction
            - Duration ('for 45 minutes', 'for 2 hours')
            - Recurrence ('daily', 'every day', 'till 13th nov', 'for 5 days')
        """
        # Extract URL and strip trailing quotes or punctuation
        url_match = re.search(
            r'(https?://(?:meet\.google\.com|teams\.microsoft\.com|teams\.live\.com)\S+)',
            command, re.IGNORECASE,
        )
        if not url_match:
            return None
        meeting_url = url_match.group(1).rstrip('"\'.,;)>]')

        # Extract time
        start_time = self._parse_time(command)
        if start_time is None:
            return None

        # Extract duration
        duration_minutes = 60
        dur_match = re.search(r'\bfor\s+(\d+)\s*(minute|minutes|min|mins|hour|hours|hr|hrs)\b', command, re.IGNORECASE)
        if dur_match:
            val = int(dur_match.group(1))
            unit = dur_match.group(2).lower()
            if unit.startswith("hour") or unit.startswith("hr"):
                duration_minutes = val * 60
            else:
                duration_minutes = val
        end_time = start_time + timedelta(minutes=duration_minutes)

        # Extract recurrence
        recurrence_days = 1
        if re.search(r'\b(daily|every\s+day)\b', command, re.IGNORECASE):
            for_days_match = re.search(r'for\s+(\d+)\s+days?', command, re.IGNORECASE)
            if for_days_match:
                recurrence_days = min(int(for_days_match.group(1)), 30)
            else:
                till_match = re.search(r'\b(?:till|until)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9\s\-_,]+)', command, re.IGNORECASE)
                if till_match:
                    target_date = self._parse_target_date(till_match.group(1), start_time.date())
                    if target_date:
                        diff = (target_date - start_time.date()).days + 1
                        recurrence_days = max(1, min(diff, 30))
                    else:
                        recurrence_days = 7
                else:
                    recurrence_days = 7

        # Extract title
        title = self._extract_title(command)

        return {
            "title": title,
            "meeting_url": meeting_url,
            "start_time": start_time,
            "end_time": end_time,
            "duration_minutes": duration_minutes,
            "recurrence_days": recurrence_days,
        }

    def _parse_time(self, text: str) -> datetime | None:
        """
        Parse time from natural language.
        Supports: "at 2:30 PM", "at 14:00", "in 15 minutes", "in 1 hour"
        """
        now = datetime.now()

        # Relative: "in X minutes/hours"
        rel_match = re.search(r'in\s+(\d+)\s+(minute|minutes|min|mins|hour|hours|hr|hrs)', text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if rel_match:
            amount = int(rel_match.group(1))
            unit = rel_match.group(2).lower()
            if unit.startswith("hour") or unit.startswith("hr"):
                return now + timedelta(hours=amount)
            else:
                return now + timedelta(minutes=amount)

        # Absolute: "at HH:MM AM/PM" or "at HH:MM"
        abs_match = re.search(r'at\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(am|pm)?', text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if abs_match:
            hour = int(abs_match.group(1))
            minute = int(abs_match.group(2))
            period = abs_match.group(3)
            if period:
                if period.lower() == "pm" and hour != 12:
                    hour += 12
                elif period.lower() == "am" and hour == 12:
                    hour = 0
            target = now.replace(hour=hour, minute=minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
            # If the time has already passed today, schedule for tomorrow
            if target <= now:
                target += timedelta(days=1)
            return target

        # Bare hour: "at 2 PM"
        bare_match = re.search(r'at\s+(\d{1,2})\s*(am|pm)', text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if bare_match:
            hour = int(bare_match.group(1))
            period = bare_match.group(2)
            if period.lower() == "pm" and hour != 12:
                hour += 12
            elif period.lower() == "am" and hour == 12:
                hour = 0
            target = now.replace(hour=hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
            if target <= now:
                target += timedelta(days=1)
            return target

        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_title(command: str) -> str:
        """Extract the meeting title from the command string."""
        # 1. Check for quoted title following named/called/titled
        named_q = re.search(r'(?:named|called|titled)\s+["\']([^"\']+)["\']', command, re.IGNORECASE)
        if named_q:
            return named_q.group(1).strip()

        # 2. Check for any quoted string that is not a URL and not a time
        for q in re.findall(r'["\']([^"\']+)["\']', command):
            q_clean = q.strip()
            if not (q_clean.startswith("http://") or q_clean.startswith("https://") or "meet.google.com" in q_clean or "teams.microsoft.com" in q_clean or "teams.live.com" in q_clean):
                if not re.match(r'^\d{1,2}(:\d{2})?\s*(am|pm)?$', q_clean, re.IGNORECASE):
                    return q_clean

        # 3. Check for unquoted 'named X' or 'called X' or 'titled X'
        unquoted_named = re.search(
            r'(?:named|called|titled)\s+([^,]+?)(?=\s+(?:with|link|at|in|for|daily|every|https?://)|$)',
            command, re.IGNORECASE,
        )
        if unquoted_named:
            cand = unquoted_named.group(1).strip('"\' ,')
            if cand:
                return cand

        # 4. Fallback stripping
        text = command
        # Remove URLs
        text = re.sub(r'https?://\S+', '', text)
        # Remove recurrence phrases
        text = re.sub(r'\b(daily|every\s+day|till\s+.*|until\s+.*)$', '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        # Remove time phrases
        text = re.sub(r'\b(at\s+\d.*|in\s+\d+\s*(?:minute|minutes|min|mins|hour|hours|hr|hrs)|for\s+\d+\s*(?:minute|minutes|min|mins|hour|hours|hr|hrs)).*$', '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        # Remove with link / link
        text = re.sub(r'\b(with\s+link|link|with)\b.*$', '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        # Remove prefixes
        text = re.sub(r'^(jarvis\s*,?\s*)?(schedule|add|create|set up|book)\s+(a\s+)?(meeting\s+)?(named\s+|called\s+|titled\s+)?', '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        text = text.strip('"\' ,;:-')
        return text if text else "Untitled Meeting"

    # ─── Shutdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def shutdown(self):
        """Gracefully stop the scheduler."""
        if self.scheduler:
            self.scheduler.shutdown(wait=False)
</file>

<file path="automation/schedule_interface.py">

"""
Schedule Interface Module
Local web dashboard (FastAPI + single-page HTML) at http://localhost:5050

Features:
  - Quick-Add Form: name, URL, start time, duration
  - Today's Timeline: pending/active/completed meetings with Cancel / Join Now buttons
  - Bulk Importer: paste JSON array or text timetable
  - Real-time API for the JARVIS voice layer to query/manage schedules
"""

import threading
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

try:

    FASTAPI_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    FASTAPI_AVAILABLE = False

  <script>
    // ── Clock ────────────────────────────────
    function updateClock(){
      const now=new Date();
      document.getElementById('clock').textContent=now.toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
    }
    setInterval(updateClock,1000);updateClock();

    // ── Set default start time to now + 5 min ─
    (function(){
      const d=new Date(Date.now()+5*60000);
      const pad=n=>(''+n).padStart(2,'0');
      document.getElementById('start').value=
        `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
    })();

    // ── Toast ────────────────────────────────
    function toast(msg,ms=3000){
      const el=document.getElementById('toast');
      el.textContent=msg;el.classList.add('show');
      setTimeout(()=>el.classList.remove('show'),ms);
    }

    // ── Add Meeting ──────────────────────────
    async function addMeeting(e){
      e.preventDefault();
      const f=new FormData(e.target);
      const start=f.get('start_time');
      const dur=parseInt(f.get('duration'))||60;
      const [datePart, timePart] = start.split('T');
      const [y, m, d] = datePart.split('-').map(Number);
      const [hr, mn] = timePart.split(':').map(Number);
      const endDt = new Date(y, m - 1, d, hr, mn + dur);
      const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
      const endTimeLocal = `${endDt.getFullYear()}-${pad(endDt.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(endDt.getDate())}T${pad(endDt.getHours())}:${pad(endDt.getMinutes())}:00`;
      const body={
        title:f.get('title'),
        meeting_url:f.get('meeting_url'),
        start_time:start+':00',
        end_time:endTimeLocal,
      };
      const res=await fetch('/api/meetings',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
      if(res.ok){toast('Meeting added!');e.target.reset();loadTimeline();}
      else{const d=await res.json();toast('Error: '+(d.detail||'unknown'));}
      return false;
    }

    // ── Load Timeline ────────────────────────
    async function loadTimeline(){
      const res=await fetch('/api/meetings/today');
      const data=await res.json();
      const el=document.getElementById('timeline');
      if(!data.length){el.innerHTML='<div class="timeline-empty">No meetings today. Add one above!</div>';return;}

      el.innerHTML=data.map(m=>{
        const t=new Date(m.start_time);
        const time=t.toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
        const badgeClass={'pending':'badge-pending','active':'badge-active','completed':'badge-completed','cancelled':'badge-cancelled'}[m.status]||'badge-pending';
        const platform=m.platform==='google_meet'?'Google Meet':m.platform==='teams'?'MS Teams':'Unknown';
        let actions='';
        if(m.status==='pending'){
          actions=`<button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="joinNow('${m.id}')">▶ Join Now</button>
                   <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="cancelMeeting('${m.id}')">✕ Cancel</button>`;
        }else if(m.status==='active'){
          actions=`<button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="leaveMeeting()">⏹ Leave</button>`;
        }
        return `<div class="meeting-row">
          <div class="meeting-time">${time}</div>
          <div class="meeting-info">
            <h4>${m.title} <span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${m.status}</span></h4>
            <div class="meta">${platform} · ${m.meeting_url.slice(0,50)}…</div>
          </div>
          <div class="meeting-actions">${actions}</div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('');
    }
    setInterval(loadTimeline,15000);loadTimeline();

    // ── Actions ──────────────────────────────
    async function cancelMeeting(id){
      await fetch(`/api/meetings/${id}`,{method:'DELETE'});
      toast('Meeting cancelled');loadTimeline();
    }
    async function joinNow(id){
      const res=await fetch(`/api/meetings/${id}/join`,{method:'POST'});
      if(res.ok) toast('Joining meeting...');
      else toast('Join failed');
      loadTimeline();
    }
    async function leaveMeeting(){
      await fetch('/api/meetings/leave',{method:'POST'});
      toast('Leaving meeting...');loadTimeline();
    }

    // ── Bulk Import ──────────────────────────
    async function bulkImport(){
      const raw=document.getElementById('bulk-input').value.trim();
      if(!raw){toast('Paste some JSON first');return;}
      try{
        const arr=JSON.parse(raw);
        const res=await fetch('/api/meetings/bulk',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(arr)});
        const d=await res.json();
        toast(`Imported ${d.count} meetings`);loadTimeline();
      }catch(e){toast('Invalid JSON: '+e.message);}
    }
  </script>

class ScheduleInterface:
    """
    Local web dashboard and REST API for managing meeting schedules.
    Runs in a daemon thread so it never blocks the JARVIS main loop.
    """

    def __init__(self, scheduler, meeting_bot=None):
        """
        Args:
            scheduler: MeetingScheduler instance
            meeting_bot: Optional MeetingBot instance (for Join Now / Leave)
        """
        self.scheduler = scheduler
        self.meeting_bot = meeting_bot
        self.app = None
        self._thread = None

        if FASTAPI_AVAILABLE:
            self._build_app()

    def _build_app(self):
        """Create the FastAPI application with all routes."""
        self.app = FastAPI(title="JARVIS Meeting Dashboard")

        @self.app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
        async def dashboard():
            return DASHBOARD_HTML

        @self.app.get("/api/meetings")
        async def list_meetings():
            return JSONResponse(self.scheduler.get_all())

        @self.app.get("/api/meetings/today")
        async def today_meetings():
            return JSONResponse(self.scheduler.get_today())

        @self.app.get("/api/meetings/pending")
        async def pending_meetings():
            return JSONResponse(self.scheduler.get_pending())

        @self.app.post("/api/meetings")
        async def add_meeting(request: Request):
            data = await request.json()
            try:
                start = datetime.fromisoformat(data["start_time"])
                end = datetime.fromisoformat(data["end_time"]) if data.get("end_time") else None
                if end and end <= start:
                    end = start + timedelta(hours=1)
                meeting = self.scheduler.add_meeting(
                    title=data.get("title", "Untitled Meeting"),
                    meeting_url=data["meeting_url"],
                    start_time=start,
                    end_time=end,
                    auto_leave=data.get("auto_leave", True),
                )
                return JSONResponse(meeting, status_code=201)
            except Exception as e:
                return JSONResponse({"detail": str(e)}, status_code=400)

        @self.app.delete("/api/meetings/{meeting_id}")
        async def cancel_meeting(meeting_id: str):
            ok = self.scheduler.cancel_meeting(meeting_id)
            if ok:
                return JSONResponse({"status": "cancelled"})
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "Meeting not found"}, status_code=404)

        @self.app.post("/api/meetings/{meeting_id}/join")
        async def join_now(meeting_id: str):
            meeting = self.scheduler.get_by_id(meeting_id)
            if not meeting:
                return JSONResponse({"detail": "Not found"}, status_code=404)
            if self.meeting_bot:
                self.scheduler.mark_active(meeting_id)
                end_time = datetime.fromisoformat(meeting["end_time"])
                threading.Thread(
                    target=self.meeting_bot.join_meeting,
                    args=(meeting["meeting_url"],),
                    kwargs={"end_time": end_time, "auto_leave": meeting.get("auto_leave", True)},
                    daemon=True,
                ).start()
                return JSONResponse({"status": "joining"})
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "Meeting bot not available"}, status_code=503)

        @self.app.post("/api/meetings/leave")
        async def leave_meeting():
            if self.meeting_bot:
                self.meeting_bot.leave_meeting()
                # Mark any active meetings as completed
                for m in self.scheduler.get_active():
                    self.scheduler.mark_completed(m["id"])
                return JSONResponse({"status": "left"})
            return JSONResponse({"detail": "No active meeting"}, status_code=404)

        @self.app.post("/api/meetings/bulk")
        async def bulk_import(request: Request):
            data = await request.json()
            count = self.scheduler.bulk_import(data)
            return JSONResponse({"count": count})

    def start(self):
        """Launch the dashboard server in a daemon thread."""
        if not FASTAPI_AVAILABLE or not self.app:
            print("[ScheduleInterface] FastAPI not available — dashboard disabled")
            return

        def _run():
            uvicorn.run(
                self.app,
                host=DASHBOARD_HOST,
                port=DASHBOARD_PORT,
                log_level="warning",
            )

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()
        print(f"[ScheduleInterface] Dashboard running at http://{DASHBOARD_HOST}:{DASHBOARD_PORT}")

    def stop(self):
        """Stop the server (best-effort; uvicorn in a thread is fire-and-forget)."""
        # Daemon thread will die when the main process exits
        pass
</file>

<file path="automation/server.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
FastAPI REST & WebSocket Server (:5050) for JARVIS 2.0
Serves the Holographic HUD React frontend and provides real-time bi-directional
telemetry and command streaming over WebSockets.
"""

import asyncio
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, HTMLResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from pydantic import BaseModel

# Internal JARVIS modules
from config.settings import (
    BASE_DIR,
    MEETINGS_FILE,
    ALARMS_FILE,
    SKILLS_FILE,
    MCP_SERVERS_FILE,
    UI_DIST_DIR,
    add_provider_key,
    get_active_key,
    get_masked_vault,
    get_vault,
    is_internet_connected,
    load_vault,
    remove_provider_key,
    save_vault,
)
from core.adapters.openai_compat import fetch_models
from core.engine import engine
from core.llm_router import router
from agent_os.code_executor import code_executor
from agent_os.os_actuator import os_actuator
from agent_os.safety_interceptor import safety_interceptor
from agent_os.state_machine import os_agent
from integrations.mcp_client import mcp_client
from integrations.skill_store import skill_store
from integrations.workspace_rag import workspace_rag
from automation.meeting_bot import MeetingBot

app = FastAPI(title="JARVIS 2.0 Holographic HUD Server", version="2.0.0")

# Enable CORS for local Vite dev and pywebview
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Active WebSocket Connections Pool
connected_clients: List[WebSocket] = []
clients_lock = threading.Lock()
main_event_loop = None


def broadcast_ws_sync(event_type: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Thread-safe synchronous helper to broadcast to WebSocket clients."""
    global main_event_loop
    message = json.dumps({"type": event_type, "payload": payload})
    with clients_lock:
        clients = list(connected_clients)

    if not clients or not main_event_loop or main_event_loop.is_closed():
        return

    for ws in clients:
        try:
            asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(ws.send_text(message), main_event_loop)
        except Exception:
            pass


# ── Wire System & Agent Listeners to WebSocket Broadcasts ────────────
router.register_telemetry_listener(lambda data: broadcast_ws_sync("TOKEN_TELEMETRY", data))
engine.register_chat_listener(lambda data: broadcast_ws_sync("CHAT_MESSAGE", data))
code_executor.register_step_listener(lambda data: broadcast_ws_sync("AGENT_STEP", data))
os_agent.register_step_listener(lambda data: broadcast_ws_sync("AGENT_STEP", data))
safety_interceptor.register_approval_listener(lambda data: broadcast_ws_sync("APPROVAL_REQUIRED", data))


# ── WebSocket Endpoint ────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.websocket("/ws")
async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
    global main_event_loop
    main_event_loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    await websocket.accept()

    with clients_lock:
        connected_clients.append(websocket)
    print(f"[WebSocket] HUD client connected (Total: {len(connected_clients)})")

    # Send initial states immediately
    try:
        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
            "type": "AUDIO_STATE",
            "payload": {
                "mic_active": engine.get_mic_active(),
                "tts_active": engine.get_tts_active(),
                "status": "idle",
                "meeting_auto_muted": getattr(engine.listener, "meeting_auto_muted", False),
            }
        }))
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            try:
                msg = json.loads(data)
                mtype = msg.get("type", "")
                mpayload = msg.get("payload", {})

                if mtype == "USER_COMMAND":
                    q = mpayload.get("query", "")
                    src = mpayload.get("source", "text")
                    threading.Thread(target=engine.process_command, args=(q, src), daemon=True).start()
            except Exception as parse_err:
                print(f"[WebSocket] Inbound parse error: {parse_err}")

    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        with clients_lock:
            if websocket in connected_clients:
                connected_clients.remove(websocket)
        print(f"[WebSocket] HUD client disconnected (Remaining: {len(connected_clients)})")


# ── Background Telemetry Broadcast Loop ───────────────────────────────
async def telemetry_broadcast_worker():
    """Periodically broadcasts SYSTEM_TELEMETRY and AUDIO_STATE every 2 seconds."""
    while True:
        try:
            import psutil
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=None)
            ram = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            batt = psutil.sensors_battery()
            battery_pct = int(batt.percent) if batt else 100
            charging = batt.power_plugged if batt else True
            online = is_internet_connected()

            broadcast_ws_sync("SYSTEM_TELEMETRY", {
                "cpu": cpu,
                "ram": ram,
                "battery": battery_pct,
                "charging": charging,
                "online": online,
            })

            broadcast_ws_sync("AUDIO_STATE", {
                "mic_active": engine.get_mic_active(),
                "tts_active": engine.get_tts_active(),
                "status": "thinking" if getattr(engine, "is_thinking", False) else ("speaking" if engine.tts.is_speaking() else ("listening" if engine.get_mic_active() else "idle")),
                "meeting_auto_muted": getattr(engine.listener, "meeting_auto_muted", False),
            })

            bot = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.bot
            broadcast_ws_sync("MEETING_STATE", {
                "current_meeting": bot.current_meeting if bot else None,
                "captions": bot.get_live_captions() if bot else [],
            })
        except Exception:
            pass

        await asyncio.sleep(2.0)


@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    global main_event_loop
    main_event_loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    asyncio.create_task(telemetry_broadcast_worker())


# ── Pydantic Request Models ───────────────────────────────────────────
class CommandRequest(BaseModel):
    query: str
    source: str = "text"

class AudioToggleRequest(BaseModel):
    target: str  # "mic" or "tts"
    enabled: bool

class KeyRequest(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    key: Optional[str] = ""
    key_index: Optional[int] = None
    action: str = "add"  # "add" or "remove"

class JoinMeetingRequest(BaseModel):
    url: str
    title: Optional[str] = "Online Meeting"

class TeachStartRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    trigger_phrases: List[str] = []

class TeachStopRequest(BaseModel):
    save: bool = True

class CustomEndpointRequest(BaseModel):
    action: str = "add"  # "add" or "delete"
    endpoint: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    endpoint_id: Optional[str] = None

class FetchModelsRequest(BaseModel):
    base_url: str
    api_key: Optional[str] = ""

class TestConnectionRequest(BaseModel):
    provider: str
    base_url: Optional[str] = None
    api_key: Optional[str] = None
    model: Optional[str] = None

class RolesRequest(BaseModel):
    roles: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    autonomous_trust_mode: Optional[bool] = None
    workspace_path: Optional[str] = None

class ApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    approved: bool

class SystemControlRequest(BaseModel):
    action: str  # "volume", "brightness", "app", "workspace_path"
    value: Any


# ── REST API Endpoints ────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/api/command")
def api_command(req: CommandRequest):
    res = engine.process_command(req.query, source=req.source)
    return res

@app.post("/api/audio/toggle")
def api_audio_toggle(req: AudioToggleRequest):
    if req.target == "mic":
        engine.set_mic_active(req.enabled)
        state = engine.get_mic_active()
    elif req.target == "tts":
        engine.set_tts_active(req.enabled)
        state = engine.get_tts_active()
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid audio target")
    return {"target": req.target, "enabled": state}

@app.get("/api/vault")
def api_get_vault(reveal: bool = False):
    if reveal:
        return load_vault()
    return get_masked_vault()

@app.post("/api/vault/key")
def api_vault_key(req: KeyRequest):
    if req.action == "add":
        success = add_provider_key(req.provider, req.key or "")
    elif req.action == "remove":
        success = remove_provider_key(req.provider, key_or_index=req.key, key_index=req.key_index)
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Action must be 'add' or 'remove'")
    return {"ok": success, "vault": get_masked_vault()}

@app.post("/api/vault/custom-endpoint")
def api_vault_custom_endpoint(req: CustomEndpointRequest):
    vault = load_vault()
    eps = vault.setdefault("providers", {}).setdefault("custom_endpoints", [])
    if req.action == "add" and req.endpoint:
        existing = next((i for i, ep in enumerate(eps) if ep.get("id") == req.endpoint.get("id")), None)
        if existing is not None:
            eps[existing] = req.endpoint
        else:
            eps.append(req.endpoint)
        save_vault(vault)
        return {"ok": True, "vault": get_masked_vault()}
    elif req.action == "delete" and req.endpoint_id:
        vault["providers"]["custom_endpoints"] = [ep for ep in eps if ep.get("id") != req.endpoint_id]
        save_vault(vault)
        return {"ok": True, "vault": get_masked_vault()}
    raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid custom-endpoint request")

@app.post("/api/vault/fetch-models")
def api_fetch_models(req: FetchModelsRequest):
    models = fetch_models(req.base_url, req.api_key or "")
    return {"ok": True, "models": models}

@app.post("/api/vault/test")
def api_test_connection(req: TestConnectionRequest):
    if req.provider == "openweathermap":
        import time
        t0 = time.time()
        old_key = getattr(engine.weather_handler, "_custom_api_key", None)
        if req.api_key:
            engine.weather_handler._custom_api_key = req.api_key
        try:
            ok = engine.weather_handler.test_api_key()
            lat = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
            return {"ok": bool(ok), "latency_ms": lat, "error": None if ok else "OpenWeatherMap API key invalid or unreachable"}
        finally:
            engine.weather_handler._custom_api_key = old_key

    adapter = router.get_adapter_for_provider(
        provider=req.provider,
        model=req.model or "",
        api_key=req.api_key,
    )
    if req.base_url and hasattr(adapter, "base_url"):
        adapter.base_url = req.base_url.rstrip("/")
    res = adapter.test_connection()
    return res

@app.post("/api/vault/roles")
def api_update_roles(req: RolesRequest):
    vault = load_vault()
    if req.roles is not None:
        vault["roles"] = req.roles
    if req.autonomous_trust_mode is not None:
        vault["autonomous_trust_mode"] = req.autonomous_trust_mode
    if req.workspace_path is not None:
        vault["workspace_path"] = req.workspace_path
    save_vault(vault)
    return {"ok": True, "vault": get_masked_vault()}

@app.get("/api/meetings")
def api_get_meetings():
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    bot = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.bot
    meetings_data = scheduler.get_all() if scheduler else []
    summaries = bot.get_summaries() if bot else []
    live_caps = bot.get_live_captions() if bot else []
    curr_mtg = bot.current_meeting if bot else None
    return {
        "meetings": meetings_data,
        "summaries": summaries,
        "live_captions": live_caps,
        "current_meeting": curr_mtg,
    }

@app.post("/api/meetings")
def api_add_meeting(meeting: Dict[str, Any]):
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    if not scheduler:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Meeting scheduler not available")
    st = meeting.get("start_time")
    if isinstance(st, str):
        try:
            st_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(st.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        except Exception:
            st_dt = datetime.now()
    elif isinstance(st, datetime):
        st_dt = st
    else:
        st_dt = datetime.now()

    # FIX 2: Convert timezone-aware datetimes to local naive time
    if st_dt.tzinfo is not None:
        st_dt = st_dt.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)

    et = meeting.get("end_time")
    et_dt = None
    if isinstance(et, str):
        try:
            et_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(et.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        except Exception:
            et_dt = None

    if et_dt is not None and et_dt.tzinfo is not None:
        et_dt = et_dt.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)

    new_m = scheduler.add_meeting(
        title=meeting.get("title", "Meeting"),
        meeting_url=meeting.get("meeting_url", ""),
        start_time=st_dt,
        end_time=et_dt,
        auto_leave=meeting.get("auto_leave", True),
    )
    return {"ok": True, "meeting": new_m}

@app.delete("/api/meetings/{meeting_id}")
def api_delete_meeting(meeting_id: str):
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    if scheduler:
        scheduler.cancel_meeting(meeting_id)
        with scheduler._lock:
            scheduler._meetings = [m for m in scheduler._meetings if m.get("id") != meeting_id]
            scheduler._save()
        return {"ok": True}
    return {"ok": False}

@app.post("/api/meetings/{meeting_id}/join")
def api_join_meeting_by_id(meeting_id: str):
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    bot = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.bot
    if not scheduler:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Meeting scheduler not available")
    meeting = scheduler.get_by_id(meeting_id)
    if not meeting:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Meeting not found")

    # Mark active immediately to avoid race with poller
    scheduler.mark_active(meeting_id)
    et = meeting.get("end_time")
    end_dt = None
    if isinstance(et, str):
        try:
            end_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(et.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            if end_dt.tzinfo is not None:
                end_dt = end_dt.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None)
        except Exception:
            end_dt = None
    elif isinstance(et, datetime):
        end_dt = et

    def _join_worker():
        try:
            if bot:
                bot.join_meeting(
                    url=meeting["meeting_url"],
                    end_time=end_dt,
                    auto_leave=meeting.get("auto_leave", True),
                    title=meeting.get("title", "Online Meeting"),
                )
        except Exception as err:
            print(f"[Server] Join error: {err}")
        finally:
            scheduler.mark_completed(meeting_id)

    if bot:
        threading.Thread(target=_join_worker, daemon=True).start()
    else:
        import webbrowser
        webbrowser.open(meeting["meeting_url"])
    return {"ok": True, "status": "joining"}

@app.post("/api/meetings/{meeting_id}/cancel")
def api_cancel_meeting_by_id(meeting_id: str):
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    if not scheduler:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Meeting scheduler not available")
    ok = scheduler.cancel_meeting(meeting_id)
    return {"ok": ok, "status": "cancelled" if ok else "not_found"}

@app.post("/api/meetings/bulk")
async def api_bulk_import_meetings(request: Request):
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    if not scheduler:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Meeting scheduler not available")
    try:
        payload = await request.json()
    except Exception:
        payload = []

    if isinstance(payload, dict) and "meetings" in payload:
        entries = payload["meetings"]
    elif isinstance(payload, list):
        entries = payload
    else:
        entries = []
    count = scheduler.bulk_import(entries)
    return {"ok": True, "count": count}

@app.post("/api/meetings/setup")
def api_meeting_setup():
    try:
        proc = MeetingBot.run_account_setup_async()
        return {"ok": True, "pid": proc.pid, "message": "Browser account setup launched"}
    except Exception as e:
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "automation.meeting_bot", "--setup"]
        p = subprocess.Popen(cmd)
        return {"ok": True, "pid": p.pid, "message": f"Browser account setup launched: {e}"}

@app.post("/api/meetings/join-url")
def api_join_meeting_url(req: JoinMeetingRequest):
    bot = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.bot
    if bot:
        threading.Thread(
            target=bot.join_meeting,
            kwargs={
                "url": req.url,
                "title": req.title or "Online Meeting",
            },
            daemon=True,
        ).start()
    else:
        import webbrowser
        webbrowser.open(req.url)
    return {"ok": True}

@app.post("/api/meetings/leave")
def api_leave_meeting():
    bot = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.bot
    scheduler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None) and engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
    if bot:
        bot.leave_meeting()
    if scheduler:
        for m in scheduler.get_active():
            scheduler.mark_completed(m["id"])
    return {"ok": True}

@app.get("/api/alarms")
def api_get_alarms():
    if ALARMS_FILE.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(ALARMS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {"alarms": [], "timers": []}

@app.post("/api/alarms")
def api_add_alarm(payload: Dict[str, Any]):
    data = api_get_alarms()
    target_list = data.setdefault("alarms" if payload.get("type") != "timer" else "timers", [])
    payload["id"] = f"alr-{int(time.time())}"
    target_list.append(payload)
    ALARMS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return {"ok": True, "data": data}

@app.delete("/api/alarms/{alarm_id}")
def api_delete_alarm(alarm_id: str):
    data = api_get_alarms()
    data["alarms"] = [a for a in data.get("alarms", []) if a.get("id") != alarm_id]
    data["timers"] = [t for t in data.get("timers", []) if t.get("id") != alarm_id]
    ALARMS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return {"ok": True}

    return {"ok": success, "request_id": request_id, "approved": req.approved}

@app.post("/api/system/control")
def api_system_control(req: SystemControlRequest):
    if req.action == "volume":
        engine.system_controls.set_volume(str(req.value))
    elif req.action == "brightness":
        engine.system_controls.set_brightness(str(req.value))
    elif req.action == "app":
        engine.app_handler.open_application(f"open {req.value}")
    elif req.action == "workspace_path":
        workspace_rag.set_workspace_path(str(req.value))
    return {"ok": True, "action": req.action, "value": req.value}

<file path="automation/web_agent.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Autonomous Web Researcher for JARVIS 2.0
Uses Playwright DOM Distiller and requests/BeautifulSoup fallback to autonomously navigate
websites, extract clean readable text/links, and synthesize multi-page research briefs.
"""

import json
import re
import urllib.parse
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from core.llm_router import router

try:
    from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright
    PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE = False


class WebResearchAgent:
    """
    Autonomous Web Research Agent with DOM Distillation and multi-source synthesis.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.headers = {
            "User-Agent": (
                "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) "
                "AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
            )
        }

    def search_duckduckgo(self, query: str, max_results: int = 4) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
        """Performs search and extracts URLs and titles."""
        clean_q = urllib.parse.quote_plus(query)
        url = f"https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q={clean_q}"
        results = []

        try:
            resp = requests.get(url, headers=self.headers, timeout=10)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                soup = BeautifulSoup(resp.text, "html.parser")
                links = soup.find_all("a", class_="result__url")
                titles = soup.find_all("a", class_="result__snippet")

                for i in range(min(max_results, len(links))):
                    href = links[i].get("href", "")
                    # Extract actual target url from DDG redirect wrapper
                    if "uddg=" in href:
                        href = urllib.parse.unquote(href.split("uddg=")[1].split("&")[0])
                    snippet = titles[i].get_text(strip=True) if i < len(titles) else ""
                    if href.startswith("http"):
                        results.append({"url": href, "snippet": snippet})
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WebAgent] DDG search notice: {e}")

        # Fallback if no DDG results
        if not results:
            results.append({
                "url": f"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/{clean_q}",
                "snippet": f"Encyclopedia reference for {query}",
            })

        return results[:max_results]

    def distill_page(self, url: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Loads URL and extracts clean readable markdown text, headings, and links,
        stripping ads, scripts, navbars, and cookie banners.
        """
        # Try requests + BeautifulSoup for fast extraction
        try:
            resp = requests.get(url, headers=self.headers, timeout=12)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                soup = BeautifulSoup(resp.text, "html.parser")

                # Remove non-content tags
                for tag in soup(["script", "style", "nav", "footer", "aside", "header", "noscript", "svg"]):
                    tag.decompose()

                title = soup.title.get_text(strip=True) if soup.title else url

                # Extract main content or body text
                main_tag = soup.find("main") or soup.find("article") or soup.body
                raw_text = main_tag.get_text(separator="\n", strip=True) if main_tag else soup.get_text()

                # Clean whitespace
                lines = [l.strip() for l in raw_text.split("\n") if len(l.strip()) > 30]
                cleaned_text = "\n".join(lines[:80])  # Cap at reasonable length

                return {
                    "ok": True,
                    "url": url,
                    "title": title,
                    "content": cleaned_text,
                }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[WebAgent] Error distilling {url}: {e}")

        return {"ok": False, "url": url, "title": "", "content": ""}

    def research(self, topic: str, max_pages: int = 3) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Full autonomous research flow:
        1. Find sources for topic.
        2. Distill page content.
        3. Synthesize comprehensive multi-source briefing with LLM.
        """
        print(f"[WebAgent] Researching topic: '{topic}'")
        sources = self.search_duckduckgo(topic, max_results=max_pages)

        distilled_pages = []
        for src in sources:
            page_data = self.distill_page(src["url"])
            if page_data["ok"] and len(page_data["content"]) > 100:
                distilled_pages.append(page_data)

        if not distilled_pages:
            # Direct LLM synthesis if pages blocked
            summary = router.query_text(
                "qa_chat",
                f"Please provide a comprehensive, well-researched briefing on: {topic}",
                system_prompt="You are JARVIS. Synthesize a structured, executive research summary with key findings.",
            )
            return {
                "ok": True,
                "topic": topic,
                "summary": summary,
                "sources": [s["url"] for s in sources],
            }

        # Build research context
        context_parts = []
        for i, p in enumerate(distilled_pages, start=1):
            context_parts.append(f"--- Source {i} ({p['title']} - {p['url']}) ---\n{p['content']}")

        full_context = "\n\n".join(context_parts)
        system_prompt = (
            "You are JARVIS Autonomous Web Research Agent. "
            "Synthesize the provided multi-page web findings into an executive intelligence report.\n"
            "Format with:\n"
            "1. Executive Overview\n"
            "2. Detailed Findings & Key Data Points\n"
            "3. Source References\n"
        )
        prompt = f"Topic: {topic}\n\nWeb Extracts:\n{full_context}"

        summary = router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)

        return {
            "ok": True,
            "topic": topic,
            "summary": summary,
            "sources": [p["url"] for p in distilled_pages],
            "pages_analyzed": len(distilled_pages),
        }


# Global web agent singleton
web_agent = WebResearchAgent()

<file path="core/adapters/anthropic_adapter.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Anthropic Claude Adapter
Supports Anthropic Claude 3.5 Sonnet and other models via SDK or REST fallback.
"""

import json
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
import requests

from .base import BaseLLMAdapter, ChatMessage, LLMResponse

try:
    import anthropic
    ANTHROPIC_SDK_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    ANTHROPIC_SDK_AVAILABLE = False


class AnthropicAdapter(BaseLLMAdapter):
    """Adapter for Anthropic Claude models."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: str = "", model: str = "claude-3-5-sonnet-latest"):
        super().__init__(api_key=api_key, model=model)
        self.provider = "anthropic"
        self._client = None
        if ANTHROPIC_SDK_AVAILABLE and self.api_key:
            try:
                self._client = anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=self.api_key)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[AnthropicAdapter] SDK init warning: {e}")

    def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not self.api_key:
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                latency_ms=0,
                error="No API key configured for Anthropic",
            )

        # Extract system prompt if present
        system_prompt = ""
        user_assistant_messages = []
        for msg in messages:
            if msg.role == "system":
                system_prompt = msg.content
            else:
                user_assistant_messages.append({"role": msg.role, "content": msg.content})

        if not user_assistant_messages:
            user_assistant_messages = [{"role": "user", "content": "Hello"}]

        # Try Anthropic SDK first if available
        if ANTHROPIC_SDK_AVAILABLE and self._client:
            try:
                kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
                    "model": self.model,
                    "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                    "temperature": temperature,
                    "messages": user_assistant_messages,
                }
                if system_prompt:
                    kwargs["system"] = system_prompt

                resp = self._client.messages.create(**kwargs)
                latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

                text_out = ""
                for block in resp.content:
                    if getattr(block, "type", "") == "text":
                        text_out += getattr(block, "text", "")

                prompt_tokens = getattr(resp.usage, "input_tokens", 0) if resp.usage else 0
                completion_tokens = getattr(resp.usage, "output_tokens", 0) if resp.usage else 0

                raw_json = None
                if json_mode and text_out:
                    try:
                        raw_json = json.loads(text_out)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                return LLMResponse(
                    text=text_out,
                    prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
                    completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
                    total_tokens=prompt_tokens + completion_tokens,
                    latency_ms=latency_ms,
                    provider=self.provider,
                    model=self.model,
                    raw_json=raw_json,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                # Fall back to REST if SDK fails or return error
                pass

        # Direct REST API fallback
        try:
            headers = {
                "x-api-key": self.api_key,
                "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
                "content-type": "application/json",
            }
            body: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "model": self.model,
                "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                "temperature": temperature,
                "messages": user_assistant_messages,
            }
            if system_prompt:
                body["system"] = system_prompt

            resp = requests.post(
                "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
                headers=headers,
                json=body,
                timeout=30,
            )
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

            if resp.status_code != 200:
                return LLMResponse(
                    text="",
                    latency_ms=latency_ms,
                    provider=self.provider,
                    model=self.model,
                    error=f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}",
                )

            data = resp.json()
            text_out = ""
            for block in data.get("content", []):
                if block.get("type") == "text":
                    text_out += block.get("text", "")

            usage = data.get("usage", {})
            prompt_tokens = usage.get("input_tokens", 0)
            completion_tokens = usage.get("output_tokens", 0)

            raw_json = None
            if json_mode and text_out:
                try:
                    raw_json = json.loads(text_out)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            return LLMResponse(
                text=text_out,
                prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
                total_tokens=prompt_tokens + completion_tokens,
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                raw_json=raw_json,
            )

        except Exception as e:
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                error=str(e),
            )

    def test_connection(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not self.api_key:
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": 0, "error": "API key is missing"}

        try:
            headers = {
                "x-api-key": self.api_key,
                "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
                "content-type": "application/json",
            }
            body = {
                "model": self.model,
                "max_tokens": 5,
                "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "ping"}],
            }
            resp = requests.post(
                "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
                headers=headers,
                json=body,
                timeout=10,
            )
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                return {"ok": True, "latency_ms": latency, "error": None}
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"}
        except Exception as e:
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": str(e)}

<file path="core/adapters/base.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Base Adapter Interface & Standardized LLM Response
Provides common data structures and abstract class for all LLM providers.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import json
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


def extract_json_from_text(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    r"""
    Robust JSON extractor:
    1. Strips ```json and ``` fences.
    2. Tries json.loads(cleaned).
    3. If that fails, uses regex re.search(r"\{.*\}", cleaned, re.DOTALL) to extract the first JSON object and parse it.
    """
    if not text:
        return None
    cleaned = text.strip()
    # 1. Strips ```json and ``` fences
    if "```" in cleaned:
        cleaned = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?\s*", "", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE)
        cleaned = re.sub(r"\s*```$", "", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE).strip()

    # 2. Tries json.loads(cleaned)
    try:
        data = json.loads(cleaned)
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            return data
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 3. Regex search for first JSON object
    match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", cleaned, re.DOTALL)
    if match:
        try:
            data = json.loads(match.group(0))
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                return data
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None


@dataclass
class ChatMessage:
    role: str  # "system", "user", "assistant"
    content: str
    image_b64: Optional[str] = None  # Base64 encoded PNG/JPEG for multimodal vision


@dataclass
class LLMResponse:
    text: str
    prompt_tokens: int = 0
    completion_tokens: int = 0
    total_tokens: int = 0
    latency_ms: int = 0
    provider: str = ""
    model: str = ""
    raw_json: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    error: Optional[str] = None


class BaseLLMAdapter(ABC):
    """Abstract interface for all LLM backend adapters."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: str = "", model: str = ""):
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.model = model

    @abstractmethod
    def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Generate response from the LLM."""
        pass

    @abstractmethod
    def test_connection(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Send a lightweight ping to verify credentials and connectivity.
        Returns: {"ok": bool, "latency_ms": int, "error": str | None}
        """
        pass
</file>

<file path="core/adapters/gemini_adapter.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Google Gemini Adapter using google-genai SDK
Supports text, multimodal vision, structured JSON responses, and connection testing.
"""

import base64
import json
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

try:
    from google import genai
    from google.genai import types
    GEMINI_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    GEMINI_AVAILABLE = False

from .base import BaseLLMAdapter, ChatMessage, LLMResponse


class GeminiAdapter(BaseLLMAdapter):
    """Adapter for Google Gemini models via google-genai SDK."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: str = "", model: str = "gemini-2.5-flash"):
        super().__init__(api_key=api_key, model=model)
        self.provider = "gemini"
        self._client = None
        if GEMINI_AVAILABLE and self.api_key:
            try:
                self._client = genai.Client(api_key=self.api_key)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[GeminiAdapter] Client init warning: {e}")

    def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not GEMINI_AVAILABLE:
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                latency_ms=0,
                error="google-genai library is not installed",
            )

        if not self.api_key:
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                latency_ms=0,
                error="No API key configured for Gemini",
            )

        try:
            client = self._client or genai.Client(api_key=self.api_key)

            # Separate system prompt and build contents
            system_instruction = None
            contents: List[Any] = []

            for msg in messages:
                if msg.role == "system":
                    system_instruction = msg.content
                elif msg.role in ("user", "assistant"):
                    parts: List[Any] = []
                    if msg.image_b64:
                        try:
                            img_data = base64.b64decode(msg.image_b64)
                            parts.append(types.Part.from_bytes(data=img_data, mime_type="image/png"))
                        except Exception as img_err:
                            print(f"[GeminiAdapter] Image decode error: {img_err}")
                    if msg.content:
                        parts.append(msg.content)
                    if parts:
                        contents.extend(parts)

            config_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "temperature": temperature,
                "max_output_tokens": max_tokens,
            }
            if system_instruction:
                config_kwargs["system_instruction"] = system_instruction
            if json_mode:
                config_kwargs["response_mime_type"] = "application/json"

            config = types.GenerateContentConfig(**config_kwargs)

            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=self.model,
                contents=contents,
                config=config,
            )

            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            text_out = response.text or ""

            prompt_tokens = 0
            completion_tokens = 0
            if hasattr(response, "usage_metadata") and response.usage_metadata:
                prompt_tokens = getattr(response.usage_metadata, "prompt_token_count", 0) or 0
                completion_tokens = getattr(response.usage_metadata, "candidates_token_count", 0) or 0

            raw_json = None
            if json_mode and text_out:
                try:
                    raw_json = json.loads(text_out)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            return LLMResponse(
                text=text_out,
                prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
                total_tokens=prompt_tokens + completion_tokens,
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                raw_json=raw_json,
            )

        except Exception as e:
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            err_msg = str(e)
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                error=err_msg,
            )

    def test_connection(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not GEMINI_AVAILABLE:
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": 0, "error": "google-genai package not installed"}
        if not self.api_key:
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": 0, "error": "API key is missing"}

        try:
            client = self._client or genai.Client(api_key=self.api_key)
            resp = client.models.generate_content(
                model=self.model,
                contents="ping",
                config=types.GenerateContentConfig(max_output_tokens=5),
            )
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            if resp.text:
                return {"ok": True, "latency_ms": latency, "error": None}
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": "Empty response received"}
        except Exception as e:
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": str(e)}

<file path="core/adapters/groq_adapter.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Groq Adapter using official groq SDK
High-speed inference adapter for Llama 3 models.
"""

import json
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

try:
    from groq import Groq
    GROQ_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    GROQ_AVAILABLE = False

from .base import BaseLLMAdapter, ChatMessage, LLMResponse


class GroqAdapter(BaseLLMAdapter):
    """Adapter for Groq cloud API."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: str = "", model: str = "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"):
        super().__init__(api_key=api_key, model=model)
        self.provider = "groq"
        self._client = None
        if GROQ_AVAILABLE and self.api_key:
            try:
                self._client = Groq(api_key=self.api_key)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[GroqAdapter] Client init warning: {e}")

    def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not GROQ_AVAILABLE:
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                latency_ms=0,
                error="groq package is not installed",
            )

        if not self.api_key:
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                latency_ms=0,
                error="No API key configured for Groq",
            )

        try:
            client = self._client or Groq(api_key=self.api_key)

            formatted_messages = []
            for msg in messages:
                formatted_messages.append({"role": msg.role, "content": msg.content})

            kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "model": self.model,
                "messages": formatted_messages,
                "temperature": temperature,
                "max_tokens": max_tokens,
            }
            if json_mode:
                kwargs["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

            completion = client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

            choice = completion.choices[0]
            text_out = choice.message.content or ""

            prompt_tokens = 0
            completion_tokens = 0
            if hasattr(completion, "usage") and completion.usage:
                prompt_tokens = getattr(completion.usage, "prompt_tokens", 0) or 0
                completion_tokens = getattr(completion.usage, "completion_tokens", 0) or 0

            raw_json = None
            if json_mode and text_out:
                try:
                    raw_json = json.loads(text_out)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            return LLMResponse(
                text=text_out,
                prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
                total_tokens=prompt_tokens + completion_tokens,
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                raw_json=raw_json,
            )

        except Exception as e:
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                error=str(e),
            )

    def test_connection(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start_time = time.time()
        if not GROQ_AVAILABLE:
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": 0, "error": "groq package not installed"}
        if not self.api_key:
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": 0, "error": "API key is missing"}

        try:
            client = self._client or Groq(api_key=self.api_key)
            completion = client.chat.completions.create(
                model=self.model,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "ping"}],
                max_tokens=5,
            )
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            if completion.choices and completion.choices[0].message.content:
                return {"ok": True, "latency_ms": latency, "error": None}
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": "Empty response received"}
        except Exception as e:
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": str(e)}
