<file path="core/adapters/openai_compat.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Universal OpenAI-Compatible Adapter
Connects to OpenRouter, Ollama, LM Studio, vLLM, DeepSeek, or any custom /v1 endpoint.
Includes dynamic model fetching via GET /v1/models and fast connection verification.
"""

import json
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
import requests

from .base import BaseLLMAdapter, ChatMessage, LLMResponse, extract_json_from_text


def fetch_models(base_url: str, api_key: str = "") -> List[str]:
    """
    Queries GET {base_url}/models and returns a list of discovered model ID strings.
    Handles trailing slashes, auth headers, and common OpenAI/Ollama model list structures.
    """
    clean_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
    if not clean_url.endswith("/models"):
        clean_url = f"{clean_url}/models"

    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
    if api_key and api_key.strip():
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key.strip()}"

    try:
        resp = requests.get(clean_url, headers=headers, timeout=8)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            data = resp.json()
            models_list: List[str] = []
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                # Standard OpenAI format: {"data": [{"id": "..."}, ...]}
                if "data" in data and isinstance(data["data"], list):
                    for item in data["data"]:
                        if isinstance(item, dict) and "id" in item:
                            models_list.append(item["id"])
                        elif isinstance(item, str):
                            models_list.append(item)
                # Ollama format: {"models": [{"name": "..."}, ...]}
                elif "models" in data and isinstance(data["models"], list):
                    for item in data["models"]:
                        if isinstance(item, dict) and "name" in item:
                            models_list.append(item["name"])
                        elif isinstance(item, str):
                            models_list.append(item)
            elif isinstance(data, list):
                for item in data:
                    if isinstance(item, dict) and "id" in item:
                        models_list.append(item["id"])
                    elif isinstance(item, str):
                        models_list.append(item)
            return sorted(list(set(models_list))) if models_list else []
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[OpenAICompat] fetch_models error: {e}")

    return []


class OpenAICompatAdapter(BaseLLMAdapter):
    """Universal adapter for any OpenAI-compatible API endpoint."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1",
        api_key: str = "",
        model: str = "gpt-4o-mini",
        provider_name: str = "openai_compat",
    ):
        super().__init__(api_key=api_key, model=model)
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.provider = provider_name

    def generate(
        self,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        start_time = time.time()

        formatted_messages = []
        for msg in messages:
            formatted_messages.append({"role": msg.role, "content": msg.content})

        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        }
        if self.api_key and self.api_key.strip():
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key.strip()}"

        body: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": formatted_messages,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
        }
        if json_mode:
            body["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        endpoint_url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"

        try:
            resp = requests.post(endpoint_url, headers=headers, json=body, timeout=45)

            # If json_mode=True and request returns HTTP != 200 or empty choices,
            # immediately retry the request without response_format!
            if json_mode:
                need_retry = False
                if resp.status_code != 200:
                    need_retry = True
                else:
                    try:
                        d = resp.json()
                        c = d.get("choices", [])
                        if not c or not c[0].get("message", {}).get("content"):
                            need_retry = True
                    except Exception:
                        need_retry = True

                if need_retry:
                    retry_body = {
                        "model": self.model,
                        "messages": formatted_messages,
                        "temperature": temperature,
                        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                    }
                    try:
                        retry_resp = requests.post(endpoint_url, headers=headers, json=retry_body, timeout=45)
                        if retry_resp.status_code == 200:
                            resp = retry_resp
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

            if resp.status_code != 200:
                return LLMResponse(
                    text="",
                    latency_ms=latency_ms,
                    provider=self.provider,
                    model=self.model,
                    error=f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}",
                )

            data = resp.json()
            choices = data.get("choices", [])
            text_out = ""
            if choices and isinstance(choices, list):
                msg_obj = choices[0].get("message", {})
                text_out = msg_obj.get("content", "") or ""

            usage = data.get("usage", {})
            prompt_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0) if usage else 0
            completion_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0) if usage else 0

            # Always use extract_json_from_text(text_out) to populate raw_json
            raw_json = extract_json_from_text(text_out) if text_out else None

            return LLMResponse(
                text=text_out,
                prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
                total_tokens=prompt_tokens + completion_tokens,
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                raw_json=raw_json,
            )

        except Exception as e:
            latency_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return LLMResponse(
                text="",
                latency_ms=latency_ms,
                provider=self.provider,
                model=self.model,
                error=str(e),
            )

    def test_connection(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start_time = time.time()
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        if self.api_key and self.api_key.strip():
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key.strip()}"

        endpoint_url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        body = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "ping"}],
            "max_tokens": 5,
        }

        try:
            resp = requests.post(endpoint_url, headers=headers, json=body, timeout=3)
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            if resp.status_code == 200:
                data = resp.json()
                choices = data.get("choices", [])
                if choices:
                    return {"ok": True, "latency_ms": latency, "error": None}
                return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": "No completion choices returned"}
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": f"HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}"}
        except Exception as e:
            latency = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)
            return {"ok": False, "latency_ms": latency, "error": str(e)}
</file>

<file path="core/context_manager.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Conversation Memory & Context Window Manager for JARVIS 2.0
Maintains conversation history, trims context windows, and formats messages for adapters.
"""

from datetime import datetime
import threading
from typing import List, Optional
from core.adapters.base import ChatMessage


class ContextManager:
    """Manages short-term conversation context for multi-turn dialogues."""

    def __init__(self, max_turns: int = 20):
        self.max_turns = max_turns
        self._history: List[ChatMessage] = []
        self._system_prompt: Optional[str] = (
            "You are J.A.R.V.I.S., an advanced AI assistant and operating system companion. "
            "Respond concisely, politely, and precisely. Always prioritize actionable solutions."
        )
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def set_system_prompt(self, prompt: str) -> None:
        """Update global system instructions."""
        with self._lock:
            self._system_prompt = prompt

    def add_user_message(self, text: str, image_b64: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Append user query to history."""
        with self._lock:
            self._history.append(ChatMessage(role="user", content=text, image_b64=image_b64))
            self._trim()

    def add_assistant_message(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Append assistant response to history."""
        with self._lock:
            self._history.append(ChatMessage(role="assistant", content=text))
            self._trim()

    def get_messages(self) -> List[ChatMessage]:
        """Return full message list including system instruction."""
        with self._lock:
            messages = []
            if self._system_prompt:
                now_dt = datetime.now()
                dynamic_sys = (
                    f"{self._system_prompt}\n"
                    f"Current System Date & Time: {now_dt.strftime('%A, %B %d, %Y at %I:%M %p')} (Current Year: {now_dt.year}).\n"
                    "CRITICAL RULE: Never say 'Let me check that for you' or 'I can help with that' without actually providing the full answer in the same message."
                )
                messages.append(ChatMessage(role="system", content=dynamic_sys))
            messages.extend(list(self._history))
            return messages

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Reset conversation memory."""
        with self._lock:
            self._history.clear()

    def _trim(self) -> None:
        """Trim messages exceeding max_turns while preserving pairs."""
        if len(self._history) > self.max_turns * 2:
            self._history = self._history[-(self.max_turns * 2):]


# Singleton instance
context_mgr = ContextManager()
</file>

<file path="core/llm_router.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Multi-Key LLM Router, Auto-Failover, Role Dispatcher & Token Telemetry
Coordinates model calls across Gemini, Groq, Anthropic, OpenRouter, Ollama, and Custom endpoints.
Automatically rotates keys upon 429 rate limit or authentication errors.
"""

import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from config.settings import (
    get_active_key,
    get_role_target,
    get_vault,
    is_internet_connected,
    rotate_provider_key,
)
from .adapters.base import BaseLLMAdapter, ChatMessage, LLMResponse
from .adapters.gemini_adapter import GeminiAdapter
from .adapters.groq_adapter import GroqAdapter
from .adapters.anthropic_adapter import AnthropicAdapter
from .adapters.openai_compat import OpenAICompatAdapter


class LLMRouter:
    """
    Central LLM router with multi-key rotation, role-based model dispatching,
    offline local fallback, and real-time token/latency telemetry tracking.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self.total_session_tokens: int = 0
        self._telemetry_listeners: List[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]] = []

    def register_telemetry_listener(self, callback: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]) -> None:
        """Register a callback for TOKEN_TELEMETRY events."""
        with self._lock:
            if callback not in self._telemetry_listeners:
                self._telemetry_listeners.append(callback)

    def _emit_telemetry(self, provider: str, model: str, latency_ms: int, prompt_tokens: int, completion_tokens: int):
        """Broadcast live token usage and latency metrics."""
        with self._lock:
            self.total_session_tokens += (prompt_tokens + completion_tokens)
            total = self.total_session_tokens
            listeners = list(self._telemetry_listeners)

        payload = {
            "provider": provider,
            "model": model,
            "latency_ms": latency_ms,
            "prompt_tokens": prompt_tokens,
            "completion_tokens": completion_tokens,
            "total_session_tokens": total,
        }

        for cb in listeners:
            try:
                cb(payload)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[LLMRouter] Telemetry listener error: {e}")

    # Default model map for each known provider
    DEFAULT_MODELS: Dict[str, str] = {
        "gemini": "gemini-2.5-flash",
        "groq": "llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
        "anthropic": "claude-3-5-sonnet-latest",
        "openrouter": "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
        "ollama": "qwen2.5:7b",
    }

    def _resolve_model(self, provider: str, model: str) -> str:
        """Resolve empty or 'default' model to a concrete model name."""
        if model and model != "default":
            return model
        vault = get_vault()
        prov_models = vault.get("providers", {}).get(provider, {})
        if isinstance(prov_models, dict):
            models_list = prov_models.get("models", [])
            if models_list and isinstance(models_list, list) and len(models_list) > 0:
                return models_list[0]
        return self.DEFAULT_MODELS.get(provider, "gpt-4o-mini")

    def get_adapter_for_provider(
        self,
        provider: str,
        model: str,
        api_key: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> BaseLLMAdapter:
        """Instantiate the appropriate adapter for a given provider and model."""
        vault = get_vault()
        prov_key = api_key if api_key is not None else get_active_key(provider)
        resolved_model = self._resolve_model(provider, model)

        if provider == "gemini":
            return GeminiAdapter(api_key=prov_key or "", model=resolved_model)
        elif provider == "groq":
            return GroqAdapter(api_key=prov_key or "", model=resolved_model)
        elif provider == "anthropic":
            return AnthropicAdapter(api_key=prov_key or "", model=resolved_model)
        elif provider == "openrouter":
            base_url = vault.get("providers", {}).get("openrouter", {}).get("base_url", "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1")
            return OpenAICompatAdapter(base_url=base_url, api_key=prov_key or "", model=resolved_model, provider_name="openrouter")
        elif provider == "ollama":
            base_url = vault.get("providers", {}).get("ollama", {}).get("base_url", "http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1")
            return OpenAICompatAdapter(base_url=base_url, api_key=prov_key or "ollama", model=resolved_model, provider_name="ollama")

        # Check custom endpoints
        custom_endpoints = vault.get("providers", {}).get("custom_endpoints", [])
        for ep in custom_endpoints:
            if ep.get("id") == provider or ep.get("name") == provider:
                ep_model = resolved_model if (model and model != "default") else (ep.get("model") or "gpt-4o-mini")
                return OpenAICompatAdapter(
                    base_url=ep.get("base_url", "https://api.openai.com/v1"),
                    api_key=ep.get("api_key", ""),
                    model=ep_model,
                    provider_name=ep.get("name", "custom"),
                )

        # Generic OpenAI fallback
        return OpenAICompatAdapter(
            base_url="https://api.openai.com/v1",
            api_key=prov_key or "",
            model=resolved_model,
            provider_name=provider,
        )

    def is_retryable_error(self, error_str: Optional[str]) -> bool:
        """Check if an error indicates rate limiting (429), quota exhaustion, or invalid auth."""
        if not error_str:
            return False
        err_lower = error_str.lower()
        retry_signatures = [
            "429",
            "rate limit",
            "quota",
            "resource_exhausted",
            "too many requests",
            "unauthorized",
            "invalid api key",
            "authentication",
            "401",
        ]
        return any(sig in err_lower for sig in retry_signatures)

    def generate_with_failover(
        self,
        provider: str,
        model: str,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """
        Executes generation with automatic multi-key rotation upon rate limit / quota exhaustion.
        """
        vault = get_vault()
        prov_data = vault.get("providers", {}).get(provider, {})
        keys = prov_data.get("keys", [])

        # If provider has multiple keys, retry up to number of keys
        max_attempts = max(1, len(keys))
        last_response = None

        for attempt in range(max_attempts):
            adapter = self.get_adapter_for_provider(provider, model)
            response = adapter.generate(
                messages=messages,
                temperature=temperature,
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
                json_mode=json_mode,
            )
            last_response = response

            if not response.error:
                # Successful call
                self._emit_telemetry(
                    provider=provider,
                    model=model,
                    latency_ms=response.latency_ms,
                    prompt_tokens=response.prompt_tokens,
                    completion_tokens=response.completion_tokens,
                )
                return response

            # Check if error qualifies for key rotation
            if self.is_retryable_error(response.error) and len(keys) > 1 and attempt < max_attempts - 1:
                new_key = rotate_provider_key(provider)
                print(f"[LLMRouter] Rate limit/auth error on {provider} (Key #{attempt + 1}). Rotated to next key: {new_key[:6]}...")
                continue
            else:
                break

        # Emit telemetry even on error so latency is captured
        if last_response:
            self._emit_telemetry(
                provider=provider,
                model=model,
                latency_ms=last_response.latency_ms,
                prompt_tokens=last_response.prompt_tokens,
                completion_tokens=last_response.completion_tokens,
            )
            return last_response

        return LLMResponse(text="", error="Unknown generation failure", provider=provider, model=model)

    def is_network_error(self, error_str: Optional[str]) -> bool:
        """Check if an error represents a network connection failure or offline condition."""
        if not error_str:
            return False
        err_lower = error_str.lower()
        net_signatures = [
            "connection",
            "connect",
            "network",
            "timeout",
            "timed out",
            "unreachable",
            "connection refused",
            "name resolution",
            "getaddrinfo",
            "dns",
            "failed to establish a new connection",
            "offline",
            "max retries exceeded",
        ]
        return any(sig in err_lower for sig in net_signatures)

    def dispatch_role(
        self,
        role: str,
        messages: List[ChatMessage],
        temperature: float = 0.7,
        max_tokens: int = 1024,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """
        Routes the request to the provider & model configured for the specified role.
        Always attempts user's configured provider first.
        Only falls back to offline_fallback if generate_with_failover returns a network/connection
        error and provider != offline_provider.
        """
        # Always attempt the user's configured (provider, model) first
        provider, model = get_role_target(role)

        resp = self.generate_with_failover(
            provider=provider,
            model=model,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=temperature,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            json_mode=json_mode,
        )

        # Only fall back to offline_fallback if actual network/connection error occurred
        if resp.error and self.is_network_error(resp.error):
            offline_provider, offline_model = get_role_target("offline_fallback")
            if provider != offline_provider:
                print(
                    f"[LLMRouter] Network error on configured provider '{provider}': {resp.error}. "
                    f"Falling back to offline_fallback ({offline_provider}/{offline_model})."
                )
                return self.generate_with_failover(
                    provider=offline_provider,
                    model=offline_model,
                    messages=messages,
                    temperature=temperature,
                    max_tokens=max_tokens,
                    json_mode=json_mode,
                )

        return resp

    def query_text(self, role: str, prompt: str, system_prompt: str = "") -> str:
        """Quick convenience method for text-to-text generation."""
        messages = []
        if system_prompt:
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role="system", content=system_prompt))
        messages.append(ChatMessage(role="user", content=prompt))

        resp = self.dispatch_role(role, messages)
        return resp.text

    def query_json(self, role: str, prompt: str, system_prompt: str = "") -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Quick convenience method for structured JSON generation."""
        messages = []
        if system_prompt:
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role="system", content=system_prompt))
        messages.append(ChatMessage(role="user", content=prompt))

        resp = self.dispatch_role(role, messages, json_mode=True)
        return resp.raw_json


# Global router singleton
router = LLMRouter()

<file path="integrations/handlers/alarm.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Alarm Module
Handles alarm and timer functionality synchronized with config/alarms.json.
"""

import datetime
import json
import os
import re
import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from config.settings import ALARMS_FILE


class AlarmHandler:
    """Handles alarm and countdown timer operations synchronized with config/alarms.json."""

    def __init__(self, jarvis):
        self.jarvis = jarvis
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self.alarms: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.timers: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._load_from_disk()
        self.alarm_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._alarm_checker, daemon=True, name="AlarmChecker")
        self.alarm_thread.start()

    def _load_from_disk(self) -> None:
        """Loads alarms and timers from config/alarms.json."""
        with self._lock:
            if not ALARMS_FILE.exists():
                self.alarms = []
                self.timers = []
                return
            try:
                data = json.loads(ALARMS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                self.alarms = data.get("alarms", [])
                self.timers = data.get("timers", [])
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[AlarmHandler] Load error: {e}")
                self.alarms = []
                self.timers = []

    def _save_to_disk(self) -> None:
        """Persists alarms and timers to config/alarms.json."""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                os.makedirs(ALARMS_FILE.parent, exist_ok=True)
                data = {"alarms": self.alarms, "timers": self.timers}
                ALARMS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[AlarmHandler] Save error: {e}")

    def set_alarm(self, command: str) -> str:
        """
        Set an alarm from a voice or text command and persist to config/alarms.json.
        e.g. 'set alarm for 7:30 AM', 'set alarm for 14:00', 'set alarm for 8 pm'
        """
        time_match = re.search(r'(\d{1,2}):?(\d{2})?\s*(am|pm)?', command, re.IGNORECASE)
        if time_match:
            hour = int(time_match.group(1))
            minute = int(time_match.group(2)) if time_match.group(2) else 0
            period = time_match.group(3)

            if period:
                if period.lower() == 'pm' and hour != 12:
                    hour += 12
                elif period.lower() == 'am' and hour == 12:
                    hour = 0

            time_24 = f"{hour:02d}:{minute:02d}"
            # Friendly 12-hour display string
            dt_dummy = datetime.datetime.now().replace(hour=hour, minute=minute, second=0)
            time_display = dt_dummy.strftime("%I:%M %p").lstrip("0")
            label = f"Alarm for {time_display}"

            alarm_entry = {
                "id": f"alr-{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
                "label": label,
                "time": time_24,
                "type": "alarm",
            }

            self._load_from_disk()
            with self._lock:
                self.alarms.append(alarm_entry)
            self._save_to_disk()

            msg = f"Alarm set for {time_display}, sir."
            self.jarvis.speak(msg)
            return msg
        else:
            msg = "Please specify the time, sir. For example, 'set alarm for 7:30 AM'."
            self.jarvis.speak(msg)
            return msg

    def _alarm_checker(self):
        """Background thread to poll and check alarms and timers from config/alarms.json."""
        while True:
            try:
                self._load_from_disk()
                now = datetime.datetime.now()
                now_hm = now.strftime("%H:%M")
                triggered_alarms = []

                with self._lock:
                    remaining_alarms = []
                    for alarm in self.alarms:
                        alarm_time = alarm.get("time", "").strip()
                        triggered = False
                        if alarm_time:
                            if alarm_time == now_hm:
                                triggered = True
                            else:
                                try:
                                    if "am" in alarm_time.lower() or "pm" in alarm_time.lower():
                                        parsed_t = datetime.datetime.strptime(alarm_time.strip(), "%I:%M %p").time()
                                    else:
                                        parts = alarm_time.split(":")
                                        parsed_t = datetime.time(int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]))
                                    if parsed_t.hour == now.hour and parsed_t.minute == now.minute:
                                        triggered = True
                                except Exception:
                                    pass

                        if triggered:
                            triggered_alarms.append(alarm)
                        else:
                            remaining_alarms.append(alarm)

                    if triggered_alarms:
                        self.alarms = remaining_alarms

                if triggered_alarms:
                    self._save_to_disk()
                    for t_alr in triggered_alarms:
                        label = t_alr.get("label", "Alarm")
                        self.jarvis.speak(f"Sir, your alarm '{label}' is going off!")

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[AlarmChecker] Error: {e}")

            time.sleep(2)  # Check every 2 seconds

    def cancel_alarm(self):
        """Cancel all active alarms."""
        self._load_from_disk()
        if not self.alarms:
            self.jarvis.speak("No active alarms to cancel, sir.")
            return

        with self._lock:
            count = len(self.alarms)
            self.alarms.clear()
        self._save_to_disk()
        self.jarvis.speak(f"All {count} active alarm{'s have' if count > 1 else ' has'} been cancelled, sir.")
</file>

<file path="integrations/handlers/applications.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Applications Module
Handles opening various applications
"""

import os
import subprocess


class ApplicationHandler:
    """Handles opening applications"""

    def __init__(self, jarvis):
        self.jarvis = jarvis
        self.app_mapping = {
            'notepad': 'notepad.exe',
            'calculator': 'calc.exe',
            'chrome': 'chrome.exe',
            'firefox': 'firefox.exe',
            'edge': 'msedge.exe',
            'word': 'winword.exe',
            'excel': 'excel.exe',
            'powerpoint': 'powerpnt.exe',
            'outlook': 'outlook.exe',
            'spotify': 'spotify.exe',
            'vscode': 'code',
            'vs code': 'code',
            'command prompt': 'cmd.exe',
            'terminal': 'cmd.exe',
            'explorer': 'explorer.exe',
            'settings': 'ms-settings:',
            'paint': 'mspaint.exe',
            'task manager': 'taskmgr.exe',
            'whatsapp': 'whatsapp:',
            'antigravity': 'antigravity',
        }

    def open_application(self, command):
        """Open applications or websites based on command"""
        import webbrowser
        cmd = command.lower()

        # 1. Handle YouTube and YouTube searches specifically
        if 'youtube' in cmd:
            query = cmd.replace('open', '').replace('youtube', '').replace('on', '').replace('channel', '').replace("'s", '').strip()
            if query:
                url = f"https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query={query.replace(' ', '+')}"
                self.jarvis.speak(f"Opening YouTube search for {query}")
            else:
                url = "https://www.youtube.com"
                self.jarvis.speak("Opening YouTube")
            webbrowser.open(url)
            return

        # 2. Handle common websites
        websites = {
            'google': 'https://www.google.com',
            'gmail': 'https://mail.google.com',
            'facebook': 'https://www.facebook.com',
            'twitter': 'https://www.twitter.com',
            'instagram': 'https://www.instagram.com',
            'reddit': 'https://www.reddit.com',
            'github': 'https://www.github.com',
            'chatgpt': 'https://chatgpt.com',
            'netflix': 'https://www.netflix.com',
        }
        for site, url in websites.items():
            if site in cmd:
                self.jarvis.speak(f"Opening {site}")
                webbrowser.open(url)
                return

        # 3. Handle local applications from predefined mapping
        app_name = None
        for app in self.app_mapping:
            if app in cmd:
                app_name = app
                break

        if app_name:
            try:
                if app_name in ['settings', 'whatsapp']:
                    os.system(f'start {self.app_mapping[app_name]}')
                else:
                    subprocess.Popen(self.app_mapping[app_name], shell=True)
                self.jarvis.speak(f"Opening {app_name}")
            except Exception:
                self.jarvis.speak(f"I'm unable to open {app_name}")
        else:
            # 4. Smart fallback: strip filler words, search Start Menu shortcuts
            filler = ['open', 'launch', 'start', 'run', 'load', 'pull up']
            guessed_name = cmd
            for word in filler:
                guessed_name = guessed_name.replace(word, '').strip()

            if guessed_name:
                shortcut = self._find_in_start_menu(guessed_name)
                if shortcut:
                    self.jarvis.speak(f"Opening {guessed_name}, sir.")
                    os.startfile(shortcut)
                else:
                    # Last resort: try shell (works for PATH/registry-registered apps)
                    self.jarvis.speak(f"Attempting to open {guessed_name}, sir.")
                    result = os.system(f'start {guessed_name}')
                    if result != 0:
                        self.jarvis.speak(f"I couldn't find {guessed_name} on this system, sir.")
            else:
                self.jarvis.speak("I'm not sure which application you want me to open. Could you be more specific?")

    def _find_in_start_menu(self, app_name):
        """Search Windows Start Menu folders for a matching .lnk shortcut.
        Returns the full path to the best matching shortcut, or None if not found.
        """
        start_menu_dirs = [
            os.path.join(
                os.environ.get('PROGRAMDATA', r'C:\ProgramData'),
                'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Start Menu', 'Programs'
            ),
            os.path.join(
                os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''),
                'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Start Menu', 'Programs'
            ),
        ]

        best_match = None
        best_score = 0
        app_lower = app_name.lower()

        for directory in start_menu_dirs:
            if not os.path.isdir(directory):
                continue
            for root, _, files in os.walk(directory):
                for filename in files:
                    if not filename.lower().endswith('.lnk'):
                        continue
                    name = filename[:-4].lower()  # strip .lnk extension
                    full_path = os.path.join(root, filename)

                    if name == app_lower:
                        return full_path  # Perfect match — return immediately
                    elif name.startswith(app_lower) and len(name) > best_score:
                        best_match = full_path
                        best_score = len(name)
                    elif app_lower in name and best_score == 0:
                        best_match = full_path

        return best_match
</file>

<file path="integrations/handlers/clipboard_copilot.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Universal Clipboard & Selection Copilot for JARVIS 2.0
Enables instant "Act on This" voice and text interactions on clipboard contents:
- "Summarize clipboard / this"
- "Fix this code"
- "Explain this"
- "Translate selection"
"""

import re
from typing import Optional
from core.llm_router import router

try:
    import pyperclip
    PYPERCLIP_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    PYPERCLIP_AVAILABLE = False


class ClipboardCopilot:
    """
    Reads OS clipboard and executes AI-assisted transformations and analysis.
    """

    def get_clipboard_text(self) -> str:
        """Fetch plain text from OS clipboard."""
        if PYPERCLIP_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                return pyperclip.paste() or ""
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ClipboardCopilot] pyperclip paste error: {e}")

        # Fallback using tkinter
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            root = tk.Tk()
            root.withdraw()
            text = root.clipboard_get()
            root.destroy()
            return text or ""
        except Exception:
            return ""

    def set_clipboard_text(self, text: str) -> bool:
        """Write plain text to OS clipboard."""
        if PYPERCLIP_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                pyperclip.copy(text)
                return True
            except Exception:
                pass
        return False

    def act_on_clipboard(self, user_command: str, custom_text: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        """
        Executes action on current clipboard contents based on command query.
        """
        content = custom_text if custom_text is not None else self.get_clipboard_text()
        if not content or not content.strip():
            return "Your clipboard is currently empty, sir. Please copy some text or code first."

        cmd = user_command.lower()

        # 1. Summarization
        if any(k in cmd for k in ["summarize", "tldr", "brief", "summary"]):
            system_prompt = (
                "You are JARVIS. Summarize the following clipboard text concisely. "
                "Highlight key takeaways in crisp bullet points."
            )
            prompt = f"Text to summarize:\n\"\"\"\n{content[:4000]}\n\"\"\""
            return router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)

        # 2. Fix Code
        elif any(k in cmd for k in ["fix code", "debug", "fix this", "correct this code", "find bug"]):
            system_prompt = (
                "You are JARVIS expert software engineer. Review the provided code snippet from the clipboard. "
                "1. Identify the bug or inefficiency.\n"
                "2. Provide the corrected, production-ready code.\n"
                "3. Explain what was fixed in 1-2 concise sentences."
            )
            prompt = f"Code to review and fix:\n```\n{content[:4000]}\n```"
            fixed = router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)
            return fixed

        # 3. Explain Selection
        elif any(k in cmd for k in ["explain", "what does this mean", "break down"]):
            system_prompt = (
                "You are JARVIS. Explain the following text or code simply, clearly, and concisely. "
                "Break down complex terminology or algorithms."
            )
            prompt = f"Content to explain:\n\"\"\"\n{content[:4000]}\n\"\"\""
            return router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)

        # 4. Translation
        elif "translate" in cmd:
            target_lang = "English"
            if "to " in cmd:
                target_lang = cmd.split("to ")[-1].strip().capitalize()
            system_prompt = f"Translate the following text faithfully and accurately into {target_lang}."
            prompt = f"Text to translate:\n\"\"\"\n{content[:4000]}\n\"\"\""
            return router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)

        # Default action: general Q&A grounded on clipboard
        system_prompt = "You are JARVIS. Answer the user query using the following clipboard content as context."
        prompt = f"Context from clipboard:\n\"\"\"\n{content[:4000]}\n\"\"\"\n\nUser Question: {user_command}"
        return router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)


# Global clipboard copilot singleton
clipboard_copilot = ClipboardCopilot()

<file path="integrations/handlers/date_time.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Date and Time Module
Handles time-related functionality
"""

import datetime


class DateTimeHandler:
    """Handles date and time operations"""

    def __init__(self, jarvis):
        self.jarvis = jarvis

    def wish_me(self):
        """Greet based on time of day"""
        hour = datetime.datetime.now().hour
        if 5 <= hour < 12:
            self.jarvis.speak("Good morning, sir.")
        elif 12 <= hour < 17:
            self.jarvis.speak("Good afternoon, sir.")
        elif 17 <= hour < 22:
            self.jarvis.speak("Good evening, sir.")
        else:
            self.jarvis.speak("Good night, sir.")
        self.jarvis.speak("JARVIS at your service. How may I assist you?")

    def tell_time(self):
        """Tell current time"""
        current_time = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%I:%M %p")
        self.jarvis.speak(f"The current time is {current_time}")

    def tell_date(self):
        """Tell current date"""
        current_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%A, %B %d, %Y")
        self.jarvis.speak(f"Today is {current_date}")
