    print("[4] Testing 'what is the date'...")
    res_date = engine.execute_command("what is the date", source="text")

    assert res_date["action"] == "get_date"

<file path="tests/test_llm_features.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Test Suite for JARVIS LLM Features
Verifies Groq Q&A, Gemini fallback, intent classification, and caching.
"""

import sys
import os
from pathlib import Path

# Add project root to sys.path
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE_DIR))

from core.engine import JARVISEngine
from core.tts_engine import TTSEngine


def test_llm_suite():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("VERIFYING JARVIS LLM CAPABILITIES (GROQ & GEMINI)")
    print("=" * 60)

    # Initialize silent engine
    tts = TTSEngine(enabled=False)
    engine = JARVISEngine(tts_engine=tts)

    # 1. Verify Groq & Gemini clients initialized
    print("\n[1] Checking Client Initialization...")
    assert engine.qa_handler.groq_client is not None, "Groq client must be initialized"
    print("  [OK] Groq client initialized.")
    assert engine.genai_client is not None, "Gemini client must be initialized"
    print("  [OK] Gemini client initialized.")

    # 2. Test Groq Q&A via QAHandler
    print("\n[2] Testing Groq Q&A via QAHandler...")
    query = "Who was Albert Einstein?"
    res_q = engine.execute_command(query, source="text")
    print(f"  Query: '{query}'")
    print(f"  Response: {res_q['response']}")
    assert res_q["status"] == "ok", f"Expected status 'ok', got {res_q['status']}"
    assert res_q["action"] == "answer_question"
    assert len(res_q["response"]) > 10, "Response should be substantive"
    print("  [OK] Groq Q&A answered successfully!")

    # 3. Test Local Cache Hit
    print("\n[3] Testing Q&A Cache Hit...")
    res_cached = engine.execute_command(query, source="text")
    print(f"  Cached Response: {res_cached['response']}")
    assert res_cached["status"] == "ok"
    assert res_cached["response"] == res_q["response"], "Cached response should match original"
    print("  [OK] Cache hit verified!")

    # 4. Test Gemini Direct Q&A
    print("\n[4] Testing Gemini Q&A Fallback...")
    gemini_ans = engine.qa_handler._ask_gemini("What is quantum entanglement?")
    print(f"  Gemini Answer: {gemini_ans}")
    assert gemini_ans is not None and len(gemini_ans) > 10, "Gemini should return an answer"
    print("  [OK] Gemini Q&A answered successfully!")

    # 5. Test AI Intent Classification
    print("\n[5] Testing AI Intent Classification (non-local command)...")
    command_query = "I am bored, play something upbeat on youtube"
    res_intent = engine.execute_command(command_query, source="text")
    print(f"  Query: '{command_query}'")
    print(f"  Result Action: {res_intent['action']}")
    print(f"  Response: {res_intent['response']}")
    assert res_intent["status"] == "ok"
    print("  [OK] Intent classification executed successfully!")

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("[ALL LLM FEATURES ARE WORKING PERFECTLY!]")
    print("=" * 60)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_llm_suite()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""

"""
import os
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


class TestLoop0Structure(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_directories_exist(self):
        expected_dirs = [
            "config",
            "core",
            "core/adapters",
            "audio",
            "agent_os",
            "integrations",
            "integrations/handlers",
            "automation",
            "ui",
            "scripts",
            "tests",
        ]
        for d in expected_dirs:
            p = BASE_DIR / d
            self.assertTrue(p.exists() and p.is_dir(), f"Directory missing: {d}")

    def test_config_files_exist(self):
        expected_files = [
            "config/api_vault.json",
            "config/meetings.json",
            "config/alarms.json",
            "config/skills.json",
            "config/mcp_servers.json",
            "config/settings.py",
        ]
        for f in expected_files:
            p = BASE_DIR / f
            self.assertTrue(p.exists() and p.is_file(), f"File missing: {f}")

    def test_audio_imports(self):
        from audio.tts_engine import TTSEngine
        from audio.voice_listener import VoiceListener
        self.assertIsNotNone(TTSEngine)
        self.assertIsNotNone(VoiceListener)

    def test_integrations_handlers_imports(self):
        from integrations.handlers import (
            DateTimeHandler,
            ApplicationHandler,
            MusicHandler,
            WebSearchHandler,
            AlarmHandler,
            WeatherHandler,
            SystemControls,
            SystemInfoHandler,
            QAHandler,
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(DateTimeHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(ApplicationHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(MusicHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(WebSearchHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(AlarmHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(WeatherHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(SystemControls)
        self.assertIsNotNone(SystemInfoHandler)
        self.assertIsNotNone(QAHandler)

    def test_backward_compatibility_shims(self):
        from core.tts_engine import TTSEngine as CoreTTSEngine
        from core.voice_listener import VoiceListener as CoreVoiceListener
        from audio.tts_engine import TTSEngine as AudioTTSEngine
        from audio.voice_listener import VoiceListener as AudioVoiceListener
        self.assertIs(CoreTTSEngine, AudioTTSEngine)
        self.assertIs(CoreVoiceListener, AudioVoiceListener)

    def test_automation_modules_import(self):
        from automation.meeting_scheduler import MeetingScheduler
        self.assertIsNotNone(MeetingScheduler)


if __name__ == "__main__":

<file path="tests/test_loop1_vault.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Test Suite for Loop 1: Multi-Key API Vault, Adapters & Role Router
"""

import json
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
from pathlib import Path

from config.settings import (
    add_provider_key,
    get_active_key,
    get_masked_vault,
    get_role_target,
    get_vault,
    load_vault,
    mask_key,
    remove_provider_key,
    rotate_provider_key,
    save_vault,
)
from core.adapters.base import ChatMessage, LLMResponse
from core.adapters.openai_compat import OpenAICompatAdapter, fetch_models
from core.llm_router import LLMRouter


class TestLoop1Vault(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # Backup vault state
        self.original_vault = get_vault()

    def tearDown(self):
        # Restore original vault
        save_vault(self.original_vault)

    def test_mask_key(self):
        self.assertEqual(mask_key(""), "")
        self.assertEqual(mask_key("short"), "•••••")
        self.assertEqual(mask_key("12345678"), "••••••••")
        long_key = "AIzaSy1234567890abcdef"
        masked = mask_key(long_key)
        self.assertTrue(masked.startswith("AIza"))
        self.assertTrue(masked.endswith("cdef"))
        self.assertIn("•", masked)

    def test_vault_seeding_and_masking(self):
        masked_vault = get_masked_vault()
        self.assertIn("providers", masked_vault)
        self.assertIn("roles", masked_vault)
        gemini_data = masked_vault["providers"].get("gemini", {})
        if gemini_data.get("keys"):
            self.assertIn("keys_masked", gemini_data)
            self.assertEqual(len(gemini_data["keys"]), len(gemini_data["keys_masked"]))

    def test_add_and_remove_provider_key(self):
        test_key = "test-mock-key-12345678"
        added = add_provider_key("groq", test_key)
        self.assertTrue(added)
        vault = get_vault()
        self.assertIn(test_key, vault["providers"]["groq"]["keys"])

        removed = remove_provider_key("groq", test_key)
        self.assertTrue(removed)
        vault_after = get_vault()
        self.assertNotIn(test_key, vault_after["providers"]["groq"]["keys"])

    def test_key_rotation(self):
        # Setup 3 dummy keys for anthropic (unseeded by .env)
        v = get_vault()
        v["providers"]["anthropic"]["keys"] = ["key_alpha", "key_beta", "key_gamma"]
        v["providers"]["anthropic"]["active_index"] = 0
        save_vault(v)

        self.assertEqual(get_active_key("anthropic"), "key_alpha")
        next_k = rotate_provider_key("anthropic")
        self.assertEqual(next_k, "key_beta")
        self.assertEqual(get_active_key("anthropic"), "key_beta")

        next_k = rotate_provider_key("anthropic")
        self.assertEqual(next_k, "key_gamma")

        # Wraps around
        next_k = rotate_provider_key("anthropic")
        self.assertEqual(next_k, "key_alpha")

    def test_custom_openai_endpoint_and_fetch_models(self):
        adapter = OpenAICompatAdapter(
            base_url="http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1",
            api_key="ollama",
            model="qwen2.5:7b",
        )
        self.assertEqual(adapter.base_url, "http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1")
        self.assertEqual(adapter.model, "qwen2.5:7b")

        # Test fetch_models mock
        with patch("requests.get") as mock_get:
            mock_resp = MagicMock()
            mock_resp.status_code = 200
            mock_resp.json.return_value = {
                "data": [{"id": "gpt-4o-mini"}, {"id": "deepseek-chat"}]
            }
            mock_get.return_value = mock_resp

            models = fetch_models("https://api.example.com/v1", "test-key")
            self.assertIn("gpt-4o-mini", models)
            self.assertIn("deepseek-chat", models)

    def test_adapter_connection_signature(self):
        adapter = OpenAICompatAdapter(
            base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999/v1",
            api_key="mock",
            model="mock-model",
        )
        with patch("requests.post") as mock_post:
            mock_resp = MagicMock()
            mock_resp.status_code = 200
            mock_resp.json.return_value = {"choices": [{"message": {"content": "pong"}}]}
            mock_post.return_value = mock_resp

            res = adapter.test_connection()
            self.assertIn("ok", res)
            self.assertIn("latency_ms", res)
            self.assertIn("error", res)
            self.assertTrue(res["ok"])
            self.assertIsNone(res["error"])

    def test_automatic_key_rotation_on_429(self):
        router = LLMRouter()
        v = get_vault()
        v["providers"]["groq"]["keys"] = ["rate_limited_key", "good_key"]
        v["providers"]["groq"]["active_index"] = 0
        save_vault(v)

        call_count = 0

        def mock_generate(*args, **kwargs):
            nonlocal call_count
            call_count += 1
            current_active = get_active_key("groq")
            if current_active == "rate_limited_key":
                return LLMResponse(
                    text="",
                    latency_ms=45,
                    provider="groq",
                    model="llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
                    error="429 Rate limit exceeded. Try again later.",
                )
            else:
                return LLMResponse(
                    text="Success from good key",
                    prompt_tokens=10,
                    completion_tokens=5,
                    total_tokens=15,
                    latency_ms=60,
                    provider="groq",
                    model="llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
                )

        with patch.object(OpenAICompatAdapter, "generate", side_effect=mock_generate), \
             patch.object(LLMRouter, "get_adapter_for_provider") as mock_get_adapter:

            mock_adapter = MagicMock()
            mock_adapter.generate.side_effect = mock_generate
            mock_get_adapter.return_value = mock_adapter

            messages = [ChatMessage(role="user", content="Hello")]
            resp = router.generate_with_failover("groq", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile", messages)

            self.assertEqual(resp.text, "Success from good key")
            self.assertIsNone(resp.error)
            self.assertEqual(get_active_key("groq"), "good_key")
            self.assertEqual(call_count, 2)

    def test_telemetry_emission(self):
        router = LLMRouter()
        telemetry_events = []

        def listener(data):
            telemetry_events.append(data)

        router.register_telemetry_listener(listener)

        with patch.object(LLMRouter, "get_adapter_for_provider") as mock_get_adapter:
            mock_adapter = MagicMock()
            mock_adapter.generate.return_value = LLMResponse(
                text="Telemetry test response",
                prompt_tokens=25,
                completion_tokens=15,
                total_tokens=40,
                latency_ms=120,
                provider="gemini",
                model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            )
            mock_get_adapter.return_value = mock_adapter

            messages = [ChatMessage(role="user", content="Ping")]
            router.generate_with_failover("gemini", "gemini-2.5-flash", messages)

            self.assertEqual(len(telemetry_events), 1)
            event = telemetry_events[0]
            self.assertEqual(event["provider"], "gemini")
            self.assertEqual(event["model"], "gemini-2.5-flash")
            self.assertEqual(event["prompt_tokens"], 25)
            self.assertEqual(event["completion_tokens"], 15)
            self.assertEqual(event["total_session_tokens"], 40)
            self.assertEqual(event["latency_ms"], 120)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<file path="tests/test_loop2_audio_engine.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Test Suite for Loop 2: Audio State Machine, Meeting Interlock & Unified Input Gateway
"""

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
from audio.tts_engine import TTSEngine
from audio.voice_listener import VoiceListener
from core.engine import JARVISEngine


class TestLoop2AudioEngine(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # Create isolated instances
        self.tts = TTSEngine(tts_active=True)
        self.listener = VoiceListener()
        # Keep listener inactive for test isolation
        self.listener.set_mic_active(False)
        # Use silent TTS for engine so process_command tests do not queue real speech
        self.silent_tts = TTSEngine(tts_active=False)
        self.engine = JARVISEngine(tts=self.silent_tts, listener=self.listener)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tts.stop()
        self.silent_tts.stop()
        self.listener.stop()

    def test_tts_toggle_independent(self):
        self.assertTrue(self.tts.get_tts_active())
        self.tts.set_tts_active(False)
        self.assertFalse(self.tts.get_tts_active())

        # Calling speak while inactive does not crash and returns immediately
        self.tts.speak("This is silent test speech", wait=False)
        self.assertFalse(self.tts.is_speaking())

        # Toggle back to active
        new_state = self.tts.toggle_tts()
        self.assertTrue(new_state)
        self.assertTrue(self.tts.get_tts_active())

    def test_mic_toggle_independent(self):
        self.assertFalse(self.listener.get_mic_active())
        self.listener.set_mic_active(True)
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())

        new_state = self.listener.toggle_mic()
        self.assertFalse(new_state)
        self.assertFalse(self.listener.get_mic_active())

    def test_meeting_auto_mute_and_manual_override(self):
        # 1. Start with mic active
        self.listener.set_mic_active(True)
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())

        # 2. Join meeting -> auto-mutes
        self.listener.on_meeting_state_change(True, "Sprint Planning")
        self.assertFalse(self.listener.get_mic_active())
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.meeting_auto_muted)

        # 3. User manually overrides unmute in meeting
        self.listener.set_mic_active(True)
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.manual_override)

        # 4. Leave meeting -> retains manual state
        self.listener.on_meeting_state_change(False, "Sprint Planning")
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())

    def test_meeting_auto_restore(self):
        # 1. Start with mic active
        self.listener.set_mic_active(True)
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())

        # 2. Join meeting -> auto-mutes
        self.listener.on_meeting_state_change(True, "Board Review")
        self.assertFalse(self.listener.get_mic_active())
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.meeting_auto_muted)

        # 3. Leave meeting without manual override -> auto-restores
        self.listener.on_meeting_state_change(False, "Board Review")
        self.assertTrue(self.listener.get_mic_active())

    def test_engine_process_command_time(self):
        res = self.engine.process_command("What time is it?", source="text")
        self.assertEqual(res["sender"], "jarvis")
        self.assertEqual(res["source"], "text")
        self.assertEqual(res["action"], "get_time")
        self.assertIn("time", res["text"].lower())

    def test_engine_process_command_system_status(self):
        res = self.engine.process_command("check system status", source="text")
        self.assertEqual(res["sender"], "jarvis")
        self.assertEqual(res["action"], "system_info")
        self.assertIn("systems nominal", res["text"].lower())
        self.assertIn("cpu", res["data"])
        self.assertIn("ram", res["data"])

    def test_engine_skill_macro_trigger(self):
        with patch.object(self.engine.app_handler, "open_application"):
            res = self.engine.process_command("start work mode", source="text")
            self.assertEqual(res["sender"], "jarvis")
            self.assertEqual(res["action"], "skill_macro")
            self.assertIn("Start Work Mode", res["text"])

    def test_chat_listener_broadcast(self):
        broadcasted = []

        def on_msg(evt):
            broadcasted.append(evt)

        self.engine.register_chat_listener(on_msg)
        self.engine.process_command("what day is it today", source="voice")

        # Must have broadcasted user input and jarvis response
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(broadcasted), 2)
        user_evt = broadcasted[0]
        jarvis_evt = broadcasted[1]

        self.assertEqual(user_evt["sender"], "user")
        self.assertEqual(user_evt["source"], "voice")
        self.assertEqual(jarvis_evt["sender"], "jarvis")
        self.assertEqual(jarvis_evt["action"], "get_date")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<file path="tests/test_loop3_agent_os.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Test Suite for Loop 3: 3-Tier Dynamic OS Agent & Safety Interceptor
"""

import base64
import threading
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
from PIL import Image

from agent_os.code_executor import CodeExecutor
from agent_os.os_actuator import OSActuator
from agent_os.safety_interceptor import SafetyInterceptor
from agent_os.som_vision import SOMVision
from agent_os.ui_distiller import UIDistiller


class TestLoop3AgentOS(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.safety = SafetyInterceptor()
        self.som = SOMVision()
        self.actuator = OSActuator()
        self.executor = CodeExecutor()
        self.distiller = UIDistiller()

    def test_som_vision_rendering(self):
        # Create a synthetic canvas
        test_img = Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), color=(15, 25, 40))
        elements = [
            {"id": 1, "name": "Submit Button", "type": "Button", "bbox": [50, 50, 120, 40], "center": [110, 70]},
            {"id": 2, "name": "Search Box", "type": "Edit", "bbox": [200, 100, 300, 35], "center": [350, 117]},
        ]

        annotated_img, b64_png = self.som.render_som(test_img, elements)
        self.assertIsNotNone(annotated_img)
        self.assertIsInstance(b64_png, str)
        self.assertGreater(len(b64_png), 100)

        # Verify decoded bytes are valid PNG
        decoded = base64.b64decode(b64_png)
        self.assertTrue(decoded.startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"))

    def test_safety_interceptor_detection(self):
        risky_code = """
import os
import shutil
os.remove('C:\\important_file.dat')
"""
        is_safe, reason = self.safety.inspect_code(risky_code)
        self.assertFalse(is_safe)
        self.assertIn("os.remove", reason)

        rmtree_code = "shutil.rmtree('/var/data')"
        is_safe_rm, reason_rm = self.safety.inspect_code(rmtree_code)
        self.assertFalse(is_safe_rm)
        self.assertIn("shutil.rmtree", reason_rm)

        safe_code = """
import math
print("The square root of 144 is", math.sqrt(144))
"""
        is_safe_good, _ = self.safety.inspect_code(safe_code)
        self.assertTrue(is_safe_good)

    def test_safety_interceptor_approval_flow(self):
        # Test approval grant
        approval_result = None

        def requester():
            nonlocal approval_result
            approval_result = self.safety.request_approval(
                action_type="file_delete",
                description="Testing deletion approval",
                preview="os.remove('test.tmp')",
                timeout_sec=5,
            )

        t = threading.Thread(target=requester)
        t.start()

        # Wait for request to register
        time.sleep(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.safety._pending_requests), 1)
        req_id = list(self.safety._pending_requests.keys())[0]

        # User approves
        success = self.safety.approve(req_id)
        self.assertTrue(success)
        t.join(timeout=2.0)
        self.assertTrue(approval_result)

        # Test denial
        denial_result = None

        def requester_deny():
            nonlocal denial_result
            denial_result = self.safety.request_approval(
                action_type="file_delete",
                description="Testing denial flow",
                preview="del *.*",
                timeout_sec=5,
            )

        t2 = threading.Thread(target=requester_deny)
        t2.start()

        time.sleep(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.safety._pending_requests), 1)
        req_id2 = list(self.safety._pending_requests.keys())[0]

        # User denies
        success = self.safety.deny(req_id2)
        self.assertTrue(success)
        t2.join(timeout=2.0)
        self.assertFalse(denial_result)

    def test_code_executor_safe_script(self):
        with patch.object(
            self.executor,
            "synthesize_script",
            return_value="print('CALCULATION_RESULT:', sum(range(10)))",
        ):
            res = self.executor.execute_task("compute sum of 0 to 9")
            self.assertTrue(res["ok"])
            self.assertIn("CALCULATION_RESULT: 45", res["output"])
            self.assertIn("duration_ms", res)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(res["duration_ms"], 0)

    def test_os_actuator_abort_kill_switch(self):
        self.actuator.reset_abort()
        self.assertFalse(self.actuator.is_aborted())

        self.actuator.abort()
        self.assertTrue(self.actuator.is_aborted())

        # When aborted, all actuator commands must abort immediately
        res_click = self.actuator.click(100, 200)
        self.assertFalse(res_click)

        res_type = self.actuator.type_text("test")
        self.assertFalse(res_type)

        res_hotkey = self.actuator.press_hotkey("ctrl+c")
        self.assertFalse(res_hotkey)

    def test_ui_distiller_fallback_grid(self):
        elements = self.distiller.get_interactive_elements(max_elements=12)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(elements), 1)
        first = elements[0]
        self.assertIn("id", first)
        self.assertIn("bbox", first)
        self.assertIn("center", first)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<file path="tests/test_loop4_integrations.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Test Suite for Loop 4: MCP Client, Skills/Teach Mode, Clipboard Copilot, Workspace RAG & Smart Meetings
"""

import os
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from automation.meeting_bot import MeetingBot
from integrations.handlers.clipboard_copilot import ClipboardCopilot
from integrations.mcp_client import MCPClientHub
from integrations.skill_store import SkillStore
from integrations.workspace_rag import WorkspaceRAG


class TestLoop4Integrations(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.skills = SkillStore()
        self.copilot = ClipboardCopilot()
        self.rag = WorkspaceRAG()
        self.mcp = MCPClientHub()
        self.bot = MeetingBot()

    def test_skill_store_add_delete_and_teach_mode(self):
        # 1. Start Teach Mode
        init_res = self.skills.start_teach_mode("Night Routine", ["night routine", "good night"])
        self.assertTrue(self.skills.is_teaching())
        self.assertEqual(init_res["name"], "Night Routine")

        # 2. Record Steps
        self.skills.record_step("mute volume")
        self.skills.record_step("tell me the time")

        # 3. Stop Teach Mode and Save
        saved_skill = self.skills.stop_teach_mode(save=True)
        self.assertFalse(self.skills.is_teaching())
        self.assertIsNotNone(saved_skill)
        self.assertEqual(len(saved_skill["steps"]), 2)

        # 4. Verify skill is present in list
        all_skills = self.skills.get_skills()
        matching = [s for s in all_skills if s.get("id") == saved_skill["id"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(matching), 1)

        # 5. Clean up
        deleted = self.skills.delete_skill(saved_skill["id"])
        self.assertTrue(deleted)

    def test_clipboard_copilot(self):
        sample_text = "JARVIS is an autonomous artificial intelligence operating system."
        with patch("core.llm_router.router.query_text", return_value="Summary: JARVIS is an AI OS."):
            res = self.copilot.act_on_clipboard("summarize this text", custom_text=sample_text)
            self.assertIn("Summary:", res)

        sample_code = "def add(a, b) return a + b"
        with patch("core.llm_router.router.query_text", return_value="Fix: added colon after add(a, b):"):
            res_fix = self.copilot.act_on_clipboard("fix this code", custom_text=sample_code)
            self.assertIn("Fix:", res_fix)

    def test_workspace_rag_indexing_and_search(self):
        temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="jarvis_rag_test_"))
        try:
            # Create sample files
            doc1 = temp_dir / "quantum_computing.md"
            doc1.write_text(
                "# Quantum Teleportation Protocol\n"
                "Quantum state transfer relies on entangled Bell pairs and classical communication channels.\n"
                "Qubits are manipulated using Hadamard and CNOT gates.\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )

            doc2 = temp_dir / "calculator.py"
            doc2.write_text(
                "def calculate_trajectory(velocity, angle):\n"
                "    import math\n"
                "    return (velocity ** 2) * math.sin(2 * angle) / 9.8\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )

            # Index temporary directory
            index_result = self.rag.index_workspace(temp_dir)
            self.assertTrue(index_result["ok"])
            self.assertEqual(index_result["files_indexed"], 2)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(index_result["chunks_count"], 2)

            # Search keyword
            results = self.rag.search("entangled Bell pairs", top_k=2)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(len(results), 1)
            first_match = results[0]
            self.assertEqual(first_match["filename"], "quantum_computing.md")
            self.assertIn("Bell pairs", first_match["text"])

        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_meeting_caption_summarization(self):
        self.bot.live_captions = [
            {"text": "Alice: Welcome everyone to the quarterly roadmap review.", "timestamp": "14:00:10", "platform": "teams"},
            {"text": "Bob: We need to finalize the API vault before Friday.", "timestamp": "14:02:40", "platform": "teams"},
            {"text": "Alice: Agreed. Bob will own the release pipeline.", "timestamp": "14:05:00", "platform": "teams"},
        ]

        with patch("core.llm_router.router.query_text", return_value="Executive Summary: Roadmaps reviewed. Bob owns release pipeline."):
            summary = self.bot._generate_post_call_summary("Quarterly Roadmap Review")
            self.assertIsNotNone(summary)
            self.assertEqual(summary["title"], "Quarterly Roadmap Review")
            self.assertEqual(summary["captions_count"], 3)
            self.assertIn("Executive Summary", summary["summary"])

    def test_mcp_client_hub_list(self):
        tools = self.mcp.list_tools()
        self.assertIsInstance(tools, list)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(tools), 1)
        self.assertEqual(tools[0]["server"], "filesystem")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<file path="tests/test_loop5_and_6.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Integration and End-to-End Tests for Loop 5 & Loop 6
Validates:
1. React Frontend Build artifacts (ui/dist/index.html, ui/dist/assets)
2. FastAPI Static Mounting & SPA route fallback
3. Backend REST Endpoints (/api/vault, /api/meetings, /api/alarms, etc.)
4. WebSocket Telemetry endpoint connectivity
5. Native desktop entry points and launcher artifacts
"""

import os
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

# Ensure root directory is on sys.path
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE_DIR))

from config.settings import UI_DIST_DIR
from automation.server import app


class TestLoop5And6(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.client = TestClient(app)

    def test_ui_dist_bundle_exists(self):
        """Verify React build output exists on disk with HTML and JS/CSS assets."""
        self.assertTrue(UI_DIST_DIR.exists(), "ui/dist directory does not exist")
        index_html = UI_DIST_DIR / "index.html"
        self.assertTrue(index_html.exists(), "ui/dist/index.html does not exist")
        self.assertGreater(index_html.stat().st_size, 0, "ui/dist/index.html is empty")

        assets_dir = UI_DIST_DIR / "assets"
        self.assertTrue(assets_dir.exists(), "ui/dist/assets does not exist")
        asset_files = list(assets_dir.glob("*"))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(asset_files), 2, f"Expected JS and CSS bundles in ui/dist/assets, found: {asset_files}")

    def test_fastapi_spa_serving(self):
        """Verify root route returns 200 with HTML SPA content."""
        res = self.client.get("/")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("text/html", res.headers.get("content-type", ""))
        self.assertTrue("<!DOCTYPE html>" in res.text or "<!doctype html>" in res.text.lower())

    def test_fastapi_spa_fallback(self):
        """Verify arbitrary client-side routes fallback to SPA index.html."""
        res = self.client.get("/settings/vault")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn("text/html", res.headers.get("content-type", ""))

    def test_backend_api_vault_endpoint(self):
        """Verify /api/vault returns masked vault structure."""
        res = self.client.get("/api/vault")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertIn("roles", data)
        self.assertIn("providers", data)
        self.assertIn("autonomous_trust_mode", data)

    def test_backend_api_meetings_endpoint(self):
        """Verify /api/meetings returns dictionary containing meetings and summaries."""
        res = self.client.get("/api/meetings")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertIsInstance(data, dict)
        self.assertIn("meetings", data)
        self.assertIn("summaries", data)
        self.assertIsInstance(data["meetings"], list)

    def test_backend_api_alarms_endpoint(self):
        """Verify /api/alarms returns dictionary containing alarms and timers."""
        res = self.client.get("/api/alarms")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertIsInstance(data, dict)
        self.assertIn("alarms", data)
        self.assertIn("timers", data)

    def test_backend_api_skills_endpoint(self):
        """Verify /api/skills returns list of custom routines."""
        res = self.client.get("/api/skills")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertIsInstance(data, list)

    def test_backend_api_mcp_endpoint(self):
        """Verify /api/mcp returns configured MCP servers and discovered tools."""
        res = self.client.get("/api/mcp")
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertIsInstance(data, dict)
        self.assertIn("servers", data)
        self.assertIn("tools", data)

    def test_main_cli_import_and_engine(self):
        """Verify main.py dependencies and core engine input gateway."""
        import main
        from core.engine import engine

        self.assertTrue(hasattr(main, "main"))
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(main, "start_backend"))

        # Test processing command through engine
        res = engine.process_command("what time is it", source="test")
        self.assertIsInstance(res, dict)
        self.assertIn("text", res)
        self.assertGreater(len(res["text"]), 0)


    def test_teach_mode_start_and_stop(self):
        """Verify POST /api/skills/teach/start and POST /api/skills/teach/stop."""
        from core.engine import engine

        # Start teach mode
        res_start = self.client.post("/api/skills/teach/start", json={
            "name": "Integration Test Routine",
            "trigger_phrases": ["run integration test", "test routine"],
        })
        self.assertEqual(res_start.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(res_start.json().get("ok"))

        # Check status
        res_status = self.client.get("/api/skills/teach/status")
        self.assertEqual(res_status.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(res_status.json().get("is_teaching"))

        # Engine processes a command while teaching
        engine.process_command("what is the date", source="text")

        # Stop and save
        res_stop = self.client.post("/api/skills/teach/stop", json={"save": True})
        self.assertEqual(res_stop.status_code, 200)
        data = res_stop.json()
        self.assertTrue(data.get("ok"))
        saved = data.get("saved")
        self.assertIsNotNone(saved)
        self.assertEqual(saved.get("name"), "Integration Test Routine")

        # Clean up created skill
        if saved and saved.get("id"):
            self.client.delete(f"/api/skills/{saved['id']}")

    def test_meetings_scheduler_and_leave(self):
        """Verify POST /api/meetings schedules via MeetingScheduler and /api/meetings/leave works."""
        meeting_payload = {
            "title": "Integration Standup Test",
            "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij",
            "start_time": "2026-10-21T16:00:00",
            "auto_leave": True,
        }
        res_add = self.client.post("/api/meetings", json=meeting_payload)
        self.assertEqual(res_add.status_code, 200)
        data_add = res_add.json()
        self.assertTrue(data_add.get("ok"))
        created_id = data_add.get("meeting", {}).get("id")
        self.assertIsNotNone(created_id)

        # Verify meeting retrieved via GET /api/meetings
        res_get = self.client.get("/api/meetings")
        self.assertEqual(res_get.status_code, 200)
        meetings = res_get.json().get("meetings", [])
        self.assertTrue(any(m.get("id") == created_id for m in meetings))

        # Test leave meeting endpoint
        res_leave = self.client.post("/api/meetings/leave")
        self.assertEqual(res_leave.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(res_leave.json().get("ok"))

        # Clean up test meeting
        res_del = self.client.delete(f"/api/meetings/{created_id}")
        self.assertEqual(res_del.status_code, 200)

    def test_delete_masked_key(self):
        """Verify deleting an API key by its masked string representation."""
        test_key = "sk_live_integration_test_secret_9999"
        # Add key
        res_add = self.client.post("/api/vault/key", json={
            "provider": "groq",
            "key": test_key,
            "action": "add",
        })
        self.assertEqual(res_add.status_code, 200)

        # Get masked vault
        res_vault = self.client.get("/api/vault")
        self.assertEqual(res_vault.status_code, 200)
        vault_data = res_vault.json()
        groq_masked = vault_data.get("providers", {}).get("groq", {}).get("keys_masked", [])
        self.assertGreater(len(groq_masked), 0)
        masked_val = groq_masked[-1]

        # Delete by masked key
        res_del = self.client.post("/api/vault/key", json={
            "provider": "groq",
            "key": masked_val,
            "action": "remove",
        })
        self.assertEqual(res_del.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(res_del.json().get("ok"))

    def test_voice_text_alarm_persistence(self):
        """Verify voice/text alarm commands persist to config/alarms.json."""
        import json
        from config.settings import ALARMS_FILE
        from core.engine import engine

        res = engine.process_command("set alarm for 6:45 AM", source="text")
        self.assertIn("6:45", res.get("text", ""))

        self.assertTrue(ALARMS_FILE.exists())
        alarms_data = json.loads(ALARMS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        alarms_list = alarms_data.get("alarms", [])
        matching = [a for a in alarms_list if "06:45" in a.get("time", "") or "6:45" in a.get("time", "")]
        self.assertGreater(len(matching), 0, f"Expected alarm for 06:45 in {alarms_list}")

        # Clean up
        alarms_data["alarms"] = [a for a in alarms_list if a not in matching]
        ALARMS_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(alarms_data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    # ── NEW: FIX 1 — Default model resolution ────────────────────────
    def test_default_model_resolution(self):
        """Verify POST /api/vault/test with no model resolves a real default, not 'default'."""
        from core.llm_router import router

        # Test that get_adapter_for_provider resolves model for gemini
        adapter = router.get_adapter_for_provider("gemini", model="", api_key="test-key")
        self.assertNotEqual(adapter.model, "default", "Model should not be 'default'")
        self.assertNotEqual(adapter.model, "", "Model should not be empty")
        self.assertEqual(adapter.model, "gemini-2.5-flash", f"Expected 'gemini-2.5-flash', got '{adapter.model}'")

        # Test that 'default' string also resolves
        adapter2 = router.get_adapter_for_provider("groq", model="default", api_key="test-key")
        self.assertNotEqual(adapter2.model, "default")
        self.assertEqual(adapter2.model, "llama-3.3-70b-versatile")

        # Test via REST endpoint
        res = self.client.post("/api/vault/test", json={"provider": "gemini"})
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        # The response should not mention "default" as the model used

    # ── NEW: FIX 2 — Timezone-aware meeting creation ─────────────────
    def test_timezone_aware_meeting_creation(self):
        """Verify POST /api/meetings with a UTC 'Z' timestamp doesn't throw naive/aware TypeError."""
        meeting_payload = {
            "title": "UTC Timezone Test Meeting",
            "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/utc-test-xyz",
            "start_time": "2026-10-21T18:00:00Z",
            "auto_leave": True,
        }
        res = self.client.post("/api/meetings", json=meeting_payload)
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200, f"Meeting creation failed: {res.text}")
        data = res.json()
        self.assertTrue(data.get("ok"), f"Meeting creation returned ok=False: {data}")
        created_id = data.get("meeting", {}).get("id")
        self.assertIsNotNone(created_id)

        # Verify the meeting start_time was stored without timezone info
        meeting = data.get("meeting", {})
        st = meeting.get("start_time", "")
        # Should not contain '+00:00' or 'Z' — it's naive local time
        self.assertNotIn("+00:00", str(st), "start_time should be naive (no timezone)")

        # Clean up
        if created_id:
            self.client.delete(f"/api/meetings/{created_id}")

    # ── NEW: FIX 5 — Multi-turn context memory ──────────────────────
    def test_multi_turn_context_memory(self):
        """Verify engine.context records user and assistant messages."""
        from core.engine import engine
        from core.context_manager import context_mgr

        # Clear existing context
        context_mgr.clear()
        self.assertEqual(len(context_mgr._history), 0)

        # Add user message
        context_mgr.add_user_message("Hello, who are you?")
        self.assertEqual(len(context_mgr._history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(context_mgr._history[0].role, "user")
        self.assertEqual(context_mgr._history[0].content, "Hello, who are you?")

        # Add assistant message
        context_mgr.add_assistant_message("I am JARVIS, sir.")
        self.assertEqual(len(context_mgr._history), 2)
        self.assertEqual(context_mgr._history[1].role, "assistant")

        # get_messages should include system prompt + history
        messages = context_mgr.get_messages()
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(messages), 3)
        self.assertEqual(messages[0].role, "system")
        self.assertEqual(messages[1].role, "user")
        self.assertEqual(messages[2].role, "assistant")

        # Clear memory
        context_mgr.clear()
        self.assertEqual(len(context_mgr._history), 0)

        # Verify engine has is_thinking attribute
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(engine, "is_thinking"), "Engine should have is_thinking attribute")
        self.assertFalse(engine.is_thinking, "is_thinking should be False when idle")

    # ── NEW: FIX 5 — Clear memory command ────────────────────────────
    def test_clear_memory_command(self):
        """Verify 'clear memory' command clears context and returns confirmation."""
        from core.engine import engine

        # Pre-fill context
        engine.context.add_user_message("test message")
        self.assertGreater(len(engine.context._history), 0)

        # Send clear memory command
        res = engine.process_command("clear memory", source="test")
        self.assertIn("cleared", res.get("text", "").lower())

        # Context should be empty
        self.assertEqual(len(engine.context._history), 0)

    # ── NEW: FIX 5 — Regression Tests for Critical Fixes ─────────────
    def test_current_year_deterministic(self):
        """Verify 'what year is it now' returns '2026' deterministically and never '2024'."""
        from core.engine import engine
        from datetime import datetime

        res = engine.process_command("what year is it now", source="test")
        self.assertIn("2026", res.get("text", ""))
        self.assertNotIn("2024", res.get("text", ""))
        self.assertEqual(res.get("action"), "get_date")
        self.assertEqual(res.get("data", {}).get("year"), datetime.now().year)

        res_which = engine.process_command("which year is it", source="test")
        self.assertIn("2026", res_which.get("text", ""))
        self.assertEqual(res_which.get("action"), "get_date")

    def test_is_internet_connected_performance(self):
        """Verify is_internet_connected() executes in < 5ms (non-blocking cached read)."""
        import time
        from config.settings import is_internet_connected

        durations = []
        for _ in range(25):
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            state = is_internet_connected()
            durations.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)

        avg_ms = sum(durations) / len(durations)
        max_ms = max(durations)
        self.assertLess(avg_ms, 5.0, f"Average execution time {avg_ms:.3f}ms exceeds 5ms limit")
        self.assertLess(max_ms, 10.0, f"Max execution time {max_ms:.3f}ms is unexpectedly high")
        self.assertIsInstance(state, bool)

    def test_malformed_llm_intent_string_sanitization(self):
        """Verify malformed LLM intent strings never leak into the action field."""
        from core.engine import engine

        res = engine._finalize_response(
            text="Testing response text",
            source="test",
            action="general_qai systems are active...",
            data={},
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(res.get("action"), "general_qai systems are active...")
        self.assertEqual(res.get("action"), "general_qa")

    def test_current_events_web_research_routing(self):
        """Verify queries about 'most heard song 2025 on spotify' route to live web_research."""
        from core.engine import engine

        with patch("automation.web_agent.web_agent.research") as mock_research:

                "summary": "In 2025, the most streamed song on Spotify was 'Birds of a Feather' by Billie Eilish.",
                "sources": ["https://spotify.com"],
            }
            res = engine.process_command("most heard song 2025 on spotify", source="test")
            self.assertEqual(res.get("action"), "web_research")
            self.assertIn("Billie Eilish", res.get("text", ""))
            self.assertNotIn("cannot predict the future", res.get("text", "").lower())
            mock_research.assert_called_once()

    def test_extract_json_from_text(self):
        """Verify extract_json_from_text parses fenced code blocks and embedded JSON objects."""
        from core.adapters.base import extract_json_from_text

        fenced = "```json\n{\"intent\": \"general_qa\", \"target\": \"test\"}\n```"
        data = extract_json_from_text(fenced)
        self.assertEqual(data, {"intent": "general_qa", "target": "test"})

        embedded = "Here is the response: {\"intent\": \"web_research\"} and more text."
        data2 = extract_json_from_text(embedded)
        self.assertEqual(data2, {"intent": "web_research"})

        invalid = "No json here."
        self.assertIsNone(extract_json_from_text(invalid))

    def test_context_manager_dynamic_year_injection(self):
        """Verify ContextManager dynamically injects the current year and real-time clock into system messages."""

    def test_parse_schedule_command_recurrence_and_quoted_title(self):
        """Verify scheduler.parse_schedule_command extracts quoted title, strips url quotes, and handles recurrence."""
        from automation.meeting_scheduler import MeetingScheduler
        scheduler = MeetingScheduler()
        cmd = 'schedule a meeting named "cn" with link "https://teams.microsoft.com/l/meetup-join/123" at 2:30 PM, daily till 13th nov'
        parsed = scheduler.parse_schedule_command(cmd)
        self.assertIsNotNone(parsed)
        self.assertEqual(parsed["title"], "cn")
        self.assertEqual(parsed["meeting_url"], "https://teams.microsoft.com/l/meetup-join/123")
        self.assertFalse(parsed["meeting_url"].endswith('"'))
        self.assertGreater(parsed.get("recurrence_days", 1), 1)

    def test_api_meetings_join_transitions_status(self):
        """Verify POST /api/meetings/{id}/join transitions meeting status from pending to active."""
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta
        from core.engine import engine

        if not getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None):
            from automation.meeting_handler import MeetingHandler
            engine.meeting_handler = MeetingHandler(engine)

        scheduler = engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
        start = datetime.now() + timedelta(hours=2)
        m = scheduler.add_meeting(
            title="Status Transition Test",
            meeting_url="https://meet.google.com/test-join-status",
            start_time=start,
            end_time=start + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        mid = m["id"]
        self.assertEqual(m["status"], "pending")

        bot = engine.meeting_handler.bot
        orig_join = getattr(bot, "join_meeting", None)
        if bot:
            bot.join_meeting = lambda *args, **kwargs: None

        try:
            res = self.client.post(f"/api/meetings/{mid}/join")
            self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
            self.assertTrue(res.json().get("ok"))
            updated = scheduler.get_by_id(mid)
            self.assertIsNotNone(updated)
            self.assertIn(updated["status"], ("active", "completed"))
        finally:
            if bot and orig_join:
                bot.join_meeting = orig_join
            self.client.delete(f"/api/meetings/{mid}")

    def test_api_meetings_bulk_import(self):
        """Verify POST /api/meetings/bulk imports multiple meetings into the scheduler."""
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta
        from core.engine import engine

        if not getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None):
            from automation.meeting_handler import MeetingHandler
            engine.meeting_handler = MeetingHandler(engine)

        scheduler = engine.meeting_handler.scheduler
        t1 = (datetime.now() + timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
        t2 = (datetime.now() + timedelta(days=2)).isoformat()
        entries = [
            {
                "title": "Bulk Sync A",
                "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/bulk-aaa",
                "start_time": t1,
            },
            {
                "title": "Bulk Sync B",
                "meeting_url": "https://teams.microsoft.com/bulk-bbb",
                "start_time": t2,
            },
        ]
        res = self.client.post("/api/meetings/bulk", json=entries)
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        data = res.json()
        self.assertTrue(data.get("ok"))
        self.assertEqual(data.get("count"), 2)

        all_meetings = scheduler.get_all()
        titles = [m["title"] for m in all_meetings]
        self.assertIn("Bulk Sync A", titles)
        self.assertIn("Bulk Sync B", titles)

        # Cleanup
        for m in all_meetings:
            if m["title"] in ("Bulk Sync A", "Bulk Sync B"):
                self.client.delete(f"/api/meetings/{m['id']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/ApiKeyVault.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Key, Plus, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff, RefreshCw, Zap, ChevronDown, ChevronRight,
  Server, Shield, ShieldCheck, Globe, Cpu, Link2, CheckCircle2, XCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';

/* ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
interface VaultProvider {
  keys: string[];
}

interface CustomEndpoint {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  base_url: string;
  api_key: string;
  model: string;
}

interface Roles {
  intent_router: { provider: string; model: string };
  qa_chat: { provider: string; model: string };
  vision_agent: { provider: string; model: string };
  offline_fallback: { provider: string; model: string };
}

interface VaultData {
  providers: Record<string, VaultProvider | CustomEndpoint[]>;
  roles: Roles;
  autonomous_trust_mode: boolean;
  workspace_path: string;
}

interface ApiKeyVaultProps {
  apiBase: string;
}

/* ── Provider metadata ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const PROVIDERS = [
  { id: 'gemini', label: 'Gemini', icon: <Cpu size={16} /> },
  { id: 'groq', label: 'Groq', icon: <Zap size={16} /> },
  { id: 'anthropic', label: 'Anthropic', icon: <Shield size={16} /> },
  { id: 'openrouter', label: 'OpenRouter', icon: <Globe size={16} /> },
  { id: 'ollama', label: 'Ollama', icon: <Server size={16} /> },
  { id: 'openweathermap', label: 'OpenWeatherMap', icon: <Globe size={16} /> },
];

const ROLE_IDS = ['intent_router', 'qa_chat', 'vision_agent', 'offline_fallback'] as const;

/* ── Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export const ApiKeyVault: React.FC<ApiKeyVaultProps> = ({ apiBase }) => {
  const [vault, setVault] = useState<VaultData | null>(null);
  const [reveal, setReveal] = useState(false);
  const [newKeys, setNewKeys] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [testResults, setTestResults] = useState<Record<string, { ok: boolean; msg: string }>>({});

  // Custom endpoint builder state
  const [ceExpanded, setCeExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [ceName, setCeName] = useState('');
  const [ceUrl, setCeUrl] = useState('');
  const [ceApiKey, setCeApiKey] = useState('');
  const [ceModel, setCeModel] = useState('');
  const [ceFetchedModels, setCeFetchedModels] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [ceTestResult, setCeTestResult] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Roles
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<Roles | null>(null);
  const [trustMode, setTrustMode] = useState(false);

  const fetchVault = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault?reveal=${reveal}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setVault(data);
      if (data.roles) setRoles(data.roles);
      if (typeof data.autonomous_trust_mode === 'boolean') setTrustMode(data.autonomous_trust_mode);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [apiBase, reveal]);

  useEffect(() => { fetchVault(); }, [fetchVault]);

  /* ── Key Management ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const addKey = async (provider: string) => {
    const key = newKeys[provider]?.trim();
    if (!key) return;
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/key`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ provider, key, action: 'add' }),
    });
    setNewKeys((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: '' }));
    fetchVault();
  };

  const removeKey = async (provider: string, keyVal: string, keyIdx?: number) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/key`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ provider, key: keyVal, key_index: keyIdx, action: 'remove' }),
    });
    fetchVault();
  };

  const testConnection = async (provider: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/test`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ provider }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setTestResults((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        [provider]: { ok: data.ok !== false, msg: data.error || 'Connected' },
      }));
    } catch {
      setTestResults((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: { ok: false, msg: 'Network error' } }));
    }
  };

  /* ── Custom Endpoint ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const fetchModels = async () => {
    if (!ceUrl.trim()) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/fetch-models`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ base_url: ceUrl, api_key: ceApiKey }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setCeFetchedModels(data.models || []);
    } catch {
      setCeFetchedModels([]);
    }
  };

  const testCustomEndpoint = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/test`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ provider: 'custom', base_url: ceUrl, api_key: ceApiKey, model: ceModel }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setCeTestResult(data.ok !== false ? '✓ Connection successful' : `✗ ${data.error || 'Failed'}`);
    } catch {
      setCeTestResult('✗ Network error');
    }
  };

  const saveCustomEndpoint = async () => {
    const ep: CustomEndpoint = {
      id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
      name: ceName || 'Custom Endpoint',
      base_url: ceUrl,
      api_key: ceApiKey,
      model: ceModel,
    };
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/custom-endpoint`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'add', endpoint: ep }),
    });
    setCeName(''); setCeUrl(''); setCeApiKey(''); setCeModel('');
    setCeFetchedModels([]); setCeTestResult(null);
    fetchVault();
  };

  const deleteCustomEndpoint = async (id: string) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/custom-endpoint`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'delete', endpoint_id: id }),
    });
    fetchVault();
  };

  /* ── Roles ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const updateRole = async (roleId: string, field: 'provider' | 'model', value: string) => {
    if (!roles) return;
    const updated = { ...roles, [roleId]: { ...roles[roleId as keyof Roles], [field]: value } };
    setRoles(updated);
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/roles`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ roles: updated }),
    });
  };

  const handleProviderChange = async (roleId: string, newProvider: string) => {
    if (!roles) return;
    // Find default model for the new provider
    let defaultModel = '';
    const builtinModels = DEFAULT_MODELS_BY_PROVIDER[newProvider];
    if (builtinModels && builtinModels.length > 0) {
      defaultModel = builtinModels[0];
    } else {
      // Check if newProvider is a saved custom endpoint
      const eps = customEndpoints();
      const ep = eps.find((e) => e.name === newProvider || e.id === newProvider);
      if (ep && ep.model) {
        defaultModel = ep.model;
      }
    }
    const updated = { ...roles, [roleId]: { provider: newProvider, model: defaultModel } };
    setRoles(updated);
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/roles`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ roles: updated }),
    });
  };

  const toggleTrustMode = async () => {
    const next = !trustMode;
    setTrustMode(next);
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/roles`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ autonomous_trust_mode: next }),
    });
  };

  /* ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  interface KeyItem {
    display: string;
    raw: string;
    index: number;
  }

  const DEFAULT_MODELS_BY_PROVIDER: Record<string, string[]> = {
    gemini: ['gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-pro', 'gemini-2.0-flash'],
    groq: ['llama-3.3-70b-versatile', 'llama-3.1-8b-instant', 'mixtral-8x7b-32768', 'deepseek-r1-distill-llama-70b'],
    anthropic: ['claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022', 'claude-3-5-haiku-20241022', 'claude-3-opus-20240229'],
    openrouter: ['google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free', 'meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct', 'anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet'],
    ollama: ['llama3', 'mistral', 'qwen2.5-coder', 'deepseek-r1'],
  };

  const providerKeys = (provider: string): KeyItem[] => {
    if (!vault || !vault.providers) return [];
    const p: any = vault.providers[provider];
    if (!p || Array.isArray(p)) return [];
    const rawList: string[] = p.keys || [];
    const maskedList: string[] = p.keys_masked || rawList.map((k) => k.length > 8 ? `${k.slice(0, 4)}...${k.slice(-4)}` : '••••••••');
    const displayList = reveal ? rawList : (maskedList.length > 0 ? maskedList : rawList);
    return displayList.map((disp, idx) => ({
      display: disp,
      raw: rawList[idx] || disp,
      index: idx,
    }));
  };

  const customEndpoints = (): CustomEndpoint[] => {
    if (!vault || !vault.providers) return [];
    const ce = vault.providers.custom_endpoints;
    if (Array.isArray(ce)) return ce as CustomEndpoint[];
    return [];
  };

  const inputClasses = 'bg-void/60 border border-cyber text-ice text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded focus:border-cyan-arc focus:outline-none w-full font-mono';

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/30 p-4 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <Key size={18} className="text-gold-reactor" />
          <span className="font-hud text-sm font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">API KEY VAULT</span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => { setReveal(!reveal); }}
          className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-xs text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc transition-colors"
        >
          {reveal ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
          <span>{reveal ? 'Mask Keys' : 'Reveal Keys'}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Provider Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        {PROVIDERS.map(({ id, label, icon }) => {
          const keys = providerKeys(id);
          const test = testResults[id];
          return (
            <div key={id} className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  {icon}
                  <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-cyan-arc">{label.toUpperCase()}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">({keys.length} key{keys.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''})</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => testConnection(id)}
                  className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyber text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors"
                >
                  Test
                </button>
              </div>

              {test && (
                <div className={`flex items-center space-x-1 text-[10px] ${test.ok ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-crimson'}`}>
                  {test.ok ? <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> : <XCircle size={12} />}
                  <span>{test.msg}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {keys.map((k) => (
                <div key={k.index} className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                  <span className="flex-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-steel truncate">{k.display}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => removeKey(id, k.display, k.index)} className="text-crimson hover:text-red-300">
                    <Trash2 size={12} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}

              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <input
                  className={inputClasses}
                  placeholder={`Add ${label} key...`}
                  value={newKeys[id] || ''}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewKeys((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: e.target.value }))}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && addKey(id)}
                />
                <button
                  onClick={() => addKey(id)}
                  className="p-1.5 rounded bg-cyber hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc transition-colors"
                >
                  <Plus size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Custom OpenAI-Compatible Endpoint Builder */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <button
          onClick={() => setCeExpanded(!ceExpanded)}
          className="flex items-center space-x-2 w-full text-left"
        >
          {ceExpanded ? <ChevronDown size={14} className="text-cyan-arc" /> : <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-steel" />}
          <Link2 size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-gold-reactor">CUSTOM OPENAI-COMPATIBLE ENDPOINT</span>
        </button>

        {ceExpanded && (
          <div className="space-y-2 pl-6">
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Endpoint Name" value={ceName} onChange={(e) => setCeName(e.target.value)} />
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Base URL (e.g. https://api.openai.com/v1)" value={ceUrl} onChange={(e) => setCeUrl(e.target.value)} />
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="API Key (optional for Ollama)" value={ceApiKey} onChange={(e) => setCeApiKey(e.target.value)} />

            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              {ceFetchedModels.length > 0 ? (
                <select
                  className={`${inputClasses} cursor-pointer`}
                  value={ceModel}
                  onChange={(e) => setCeModel(e.target.value)}
                >
                  <option value="">Select model…</option>
                  {ceFetchedModels.map((m) => <option key={m} value={m}>{m}</option>)}
                </select>
              ) : (
                <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Model ID" value={ceModel} onChange={(e) => setCeModel(e.target.value)} />
              )}
              <button
                onClick={fetchModels}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1 whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-2 rounded border border-cyber text-xs text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors"
              >
                <RefreshCw size={12} /> <span>Fetch Models</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {ceTestResult && (
              <div className={`text-[11px] ${ceTestResult.startsWith('✓') ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-crimson'}`}>{ceTestResult}</div>
            )}

            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <button onClick={testCustomEndpoint} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-cyber text-xs text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors">
                Test Endpoint
              </button>
              <button onClick={saveCustomEndpoint} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc/30 text-xs text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors">
                Save Endpoint
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Saved Custom Endpoints */}
            {customEndpoints().map((ep) => {
              const epTestKey = `ep-${ep.id}`;
              const epTest = testResults[epTestKey];
              return (
                <div key={ep.id} className="bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2 text-[11px] text-steel space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-ice font-bold">{ep.name}</span> — <span className="font-mono">{ep.base_url}</span>
                      {ep.model && <span className="ml-2 text-gold-reactor">[{ep.model}]</span>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={async () => {
                          try {
                            const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/test`, {
                              method: 'POST',
                              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                              body: JSON.stringify({ provider: ep.id, base_url: ep.base_url, api_key: ep.api_key, model: ep.model }),
                            });
                            const data = await res.json();
                            setTestResults((prev) => ({
                              ...prev,
                              [epTestKey]: { ok: data.ok !== false, msg: data.error || 'Connected' },
                            }));
                          } catch {
                            setTestResults((prev) => ({ ...prev, [epTestKey]: { ok: false, msg: 'Network error' } }));
                          }
                        }}
                        className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyber text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors"
                      >
                        Test
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => deleteCustomEndpoint(ep.id)} className="text-crimson hover:text-red-300"><Trash2 size={12} /></button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {epTest && (
                    <div className={`flex items-center space-x-1 text-[10px] ${epTest.ok ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-crimson'}`}>
                      {epTest.ok ? <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> : <XCircle size={12} />}
                      <span>{epTest.msg}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Role Router Matrix */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <Cpu size={16} className="text-cyan-arc" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">ROLE ROUTER MATRIX</span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          {ROLE_IDS.map((roleId) => {
            const role = roles?.[roleId] || { provider: 'gemini', model: 'gemini-2.5-flash' };
            const endpoints = customEndpoints();
            const providerOptions = [
              'gemini',
              'groq',
              'anthropic',
              'openrouter',
              'ollama',
              ...endpoints.map((ep) => ep.name || ep.id),
            ];
            const currentProvider = role.provider || 'gemini';
            const modelSuggestions = DEFAULT_MODELS_BY_PROVIDER[currentProvider] || [];
            const datalistId = `models-${roleId}`;

            return (
              <div key={roleId} className="bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2.5 space-y-1.5">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-hud tracking-wider text-gold-reactor">
                  {roleId.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                </span>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <select
                    className={`${inputClasses} text-[11px] cursor-pointer`}
                    value={role.provider}
                    onChange={(e) => handleProviderChange(roleId, e.target.value)}
                  >
                    {providerOptions.map((p) => (
                      <option key={p} value={p}>{p}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  <input
                    list={datalistId}
                    className={`${inputClasses} text-[11px]`}
                    placeholder="Model ID (select or type)..."
                    value={role.model}
                    onChange={(e) => updateRole(roleId, 'model', e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <datalist id={datalistId}>
                    {modelSuggestions.map((m) => (
                      <option key={m} value={m} />
                    ))}
                  </datalist>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Autonomous Trust Mode */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            {trustMode ? <ShieldCheck size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" /> : <Shield size={16} className="text-steel" />}
            <span className="text-xs text-ice">Autonomous Trust Mode</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">(skip safety approval)</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={toggleTrustMode}
            className={`relative w-10 h-5 rounded-full transition-colors ${trustMode ? 'bg-gold-reactor/40' : 'bg-cyber'}`}
          >
            <span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full transition-all ${trustMode ? 'left-5 bg-gold-reactor' : 'left-0.5 bg-steel'}`} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ApiKeyVault;
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/ApprovalModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { ShieldAlert, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

/* ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export interface ApprovalRequest {
  request_id: string;
  action_type: string;
  description: string;
  preview?: string;
  timeout_seconds?: number;
  timeout_sec?: number;
}

interface ApprovalModalProps {
  request: ApprovalRequest | null;
  apiBase: string;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

/* ── Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export const ApprovalModal: React.FC<ApprovalModalProps> = ({ request, apiBase, onDismiss }) => {
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(0);
  const [responding, setResponding] = useState(false);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval>>();

  const totalTimeout = request ? (request.timeout_sec || request.timeout_seconds || 15) : 15;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!request) return;
    const timeout = request.timeout_sec || request.timeout_seconds || 15;
    setCountdown(timeout);
    setResponding(false);

    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setCountdown((prev) => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          clearInterval(timerRef.current);
          return 0;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);

    return () => { if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current); };
  }, [request]);

  const respond = async (approved: boolean) => {
    if (!request || responding) return;
    setResponding(true);
    try {
      await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/approval/${request.request_id}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ approved }),
      });
    } catch { /* network error handled gracefully */ }
    onDismiss();
  };

  if (!request) return null;

  const progressPct = totalTimeout > 0 ? (countdown / totalTimeout) * 100 : 100;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center">
      {/* Backdrop */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-void/80 backdrop-blur-sm" />

      {/* Modal */}
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md mx-4 hud-panel hud-chamfer p-0 border-crimson/50 shadow-crimson-glow animate-pulse-glow">
        {/* Countdown bar */}
        <div className="h-1 bg-void/60 rounded-t overflow-hidden">
          <div
            className="h-full transition-all duration-1000 ease-linear"
            style={{
              width: `${progressPct}%`,
              background: countdown > 10
                ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #00E5FF, #00E5FF)'
                : countdown > 5
                ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #FFB300, #FFB300)'
                : 'linear-gradient(90deg, #FF3B5C, #FF3B5C)',
            }}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-full bg-crimson/10 border border-crimson/30">
              <ShieldAlert size={22} className="text-crimson" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-hud text-sm font-bold tracking-wider text-crimson glow-crimson">
                SAFETY APPROVAL REQUIRED
              </h3>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mt-0.5">
                <AlertTriangle size={12} className="text-gold-reactor" />
                <span className="text-[11px] text-gold-reactor font-mono">{request.action_type.toUpperCase()}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Description */}
          <div className="bg-void/60 border border-cyber rounded p-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-ice leading-relaxed">{request.description}</p>
          </div>

          {/* Code/Command Preview */}
          {request.preview && (
            <div className="bg-void/80 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-3 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">
              <pre className="text-[11px] font-mono text-steel whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">{request.preview}</pre>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Countdown */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-2">
            <Clock size={14} className={countdown <= 5 ? 'text-crimson' : 'text-steel'} />
            <span className={`text-sm font-mono ${countdown <= 5 ? 'text-crimson glow-crimson' : 'text-steel'}`}>
              {countdown}s remaining
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <button
              onClick={() => respond(false)}
              disabled={responding}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 py-2.5 rounded border border-crimson/50 bg-crimson/5 text-crimson text-sm font-hud tracking-wider hover:bg-crimson/15 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <XCircle size={16} />
              <span>DENY</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => respond(true)}
              disabled={responding}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 py-2.5 rounded border border-cyan-arc/50 bg-cyan-arc/5 text-cyan-arc text-sm font-hud tracking-wider hover:bg-cyan-arc/15 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
              <span>APPROVE</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ApprovalModal;
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/CommandStream.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Send, Mic, MicOff, Volume2, VolumeX, ShieldAlert, Sparkles, Terminal } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ChatMessageItem {
  id: string;
  sender: 'user' | 'jarvis';
  source: 'voice' | 'text' | 'macro';
  text: string;
  timestamp: string;
  action?: string;
  data?: Record<string, any>;
}

interface CommandStreamProps {
  messages: ChatMessageItem[];
  micActive: boolean;
  ttsActive: boolean;
  meetingAutoMuted: boolean;
  onSendCommand: (query: string) => void;
  onToggleMic: () => void;
  onToggleTts: () => void;
}

export const CommandStream: React.FC<CommandStreamProps> = ({
  messages,
  micActive,
  ttsActive,
  meetingAutoMuted,
  onSendCommand,
  onToggleMic,
  onToggleTts,
}) => {
  const [inputText, setInputText] = useState('');
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [historyIndex, setHistoryIndex] = useState<number>(-1);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Auto-scroll on new messages
  useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  const handleSubmit = (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    if (!inputText.trim()) return;

    const cmd = inputText.trim();
    setHistory((prev) => [...prev, cmd]);
    setHistoryIndex(-1);
    onSendCommand(cmd);
    setInputText('');
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (history.length === 0) return;
      const nextIdx = historyIndex === -1 ? history.length - 1 : Math.max(0, historyIndex - 1);
      setHistoryIndex(nextIdx);
      setInputText(history[nextIdx]);
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (historyIndex === -1) return;
      const nextIdx = historyIndex + 1;
      if (nextIdx >= history.length) {
