        setHistoryIndex(-1);
        setInputText('');
      } else {
        setHistoryIndex(nextIdx);
        setInputText(history[nextIdx]);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/40 relative overflow-hidden select-text">
      {/* Messages Feed */}
      <div
        ref={scrollRef}
        className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 font-mono text-xs scroll-smooth"
      >
        {messages.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-steel/60 space-y-3 select-none">
            <Sparkles size={36} className="text-cyan-arc/40 animate-pulse" />
            <div className="text-sm font-hud text-ice/70 tracking-widest">SYSTEM READY FOR INPUT</div>
            <div className="text-[11px] max-w-sm text-center text-steel/80 leading-relaxed">
              Speak via voice commands or type queries below. Say <span className="text-cyan-arc">"What time is it"</span>, <span className="text-cyan-arc">"Check system status"</span>, or describe desktop tasks.
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          messages.map((msg) => {
            const isUser = msg.sender === 'user';
            return (
              <div
                key={msg.id}
                className={`flex flex-col ${isUser ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'}`}
              >
                {/* Meta header */}
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-[9px] text-steel mb-1">
                  <span className={isUser ? 'text-cyan-arc font-bold' : 'text-gold-reactor font-bold'}>
                    {isUser ? 'OPERATOR' : 'J.A.R.V.I.S.'}
                  </span>
                  <span>•</span>
                  <span>{new Date(msg.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                  <span>•</span>
                  <span className="uppercase text-[8px] bg-cyber/40 px-1 rounded">{msg.source}</span>
                  {msg.action && msg.action !== 'none' && (
                    <span className="text-cyan-arc/80 font-bold">[{msg.action}]</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Card Bubble */}
                <div
                  className={`max-w-[82%] px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg border text-xs leading-relaxed transition-all ${
                    isUser
                      ? 'bg-tactical border-cyan-arc/30 text-ice shadow-arc-glow'
                      : 'bg-[#0b1526]/90 border-cyber text-ice/95'
                  }`}
                >
                  <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{msg.text}</p>

                  {/* Render Structured Card Data if present */}
                  {msg.data && Object.keys(msg.data).length > 0 && msg.data.summary && (
                    <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-cyber/50 text-[10px] text-steel font-mono">
                      <span className="text-cyan-arc font-bold">TELEMETRY: </span>
                      {msg.data.summary}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Persistent Bottom Hybrid Command Dock */}
      <div className="p-3 bg-tactical border-t border-cyber/70 hud-panel relative z-30">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          {/* Mic Toggle Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleMic}
            className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded font-mono text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider transition-all border ${
              micActive
                ? 'bg-cyan-arc/15 text-cyan-arc border-cyan-arc shadow-arc-glow'
                : meetingAutoMuted
                ? 'bg-crimson/20 text-crimson border-crimson/60 animate-pulse'
                : 'bg-cyber/40 text-steel border-cyber hover:border-steel'
            }`}
            title={
              meetingAutoMuted
                ? 'Microphone auto-muted by active meeting (Click to manually unmute)'
                : micActive
                ? 'Microphone active (Click to mute)'
                : 'Microphone muted (Click to listen)'
            }
          >
            {micActive ? <Mic size={14} className="animate-pulse" /> : <MicOff size={14} />}
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">
              {meetingAutoMuted ? 'AUTO-MUTED' : micActive ? 'MIC ACTIVE' : 'MIC MUTED'}
            </span>
          </button>

          {/* JARVIS Voice (TTS) Toggle Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleTts}
            className={`flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded font-mono text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider transition-all border ${
              ttsActive
                ? 'bg-cyan-arc/15 text-cyan-arc border-cyan-arc/70 shadow-arc-glow'
                : 'bg-gold-reactor/15 text-gold-reactor border-gold-reactor/60'
            }`}
            title={ttsActive ? 'Spoken Voice Output ON' : 'Silent Text-Only Mode ON'}
          >
            {ttsActive ? <Volume2 size={14} /> : <VolumeX size={14} />}
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{ttsActive ? 'VOICE ON' : 'SILENT'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Persistent Text Input Bar */}
          <div className="flex-1 relative flex items-center">
            <Terminal size={14} className="absolute left-3 text-steel pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={inputText}
              onChange={(e) => setInputText(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
              placeholder="Type an OS instruction, question, or query... (Enter to send, ↑/↓ for history)"
              className="w-full bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice text-xs font-mono rounded pl-8 pr-10 py-2 focus:outline-none transition-colors"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!inputText.trim()}
              className="absolute right-1.5 p-1 text-cyan-arc disabled:text-steel/40 hover:bg-cyan-arc/10 rounded transition-colors"
            >
              <Send size={15} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/MeetingHub.tsx">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Video,
  PhoneOff,
  Plus,
  FileText,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  MessageSquareQuote,
  Globe,
  Upload,
  Copy,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Play,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface MeetingItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  platform?: string;
  meeting_url: string;
  start_time: string;
  end_time?: string;
  auto_leave?: boolean;
  status?: string;
}

export interface SummaryItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  date: string;
  captions_count: number;
  summary: string;
}

export interface CaptionItem {
  text: string;
  timestamp: string;
  platform: string;
}

interface MeetingHubProps {
  meetings: MeetingItem[];
  summaries: SummaryItem[];
  liveCaptions: CaptionItem[];
  currentMeeting: Record<string, any> | null;
  onAddMeeting: (meeting: Partial<MeetingItem>) => void;
  onDeleteMeeting: (id: string) => void;
  onJoinMeeting: (url: string, title?: string, id?: string) => void;
  onLeaveMeeting: () => void;
  apiBase?: string;
}

export const MeetingHub: React.FC<MeetingHubProps> = ({
  meetings,
  summaries,
  liveCaptions,
  currentMeeting,
  onAddMeeting,
  onDeleteMeeting,
  onJoinMeeting,
  onLeaveMeeting,
  apiBase,
}) => {
  const getDefaultTime = () => {
    const d = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000);
    const pad = (n: number) => n.toString().padStart(2, '0');
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  };

  const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState('');
  const [newUrl, setNewUrl] = useState('');
  const [newTime, setNewTime] = useState(getDefaultTime());
  const [durationMinutes, setDurationMinutes] = useState(60);
  const [instantUrl, setInstantUrl] = useState('');
  const [expandedSummary, setExpandedSummary] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Setup button state
  const [setupLoading, setSetupLoading] = useState(false);
  const [setupMessage, setSetupMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Bulk import/export state
  const [bulkOpen, setBulkOpen] = useState(false);
  const [bulkJson, setBulkJson] = useState('');
  const [bulkStatus, setBulkStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const baseUrl = apiBase || window.location.origin;

  const handleCreate = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newTitle || !newUrl) return;

    const startDate = new Date(newTime || Date.now());
    const endDate = new Date(startDate.getTime() + (durationMinutes || 60) * 60 * 1000);

    onAddMeeting({
      title: newTitle,
      meeting_url: newUrl,
      start_time: startDate.toISOString(),
      end_time: endDate.toISOString(),
      platform: newUrl.includes('google.com') ? 'google_meet' : 'teams',
      auto_leave: true,
      status: 'pending',
    });

    setNewTitle('');
    setNewUrl('');
    setNewTime(getDefaultTime());
    setDurationMinutes(60);
  };

  const handleInstantJoin = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!instantUrl.trim()) return;
    onJoinMeeting(instantUrl.trim(), 'Ad-hoc Call');
    setInstantUrl('');
  };

  const handleAccountSetup = async () => {
    setSetupLoading(true);
    setSetupMessage(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/meetings/setup`, { method: 'POST' });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        setSetupMessage('Chrome profile launched! Sign in to Google & Teams, then close the window.');
      } else {
        setSetupMessage('Failed to launch browser setup.');
      }
    } catch {
      setSetupMessage('Error contacting server for account setup.');
    } finally {
      setSetupLoading(false);
      setTimeout(() => setSetupMessage(null), 7000);
    }
  };

  const handleBulkImport = async () => {
    if (!bulkJson.trim()) return;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(bulkJson);
      const res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/meetings/bulk`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(parsed),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        setBulkStatus(`Successfully imported ${data.count} meeting(s)!`);
        setBulkJson('');
        // Refresh by triggering a state fetch or refetch via parent
        const refreshRes = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/meetings`);
        const refreshData = await refreshRes.json();
        if (refreshData.meetings) {
          // Parent will update on polling or tab switch
        }
      } else {
        setBulkStatus('Bulk import rejected by server.');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setBulkStatus(`JSON parse error: ${err.message || 'Invalid format'}`);
    }
    setTimeout(() => setBulkStatus(null), 5000);
  };

  const handleExportJson = () => {
    const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(meetings, null, 2);
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(jsonStr);
    setBulkStatus('Schedule copied to clipboard!');
    setTimeout(() => setBulkStatus(null), 4000);
  };

  const renderStatusBadge = (status?: string) => {
    const s = (status || 'pending').toLowerCase();
    if (s === 'active') {
      return (
        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc animate-pulse uppercase tracking-wider">
          ACTIVE
        </span>
      );
    }
    if (s === 'completed') {
      return (
        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-steel/20 text-steel border border-steel/30 uppercase tracking-wider">
          COMPLETED
        </span>
      );
    }
    if (s === 'cancelled') {
      return (
        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-crimson/20 text-crimson border border-crimson/30 uppercase tracking-wider">
          CANCELLED
        </span>
      );
    }
    return (
      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/30 uppercase tracking-wider">
        PENDING
      </span>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/30 p-4 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto font-mono text-xs">
      {/* Top Header Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-cyber/50 pb-3">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <Calendar className="text-cyan-arc" size={18} />
          <div>
            <div className="font-hud text-sm font-bold text-ice tracking-wider">MEETING ASSISTANT HUB</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-steel">Playwright Autonomous Telemetry & Closed Caption Engine</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleAccountSetup}
            disabled={setupLoading}
            title="Open Chrome profile to save Google Meet & MS Teams login sessions"
            className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-tactical hover:bg-tactical/80 text-ice border border-cyber hover:border-cyan-arc/60 rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-all text-[11px]"
          >
            <Globe size={13} className="text-cyan-arc" />
            <span>{setupLoading ? 'OPENING...' : 'BROWSER ACCOUNT SETUP'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {setupMessage && (
        <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc text-ice text-[11px] flex items-center space-x-2">
          <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-cyan-arc flex-shrink-0" />
          <span>{setupMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Live Active Meeting Banner */}
      {currentMeeting ? (
        <div className="bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc p-3.5 rounded flex items-center justify-between hud-panel shadow-arc-glow">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-cyan-arc animate-ping" />
            <div>
              <div className="font-hud text-sm font-bold text-ice tracking-wider">
                ACTIVE MEETING: {currentMeeting.title?.toUpperCase() || 'ONLINE CALL'}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-steel">
                Connected via Playwright Attendant • Microphone Auto-Muted
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onLeaveMeeting}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-crimson/20 hover:bg-crimson/30 text-crimson border border-crimson rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-all"
          >
            <PhoneOff size={14} />
            <span>LEAVE CALL</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {/* Ad-Hoc Direct Instant Join Bar */}
      <div className="bg-tactical/80 border border-cyber p-2.5 rounded hud-panel">
        <form onSubmit={handleInstantJoin} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-cyan-arc flex-shrink-0 pl-1">
            <Play size={13} fill="currentColor" />
            <span className="font-bold text-[11px] text-ice">INSTANT CALL:</span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Paste Google Meet or Teams link for immediate direct join..."
            value={instantUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setInstantUrl(e.target.value)}
            className="flex-1 bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice px-3 py-1.5 rounded focus:outline-none text-[11px]"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-cyan-arc/20 hover:bg-cyan-arc/30 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-colors flex-shrink-0 text-[11px]"
          >
            INSTANT JOIN
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid: Schedule & Summaries */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {/* Left Column: Schedule & Quick-Add */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Quick-Add Form */}
          <div className="bg-tactical border border-cyber p-3.5 rounded hud-panel space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-ice font-hud font-bold text-xs tracking-wider">
              <Plus size={14} className="text-cyan-arc" />
              <span>SCHEDULE NEW MEETING</span>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleCreate} className="space-y-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Meeting Title (e.g. Sprint Planning)"
                value={newTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setNewTitle(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice p-2 rounded focus:outline-none"
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Meeting URL (Google Meet or Teams link)"
                value={newUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setNewUrl(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice p-2 rounded focus:outline-none"
              />
              <div className="flex flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-2">
                <input
                  type="datetime-local"
                  value={newTime}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTime(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1 bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice p-2 rounded focus:outline-none text-[11px]"
                />
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 bg-void/80 border border-cyber px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded flex-shrink-0">
                  <Clock size={12} className="text-steel" />
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={5}
                    max={480}
                    step={5}
                    value={durationMinutes}
                    onChange={(e) => setDurationMinutes(Number(e.target.value) || 60)}
                    className="w-10 bg-transparent text-ice text-center focus:outline-none text-xs"
                    title="Duration in minutes"
                  />
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">min</span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-cyan-arc/20 hover:bg-cyan-arc/30 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-colors flex-shrink-0"
                >
                  SCHEDULE
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>

          {/* Scheduled Meetings List */}
          <div className="bg-tactical border border-cyber p-3.5 rounded hud-panel space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-steel text-xs border-b border-cyber/50 pb-2">
              <span className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 font-hud font-bold text-ice">
                <Calendar size={13} className="text-cyan-arc" />
                <span>UPCOMING SCHEDULES</span>
              </span>
              <span>{meetings.length} REGISTERED</span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {meetings.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-6 text-steel/60">No scheduled meetings.</div>
              ) : (
                meetings.map((m) => (
                  <div
                    key={m.id}
                    className="p-2.5 bg-void/70 border border-cyber rounded flex items-center justify-between hover:border-cyan-arc/50 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <span className="text-ice font-bold">{m.title}</span>
                        {renderStatusBadge(m.status)}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-steel flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <Clock size={10} />
                        <span>{new Date(m.start_time).toLocaleString()}</span>
                        <span>•</span>
                        <span className="uppercase text-cyan-arc">{m.platform || 'MEETING'}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onJoinMeeting(m.meeting_url, m.title, m.id)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-cyan-arc/20 hover:bg-cyan-arc/30 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc/50 rounded font-bold transition-colors"
                      >
                        JOIN
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onDeleteMeeting(m.id)}
                        className="px-2 py-1 text-steel hover:text-crimson transition-colors"
                        title="Delete meeting"
                      >
                        ×
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Live Captions & AI Post-Call Summaries */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Live Captions Feed */}
          <div className="bg-tactical border border-cyber p-3.5 rounded hud-panel space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-steel text-xs border-b border-cyber/50 pb-2">
              <span className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 font-hud font-bold text-ice">
                <MessageSquareQuote size={13} className="text-cyan-arc" />
                <span>REAL-TIME DOM CAPTIONS</span>
              </span>
              <span>{liveCaptions.length} CAPTURED</span>
            </div>

            <div className="h-44 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 bg-void/80 p-2.5 rounded border border-cyber/40 text-[11px]">
              {liveCaptions.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-steel/40 text-center">
                  Captions stream live during Google Meet / Teams calls.
                </div>
              ) : (
                liveCaptions.map((c, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="text-ice/90 leading-relaxed">
                    <span className="text-steel text-[9px] mr-1.5">[{c.timestamp}]</span>
                    <span>{c.text}</span>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* AI Post-Call Summaries */}
          <div className="bg-tactical border border-cyber p-3.5 rounded hud-panel space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-steel text-xs border-b border-cyber/50 pb-2">
              <span className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 font-hud font-bold text-ice">
                <FileText size={13} className="text-gold-reactor" />
                <span>AI POST-CALL SUMMARIES & ACTION ITEMS</span>
              </span>
              <span>{summaries.length} SAVED</span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {summaries.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-6 text-steel/60">No post-call summaries yet.</div>
              ) : (
                summaries.map((s) => {
                  const isExp = expandedSummary === s.id;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={s.id}
                      className="p-2.5 bg-void/70 border border-cyber rounded space-y-1.5 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <div
                        onClick={() => setExpandedSummary(isExp ? null : s.id)}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:text-cyan-arc transition-colors"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                          {isExp ? <ChevronDown size={14} /> : <ChevronRight size={14} />}
                          <span className="font-bold text-ice">{s.title}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">{s.date}</span>
                      </div>

                      {isExp && (
                        <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-cyber/50 text-[11px] text-ice/90 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">
                          {s.summary}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Collapsible Bulk JSON Import / Export Section */}
      <div className="bg-tactical border border-cyber rounded hud-panel">
        <button
          onClick={() => setBulkOpen(!bulkOpen)}
          className="w-full p-3 flex items-center justify-between text-ice hover:text-cyan-arc transition-colors font-bold tracking-wider"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Upload size={14} className="text-cyan-arc" />
            <span>BULK JSON IMPORT / EXPORT TIMETABLE</span>
          </div>
          {bulkOpen ? <ChevronDown size={14} /> : <ChevronRight size={14} />}
        </button>

        {bulkOpen && (
          <div className="p-3.5 border-t border-cyber/50 space-y-3">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-steel">
              Paste a JSON array of meetings to bulk-schedule, or export your current schedule JSON:
            </div>
            <textarea
              value={bulkJson}
              onChange={(e) => setBulkJson(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={`[\n  {\n    "title": "Team Standup",\n    "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij",\n    "start_time": "2026-10-22T14:00:00",\n    "end_time": "2026-10-22T15:00:00"\n  }\n]`}
              className="w-full h-28 bg-void/80 border border-cyber focus:border-cyan-arc text-ice p-2.5 rounded focus:outline-none font-mono text-[11px]"
            />
            {bulkStatus && (
              <div className="text-[11px] text-cyan-arc flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <CheckCircle2 size={13} />
                <span>{bulkStatus}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={handleBulkImport}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-cyan-arc/20 hover:bg-cyan-arc/30 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-colors"
              >
                <Upload size={13} />
                <span>IMPORT JSON</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleExportJson}
                className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-tactical hover:bg-tactical/80 text-ice border border-cyber rounded font-bold tracking-wider transition-colors"
              >
                <Copy size={13} />
                <span>EXPORT CURRENT SCHEDULE (COPY JSON)</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/SkillsAndMcp.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Zap, Plus, Trash2, Play, BookOpen,
  Server, Settings, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Wrench,
} from 'lucide-react';

/* ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
interface Skill {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trigger_phrases?: string[];
  trigger?: string;
  steps: (string | { command: string })[];
}

interface McpServer {
  name: string;
  command?: string;
  url?: string;
  transport?: string;
  tools?: string[];
}

interface McpTool {
  server: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
}

interface SkillsAndMcpProps {
  apiBase: string;
}

/* ── Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export const SkillsAndMcp: React.FC<SkillsAndMcpProps> = ({ apiBase }) => {
  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<Skill[]>([]);
  const [mcpServers, setMcpServers] = useState<McpServer[]>([]);
  const [mcpTools, setMcpTools] = useState<McpTool[]>([]);
  const [runResult, setRunResult] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  // Teach Mode state
  const [isTeaching, setIsTeaching] = useState(false);
  const [teachName, setTeachName] = useState('');
  const [teachTrigger, setTeachTrigger] = useState('');

  // New skill form
  const [showNewSkill, setShowNewSkill] = useState(false);
  const [newSkillName, setNewSkillName] = useState('');
  const [newSkillTrigger, setNewSkillTrigger] = useState('');
  const [newSkillSteps, setNewSkillSteps] = useState('');

  // New MCP server form
  const [showNewMcp, setShowNewMcp] = useState(false);
  const [newMcpName, setNewMcpName] = useState('');
  const [newMcpCommand, setNewMcpCommand] = useState('');
  const [newMcpUrl, setNewMcpUrl] = useState('');

  // MCP expanded
  const [mcpExpanded, setMcpExpanded] = useState(false);

  const fetchSkills = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setSkills(Array.isArray(data) ? data : data.skills || []);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [apiBase]);

  const fetchMcp = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/mcp`);
      const data = await res.json();
      const rawServers = data.servers || {};
      const serversList = Array.isArray(rawServers)
        ? rawServers
        : Object.entries(rawServers).map(([name, conf]: [string, any]) => ({ name, ...conf }));
      setMcpServers(serversList);
      setMcpTools(data.tools || []);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [apiBase]);

  const fetchTeachStatus = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills/teach/status`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setIsTeaching(Boolean(data.is_teaching));
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [apiBase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSkills();
    fetchMcp();
    fetchTeachStatus();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchTeachStatus, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [fetchSkills, fetchMcp, fetchTeachStatus]);

  /* ── Teach Mode Actions ─────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const startTeachMode = async () => {
    if (!teachName.trim()) return;
    const triggers = teachTrigger
      ? teachTrigger.split(',').map((t) => t.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)
      : [teachName.trim().toLowerCase()];
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills/teach/start`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: teachName.trim(), trigger_phrases: triggers }),
    });
    setIsTeaching(true);
  };

  const stopTeachMode = async (save: boolean = true) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills/teach/stop`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ save }),
    });
    setIsTeaching(false);
    setTeachName('');
    setTeachTrigger('');
    fetchSkills();
  };

  /* ── Skills CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const addSkill = async () => {
    const steps: string[] = newSkillSteps
      .split('\n')
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (!newSkillName.trim() || steps.length === 0) return;
    const triggers = newSkillTrigger
      ? newSkillTrigger.split(',').map((t) => t.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)
      : [newSkillName.trim().toLowerCase()];
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        name: newSkillName.trim(),
        trigger_phrases: triggers,
        steps,
      }),
    });
    setNewSkillName(''); setNewSkillTrigger(''); setNewSkillSteps('');
    setShowNewSkill(false);
    fetchSkills();
  };

  const runSkill = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills/${id}/run`, { method: 'POST' });
      const data = await res.json();
      setRunResult((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: data.ok !== false ? 'Executed ✓' : 'Error' }));
      setTimeout(() => setRunResult((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: '' })), 3000);
    } catch {
      setRunResult((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: 'Network error' }));
    }
  };

  const deleteSkill = async (id: string) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/skills/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    fetchSkills();
  };

  /* ── MCP CRUD ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const addMcpServer = async () => {
    if (!newMcpName.trim()) return;
    const config: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (newMcpCommand.trim()) config.command = newMcpCommand;
    if (newMcpUrl.trim()) config.url = newMcpUrl;
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/mcp`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: newMcpName, config }),
    });
    setNewMcpName(''); setNewMcpCommand(''); setNewMcpUrl('');
    setShowNewMcp(false);
    fetchMcp();
  };

  const deleteMcpServer = async (name: string) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/mcp/${name}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    fetchMcp();
  };

  const inputClasses = 'bg-void/60 border border-cyber text-ice text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded focus:border-cyan-arc focus:outline-none w-full font-mono';

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/30 p-4 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto">
      {/* ── Teach Mode Recorder Card / Banner ──────────────────────── */}
      {isTeaching ? (
        <div className="bg-gold-reactor/10 border-2 border-gold-reactor rounded p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-pulse-glow shadow-gold-glow">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-gold-reactor animate-ping" />
            <div>
              <div className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-gold-reactor">
                TEACH MODE ACTIVE — Any command you say or type is being recorded
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-steel font-mono mt-0.5">
                Recording routine: <span className="text-ice font-bold">{teachName || 'Custom Routine'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => stopTeachMode(false)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-crimson/50 text-crimson text-xs hover:bg-crimson/10 transition-colors"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => stopTeachMode(true)}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded bg-gold-reactor text-void font-bold text-xs hover:bg-yellow-400 transition-colors"
            >
              Stop & Save Routine
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-2 border-cyber/50">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Zap size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" />
            <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">TEACH MODE RECORDER</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">(Record multi-step routines live by talking or typing)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <input
              className={inputClasses}
              placeholder="New Routine Name (e.g. Morning Briefing)"
              value={teachName}
              onChange={(e) => setTeachName(e.target.value)}
            />
            <input
              className={inputClasses}
              placeholder="Voice trigger phrase (e.g. start morning briefing)"
              value={teachTrigger}
              onChange={(e) => setTeachTrigger(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end pt-1">
            <button
              onClick={startTeachMode}
              disabled={!teachName.trim()}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-gold-reactor/15 border border-gold-reactor/40 text-xs font-hud font-bold tracking-wider text-gold-reactor hover:bg-gold-reactor/25 transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              Start Recording (Teach Mode)
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Skills Section ────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Zap size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" />
            <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">VOICE MACRO SKILLS</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">({skills.length})</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowNewSkill(!showNewSkill)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded border border-cyber text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus size={12} /> <span>New Skill</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* New skill form */}
        {showNewSkill && (
          <div className="bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-3 space-y-2">
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Skill Name (e.g. Morning Routine)" value={newSkillName} onChange={(e) => setNewSkillName(e.target.value)} />
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Voice trigger phrase (optional, comma-separated)" value={newSkillTrigger} onChange={(e) => setNewSkillTrigger(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea
              className={`${inputClasses} h-20 resize-none`}
              placeholder="Commands (one per line):&#10;open spotify&#10;set volume to 30&#10;what is the weather"
              value={newSkillSteps}
              onChange={(e) => setNewSkillSteps(e.target.value)}
            />
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <button onClick={addSkill} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc/30 text-xs text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors">
                Save Skill
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => setShowNewSkill(false)} className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-steel hover:text-ice transition-colors">
                Cancel
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Skill cards */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {skills.map((skill) => {
            const triggerText = (skill.trigger_phrases && skill.trigger_phrases.length > 0)
              ? skill.trigger_phrases.join(', ')
              : (skill.trigger || '');
            const stepsList = Array.isArray(skill.steps) ? skill.steps : [];

            return (
              <div key={skill.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2 group">
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <BookOpen size={12} className="text-cyan-arc flex-shrink-0" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-ice font-bold truncate">{skill.name}</span>
                    {triggerText && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-steel bg-void/60 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded truncate max-w-xs">"{triggerText}"</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-steel mt-0.5 pl-5">
                    {stepsList.length} step{stepsList.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
                    {stepsList.slice(0, 2).map((s, i) => {
                      const cmdText = typeof s === 'string' ? s : (s && (s as any).command ? (s as any).command : String(s));
                      return <span key={i} className="ml-2 font-mono">→ {cmdText}</span>;
                    })}
                    {stepsList.length > 2 && <span className="ml-1">…</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                  {runResult[skill.id] && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-green-400 mr-1">{runResult[skill.id]}</span>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => runSkill(skill.id)} className="p-1 text-cyan-arc hover:text-ice" title="Run routine"><Play size={14} /></button>
                  <button onClick={() => deleteSkill(skill.id)} className="p-1 text-crimson hover:text-red-300" title="Delete routine"><Trash2 size={14} /></button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {skills.length === 0 && (
            <div className="text-[11px] text-steel text-center py-4">No skills configured. Create one above or use Teach Mode via voice or button above.</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── MCP Section ───────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <button onClick={() => setMcpExpanded(!mcpExpanded)} className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            {mcpExpanded ? <ChevronDown size={14} className="text-cyan-arc" /> : <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-steel" />}
            <Server size={16} className="text-cyan-arc" />
            <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">MCP SERVER MANAGER</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">({mcpServers.length} server{mcpServers.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}, {mcpTools.length} tool{mcpTools.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''})</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowNewMcp(!showNewMcp)}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded border border-cyber text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc hover:border-cyan-arc transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus size={12} /> <span>Add Server</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {showNewMcp && (
          <div className="bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-3 space-y-2">
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Server Name" value={newMcpName} onChange={(e) => setNewMcpName(e.target.value)} />
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Command (e.g. npx -y @mcp/server)" value={newMcpCommand} onChange={(e) => setNewMcpCommand(e.target.value)} />
            <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="URL (for HTTP transport)" value={newMcpUrl} onChange={(e) => setNewMcpUrl(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <button onClick={addMcpServer} className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc/30 text-xs text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors">
                Add Server
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => setShowNewMcp(false)} className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-steel hover:text-ice transition-colors">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {mcpExpanded && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {mcpServers.map((srv) => (
              <div key={srv.name} className="bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2 space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Settings size={12} className="text-gold-reactor" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-ice font-bold">{srv.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-steel font-mono">{srv.transport || 'stdio'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => deleteMcpServer(srv.name)} className="text-crimson hover:text-red-300"><Trash2 size={12} /></button>
                </div>
                {srv.command && <div className="text-[10px] text-steel font-mono pl-5">{srv.command}</div>}
                {srv.url && <div className="text-[10px] text-steel font-mono pl-5">{srv.url}</div>}
                {srv.tools && srv.tools.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-steel pl-5">
                    Tools: {srv.tools.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}{srv.tools.length > 5 ? ` +${srv.tools.length - 5} more` : ''}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
            {mcpServers.length === 0 && (
              <div className="text-[11px] text-steel text-center py-3">No MCP servers configured.</div>
            )}

            {mcpTools.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-2">
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 mb-1">
                  <Wrench size={12} className="text-gold-reactor" />
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-hud tracking-wider text-steel">DISCOVERED TOOLS</span>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1">
                  {mcpTools.slice(0, 12).map((tool, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="text-[10px] text-steel bg-void/40 px-2 py-1 rounded font-mono truncate">
                      <span className="text-cyan-arc">{tool.server}</span>:{tool.name}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                {mcpTools.length > 12 && <div className="text-[10px] text-steel text-center mt-1">+{mcpTools.length - 12} more tools</div>}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SkillsAndMcp;
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/SystemAndAlarms.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  Volume2, Sun, AppWindow, Plus, Trash2, Clock, Bell, FolderSearch,
  Save, Chrome, Code, Music, FileText, Calculator, Terminal, FolderOpen,
  Timer, Clipboard, Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';

/* ── Types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
interface AlarmItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  time?: string;
  duration_seconds?: number;
  type?: string;
}

interface AlarmsData {
  alarms: AlarmItem[];
  timers: AlarmItem[];
}

interface SystemAndAlarmsProps {
  apiBase: string;
}

/* ── Quick-Launch App Grid ────────────────────────────────────────── */
const QUICK_APPS = [
  { name: 'Chrome', cmd: 'chrome', icon: <Chrome size={20} /> },
  { name: 'VS Code', cmd: 'code', icon: <Code size={20} /> },
  { name: 'Spotify', cmd: 'spotify', icon: <Music size={20} /> },
  { name: 'Notepad', cmd: 'notepad', icon: <FileText size={20} /> },
  { name: 'Calculator', cmd: 'calculator', icon: <Calculator size={20} /> },
  { name: 'Terminal', cmd: 'cmd', icon: <Terminal size={20} /> },
  { name: 'Explorer', cmd: 'explorer', icon: <FolderOpen size={20} /> },
];

/* ── Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export const SystemAndAlarms: React.FC<SystemAndAlarmsProps> = ({ apiBase }) => {
  const [volume, setVolume] = useState(50);
  const [brightness, setBrightness] = useState(70);
  const [alarms, setAlarms] = useState<AlarmItem[]>([]);
  const [timers, setTimers] = useState<AlarmItem[]>([]);

  // New alarm form
  const [newAlarmLabel, setNewAlarmLabel] = useState('');
  const [newAlarmTime, setNewAlarmTime] = useState('');

  // New countdown timer form
  const [newTimerLabel, setNewTimerLabel] = useState('');
  const [newTimerMinutes, setNewTimerMinutes] = useState('');

  // Workspace RAG path
  const [workspacePath, setWorkspacePath] = useState('');
  const [pathSaved, setPathSaved] = useState(false);

  // Clipboard Copilot
  const [clipboardResult, setClipboardResult] = useState('');
  const [clipboardLoading, setClipboardLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchAlarms = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/alarms`);
      const data: AlarmsData = await res.json();
      setAlarms(data.alarms || []);
      setTimers(data.timers || []);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [apiBase]);

  useEffect(() => { fetchAlarms(); }, [fetchAlarms]);

  // FIX 7: Load saved workspace path from vault on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault`);
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.workspace_path) {
          setWorkspacePath(data.workspace_path);
        }
      } catch { /* offline */ }
    })();
  }, [apiBase]);

  /* ── System Controls ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const setSystemVolume = async (v: number) => {
    setVolume(v);
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/system/control`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'volume', value: v }),
    });
  };

  const setSystemBrightness = async (b: number) => {
    setBrightness(b);
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/system/control`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'brightness', value: b }),
    });
  };

  const launchApp = async (cmd: string) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/system/control`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'app', value: cmd }),
    });
  };

  /* ── Alarms ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const addAlarm = async () => {
    if (!newAlarmLabel.trim()) return;
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/alarms`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        label: newAlarmLabel,
        time: newAlarmTime || undefined,
        type: 'alarm',
      }),
    });
    setNewAlarmLabel(''); setNewAlarmTime('');
    fetchAlarms();
  };

  /* ── Countdown Timer ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const addTimer = async () => {
    const mins = parseInt(newTimerMinutes, 10);
    if (!newTimerLabel.trim() || isNaN(mins) || mins <= 0) return;
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/alarms`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        label: newTimerLabel,
        duration_seconds: mins * 60,
        type: 'timer',
      }),
    });
    // Also fire a voice command so the backend timer worker actually counts down
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/command`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ query: `set timer for ${mins} minutes`, source: 'text' }),
    });
    setNewTimerLabel(''); setNewTimerMinutes('');
    fetchAlarms();
  };

  const deleteAlarm = async (id: string) => {
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/alarms/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    fetchAlarms();
  };

  /* ── Clipboard Copilot ──────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const clipboardAction = async (commandPrefix: string) => {
    setClipboardLoading(true);
    setClipboardResult('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/command`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query: commandPrefix, source: 'text' }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      setClipboardResult(data.text || data.response || 'Done.');
    } catch {
      setClipboardResult('Failed to reach JARVIS backend.');
    } finally {
      setClipboardLoading(false);
    }
  };

  /* ── Workspace RAG ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const saveWorkspacePath = async () => {
    if (!workspacePath.trim()) return;
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/system/control`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'workspace_path', value: workspacePath }),
    });
    // Also persist in vault
    await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/vault/roles`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ workspace_path: workspacePath }),
    });
    setPathSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setPathSaved(false), 3000);
  };

  const inputClasses = 'bg-void/60 border border-cyber text-ice text-xs px-3 py-2 rounded focus:border-cyan-arc focus:outline-none w-full font-mono';

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/30 p-4 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto">
      {/* ── Volume & Brightness ───────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-1">
          <Volume2 size={16} className="text-cyan-arc" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">SYSTEM CONTROLS</span>
        </div>

        {/* Volume */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-steel flex items-center space-x-1"><Volume2 size={12} /> <span>Volume</span></span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-cyan-arc font-mono">{volume}%</span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="range" min="0" max="100" value={volume}
            onChange={(e) => setSystemVolume(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="w-full h-1.5 rounded-full appearance-none cursor-pointer"
            style={{
              background: `linear-gradient(to right, #00E5FF ${volume}%, #16324A ${volume}%)`,
            }}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Brightness */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-steel flex items-center space-x-1"><Sun size={12} /> <span>Brightness</span></span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-gold-reactor font-mono">{brightness}%</span>
          </div>
          <input
            type="range" min="0" max="100" value={brightness}
            onChange={(e) => setSystemBrightness(Number(e.target.value))}
            className="w-full h-1.5 rounded-full appearance-none cursor-pointer"
            style={{
              background: `linear-gradient(to right, #FFB300 ${brightness}%, #16324A ${brightness}%)`,
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── App Launcher Grid ─────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 mb-1">
          <AppWindow size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">QUICK LAUNCH</span>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          {QUICK_APPS.map(({ name, cmd, icon }) => (
            <button
              key={cmd}
              onClick={() => launchApp(cmd)}
              className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-1 p-2.5 rounded border border-cyber/50 bg-void/40 hover:border-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/5 transition-all group"
            >
              <span className="text-steel group-hover:text-cyan-arc transition-colors">{icon}</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-steel group-hover:text-ice transition-colors">{name}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Alarms & Timers ───────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Bell size={16} className="text-gold-reactor" />
            <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">ALARMS & TIMERS</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-steel">({alarms.length + timers.length})</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Add alarm */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Alarm label" value={newAlarmLabel} onChange={(e) => setNewAlarmLabel(e.target.value)} />
          <input
            type="time"
            className={`${inputClasses} w-32`}
            value={newAlarmTime}
            onChange={(e) => setNewAlarmTime(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button onClick={addAlarm} className="p-2 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc/30 text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors">
            <Plus size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Add countdown timer */}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <input className={inputClasses} placeholder="Timer label" value={newTimerLabel} onChange={(e) => setNewTimerLabel(e.target.value)} />
          <input
            type="number"
            min="1"
            placeholder="Min"
            className={`${inputClasses} w-20`}
            value={newTimerMinutes}
            onChange={(e) => setNewTimerMinutes(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button onClick={addTimer} className="flex items-center space-x-1 whitespace-nowrap px-2.5 py-2 rounded bg-gold-reactor/10 border border-gold-reactor/30 text-gold-reactor text-[11px] hover:bg-gold-reactor/20 transition-colors">
            <Timer size={12} />
            <span>Timer</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Alarm + timer list */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {[...alarms, ...timers].map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-void/40 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2 group">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <Clock size={12} className="text-gold-reactor" />
                <span className="text-xs text-ice">{item.label}</span>
                {item.time && <span className="text-[10px] text-steel font-mono">{item.time}</span>}
                {item.duration_seconds && <span className="text-[10px] text-gold-reactor font-mono">{Math.round(item.duration_seconds / 60)}m</span>}
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => deleteAlarm(item.id)}
                className="text-crimson opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
              >
                <Trash2 size={12} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {alarms.length + timers.length === 0 && (
            <div className="text-[11px] text-steel text-center py-3">No alarms or timers set.</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Clipboard Copilot Quick-Actions ────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <Clipboard size={16} className="text-cyan-arc" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">CLIPBOARD COPILOT</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => clipboardAction('summarize clipboard')}
            disabled={clipboardLoading}
            className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded border border-cyan-arc/30 bg-cyan-arc/10 text-xs text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Summarize Clipboard
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => clipboardAction('fix this code')}
            disabled={clipboardLoading}
            className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded border border-gold-reactor/30 bg-gold-reactor/10 text-xs text-gold-reactor hover:bg-gold-reactor/20 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Fix Code in Clipboard
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => clipboardAction('explain this')}
            disabled={clipboardLoading}
            className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded border border-cyber text-xs text-ice hover:bg-void/60 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Explain Clipboard
          </button>
        </div>
        {clipboardLoading && (
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[11px] text-steel">
            <Loader2 size={12} className="animate-spin" />
            <span>Processing clipboard content...</span>
          </div>
        )}
        {clipboardResult && !clipboardLoading && (
          <div className="bg-void/60 border border-cyber/50 rounded p-2.5 text-[11px] text-ice/90 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
            {clipboardResult}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ── Workspace RAG Folder Picker ───────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="hud-panel hud-chamfer-sm p-3 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <FolderSearch size={16} className="text-cyan-arc" />
          <span className="font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">WORKSPACE RAG</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <input
            className={inputClasses}
            placeholder="Workspace folder path (e.g. D:\Projects\MyApp)"
            value={workspacePath}
            onChange={(e) => setWorkspacePath(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button
            onClick={saveWorkspacePath}
            className="flex items-center space-x-1 whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-2 rounded bg-cyan-arc/10 border border-cyan-arc/30 text-xs text-cyan-arc hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 transition-colors"
          >
            <Save size={12} />
            <span>{pathSaved ? 'Saved ✓' : 'Save'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="text-[10px] text-steel">
          Set the folder path for workspace document search (RAG). Files will be indexed for grounded Q&A.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SystemAndAlarms;
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/TaskInspector.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import { OctagonAlert, Play, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Eye, Code, Terminal, Layers } from 'lucide-react';

export interface AgentStepItem {
  step: number;
  tier: 'code' | 'mcp' | 'vision_gui' | 'web';
  thought: string;
  action: string;
  status: 'running' | 'done' | 'error' | 'aborted';
  screenshot_b64?: string;
  output?: string;
}

interface TaskInspectorProps {
  steps: AgentStepItem[];
  onAbortAgent: () => void;
}

export const TaskInspector: React.FC<TaskInspectorProps> = ({ steps, onAbortAgent }) => {
  const currentStep = steps.length > 0 ? steps[steps.length - 1] : null;

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full bg-void/30 p-4 space-y-4 overflow-hidden">
      {/* Top Controls Bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-tactical border border-cyber p-3 rounded hud-panel">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <Layers size={18} className="text-cyan-arc" />
          <span className="font-hud text-sm font-bold tracking-wider text-ice">
            3-TIER OS AUTONOMY INSPECTOR
          </span>
          {currentStep && currentStep.status === 'running' && (
            <span className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-[10px] font-mono bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-arc/40 animate-pulse">
              <Play size={10} />
              <span>ACTIVE STEP {currentStep.step}</span>
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Emergency Abort Button */}
        <button
          onClick={onAbortAgent}
          className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-crimson/20 hover:bg-crimson/30 text-crimson border border-crimson rounded text-xs font-mono font-bold tracking-wider transition-all shadow-crimson-glow"
          title="Instant Hardware/Software Kill-Switch"
        >
          <OctagonAlert size={15} />
          <span>[ESC] ABORT AGENT</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid: Steps Feed & Live Screenshot Viewer */}
      <div className="flex-1 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4 min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Left Column: Observe-Think-Act Step Log */}
        <div className="flex flex-col bg-tactical border border-cyber rounded p-3 overflow-hidden hud-panel">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-cyber/50 text-xs font-mono text-steel">
            <span>EXECUTION PIPELINE</span>
            <span>{steps.length} EVENTS RECORDED</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 mt-3 pr-1 font-mono text-xs">
            {steps.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-steel/50 select-none">
                <Terminal size={32} className="mb-2 opacity-50" />
                <span>No active autonomous OS tasks.</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] mt-1 text-steel/40">Tier 1-3 tasks will stream steps live here.</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              steps.map((s, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className={`p-2.5 rounded border text-xs space-y-1.5 ${
                    s.status === 'done'
                      ? 'bg-cyan-arc/5 border-cyan-arc/30'
                      : s.status === 'error' || s.status === 'aborted'
                      ? 'bg-crimson/10 border-crimson/40'
                      : 'bg-gold-reactor/5 border-gold-reactor/30'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="font-bold text-ice flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                      <span className="text-gold-reactor">STEP {s.step}</span>
                      <span className="text-steel">[{s.tier.toUpperCase()}]</span>
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[9px] uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                        s.status === 'done'
                          ? 'text-cyan-arc bg-cyan-arc/20'
                          : s.status === 'error' || s.status === 'aborted'
                          ? 'text-crimson bg-crimson/20'
                          : 'text-gold-reactor bg-gold-reactor/20 animate-pulse'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {s.status}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-steel text-[11px] leading-relaxed">{s.thought}</p>

                  {s.output && (
                    <pre className="bg-void/80 p-2 rounded text-[10px] text-ice/90 overflow-x-auto border border-cyber/40 whitespace-pre-wrap">
                      {s.output}
                    </pre>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Set-of-Mark (SoM) Live Vision Preview */}
        <div className="flex flex-col bg-tactical border border-cyber rounded p-3 overflow-hidden hud-panel">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-cyber/50 text-xs font-mono text-steel">
            <span className="flex items-center space-x-1">
              <Eye size={13} className="text-cyan-arc" />
              <span>SET-OF-MARK VISION GROUNDING</span>
            </span>
            <span>CYAN BOUNDING BOXES</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center bg-void/80 rounded mt-3 p-2 overflow-hidden border border-cyber/40 relative">
            {currentStep?.screenshot_b64 ? (
              <img
                src={`data:image/png;base64,${currentStep.screenshot_b64}`}
                alt="Set-of-Mark Vision Grounding"
                className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain rounded border border-cyan-arc/40 shadow-arc-glow"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-steel/50 select-none">
                <Eye size={36} className="mb-2 opacity-40 text-cyan-arc" />
                <span className="text-xs font-mono">No active visual snapshot.</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] mt-1 text-steel/40">Tier 3 vision grounding previews appear here.</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="ui/src/components/TopTelemetryBar.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import { Cpu, HardDrive, Battery, BatteryCharging, Wifi, WifiOff, Minimize2, Maximize2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface TelemetryData {
  cpu: number;
  ram: number;
  battery: number;
  charging: boolean;
  online: boolean;
}

interface AudioData {
  mic_active: boolean;
  tts_active: boolean;
  status: 'idle' | 'listening' | 'thinking' | 'speaking';
  meeting_auto_muted: boolean;
}

interface TokenData {
  provider: string;
  model: string;
  latency_ms: number;
  prompt_tokens: number;
  completion_tokens: number;
  total_session_tokens: number;
}

interface TopTelemetryBarProps {
  telemetry: TelemetryData;
  audioState: AudioData;
  tokenData: TokenData;
  isMiniMode: boolean;
  onToggleMiniMode: () => void;
}

export const TopTelemetryBar: React.FC<TopTelemetryBarProps> = ({
  telemetry,
  audioState,
  tokenData,
  isMiniMode,
  onToggleMiniMode,
}) => {
  // Determine Core Visualizer State
  const isAgentWorking = audioState.status === 'thinking';
  const isAudioActive = audioState.status === 'listening' || audioState.status === 'speaking';

  const coreColor = isAudioActive
    ? '#00E5FF'
    : isAgentWorking
    ? '#FFB300'
    : '#6488A4';

  const coreGlow = isAudioActive
    ? 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.4)'
    : isAgentWorking
    ? 'rgba(255, 179, 0, 0.4)'
    : 'rgba(100, 136, 164, 0.15)';

  // Arc Gauge renderer
  const renderRadialGauge = (value: number, label: string, icon: React.ReactNode, color: string) => {
    const radius = 22;
    const stroke = 3;
    const normalizedRadius = radius - stroke * 2;
    const circumference = normalizedRadius * 2 * Math.PI;
    const strokeDashoffset = circumference - (value / 100) * circumference;

    return (
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 bg-void/60 border border-cyber/50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
        <div className="relative w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center">
          <svg height={radius * 2} width={radius * 2} className="transform -rotate-90">
            <circle
              stroke="#16324A"
              fill="transparent"
              strokeWidth={stroke}
              r={normalizedRadius}
              cx={radius}
              cy={radius}
            />
            <circle
              stroke={color}
              fill="transparent"
              strokeWidth={stroke}
              strokeDasharray={`${circumference} ${circumference}`}
              style={{ strokeDashoffset, transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 0.5s ease-in-out' }}
              strokeLinecap="round"
              r={normalizedRadius}
              cx={radius}
              cy={radius}
            />
          </svg>
          <div className="absolute text-[10px] font-mono text-ice font-bold">{Math.round(value)}%</div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-1 text-steel text-[10px] font-mono">
            {icon}
            <span>{label}</span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-ice">{value.toFixed(1)}%</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <header className="w-full bg-tactical border-b border-cyber px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between hud-panel relative z-40 select-none">
      {/* Left: Branding & Core Visualizer */}
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
        {/* Dynamic Geometric Core Visualizer (NO MARVEL LOGOS) */}
        <div className="relative w-12 h-12 flex items-center justify-center">
          {/* Outer Segmented Tech Ring */}
          <svg className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full animate-spin-slow" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
            <circle
              cx="50"
              cy="50"
              r="44"
              fill="none"
              stroke={coreColor}
              strokeWidth="2"
              strokeDasharray="18 10 35 12"
              opacity="0.85"
            />
          </svg>
          {/* Inner Counter-Rotating Ring */}
          <svg className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full animate-spin-reverse" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
            <circle
              cx="50"
              cy="50"
              r="34"
              fill="none"
              stroke={coreColor}
              strokeWidth="1.5"
              strokeDasharray="10 8 20 6"
              opacity="0.65"
            />
          </svg>
          {/* Concentric Geometric Hexagon Center */}
          <div
            className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300"
            style={{
              backgroundColor: coreColor,
              boxShadow: `0 0 16px ${coreGlow}`,
            }}
          >
            <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-void animate-ping" />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Brand & Status Text */}
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span className="text-sm font-hud font-black tracking-widest text-ice glow-cyan">
              J.A.R.V.I.S. <span className="text-cyan-arc font-mono font-normal text-xs">2.0</span>
            </span>
            <span
              className={`text-[9px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded uppercase font-bold tracking-wider ${
                audioState.status === 'listening'
                  ? 'bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc/40'
                  : audioState.status === 'speaking'
                  ? 'bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc border border-cyan-arc/40'
                  : audioState.status === 'thinking'
                  ? 'bg-gold-reactor/20 text-gold-reactor border border-gold-reactor/40'
                  : 'bg-cyber/40 text-steel border border-cyber'
              }`}
            >
              {audioState.status}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-steel flex items-center space-x-2">
            <span>SYS::ONLINE</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span className="text-ice/70">{new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Center: Live Radial Gauges */}
      <div className="hidden md:flex items-center space-x-3">
        {renderRadialGauge(telemetry.cpu, 'CPU LOAD', <Cpu size={11} />, '#00E5FF')}
        {renderRadialGauge(telemetry.ram, 'MEM USAGE', <HardDrive size={11} />, '#00E5FF')}
        {renderRadialGauge(
          telemetry.battery,
          telemetry.charging ? 'CHARGING' : 'BATTERY',
          telemetry.charging ? <BatteryCharging size={11} /> : <Battery size={11} />,
          telemetry.battery < 20 ? '#FF3B5C' : '#00E5FF'
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Right: Live Token & Latency Telemetry + Mini-HUD Toggle */}
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
        {/* Token Telemetry HUD */}
        <div className="hidden lg:flex flex-col text-right font-mono text-[10px] bg-void/60 border border-cyber/50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-1.5 text-ice font-bold">
            <span className="text-gold-reactor">{tokenData.provider.toUpperCase()}</span>
            <span className="text-steel">/</span>
            <span className="text-cyan-arc">{tokenData.model}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-2 text-steel text-[9px]">
            <span>{tokenData.latency_ms}ms</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span>{tokenData.prompt_tokens + tokenData.completion_tokens} tok</span>
            <span>•</span>
            <span className="text-ice/90">Total: {tokenData.total_session_tokens}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Online Network Badge */}
        <div
          className={`flex items-center space-x-1 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-mono border ${
            telemetry.online
              ? 'border-cyan-arc/30 bg-cyan-arc/10 text-cyan-arc'
              : 'border-crimson/30 bg-crimson/10 text-crimson'
          }`}
          title={telemetry.online ? 'Global Network Connected' : 'Offline Mode (Local Fallback Active)'}
        >
          {telemetry.online ? <Wifi size={12} /> : <WifiOff size={12} />}
          <span className="hidden sm:inline font-bold">{telemetry.online ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE'}</span>
        </div>

        {/* Mini-HUD Mode Toggle */}
        <button
          onClick={onToggleMiniMode}
          className="p-1.5 bg-cyber/40 hover:bg-cyan-arc/20 border border-cyber hover:border-cyan-arc text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc rounded transition-colors"
          title={isMiniMode ? 'Restore Full Tactical HUD' : 'Collapse to Ghost Mini-HUD'}
        >
          {isMiniMode ? <Maximize2 size={15} /> : <Minimize2 size={15} />}
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="ui/src/styles/hud.css">
@tailwind base;
