<file path="ui/src/App.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  MessageSquare, Layers, Calendar, Key, Zap, Settings,
  Hexagon, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Mic, MicOff, Volume2, VolumeX,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { TopTelemetryBar } from './components/TopTelemetryBar';
import { CommandStream, ChatMessageItem } from './components/CommandStream';
import { TaskInspector, AgentStepItem } from './components/TaskInspector';
import { MeetingHub } from './components/MeetingHub';
import { ApiKeyVault } from './components/ApiKeyVault';
import { SkillsAndMcp } from './components/SkillsAndMcp';
import { SystemAndAlarms } from './components/SystemAndAlarms';
import { ApprovalModal, ApprovalRequest } from './components/ApprovalModal';

/* ── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const API_BASE = window.location.origin;
const WS_URL = `${window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws'}://${window.location.host}/ws`;

const TABS = [
  { id: 'command', label: 'COMMAND', icon: <MessageSquare size={18} /> },
  { id: 'agent', label: 'AGENT OS', icon: <Layers size={18} /> },
  { id: 'meetings', label: 'MEETINGS', icon: <Calendar size={18} /> },
  { id: 'vault', label: 'API VAULT', icon: <Key size={18} /> },
  { id: 'skills', label: 'SKILLS', icon: <Zap size={18} /> },
  { id: 'system', label: 'SYSTEM', icon: <Settings size={18} /> },
] as const;

type TabId = typeof TABS[number]['id'];

/* ── WebSocket State Types ────────────────────────────────────────── */
interface TelemetryData {
  cpu: number; ram: number; battery: number; charging: boolean; online: boolean;
}
interface AudioData {
  mic_active: boolean; tts_active: boolean;
  status: 'idle' | 'listening' | 'thinking' | 'speaking';
  meeting_auto_muted: boolean;
}
interface TokenData {
  provider: string; model: string; latency_ms: number;
  prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number; total_session_tokens: number;
}

/* ── App Component ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const App: React.FC = () => {
  /* ─ Navigation State ─ */
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabId>('command');
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(true);
  const [isMiniMode, setIsMiniMode] = useState(false);
  const [miniInput, setMiniInput] = useState('');

  /* ─ WebSocket Data Stores ─ */
  const [telemetry, setTelemetry] = useState<TelemetryData>({ cpu: 0, ram: 0, battery: 100, charging: true, online: true });
  const [audioState, setAudioState] = useState<AudioData>({ mic_active: false, tts_active: true, status: 'idle', meeting_auto_muted: false });
  const [tokenData, setTokenData] = useState<TokenData>({ provider: '-', model: '-', latency_ms: 0, prompt_tokens: 0, completion_tokens: 0, total_session_tokens: 0 });
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessageItem[]>([]);
  const [agentSteps, setAgentSteps] = useState<AgentStepItem[]>([]);
  const [approvalRequest, setApprovalRequest] = useState<ApprovalRequest | null>(null);

  /* ─ Meeting data ─ */
  const [meetings, setMeetings] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [summaries, setSummaries] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [captions, setCaptions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentMeeting, setCurrentMeeting] = useState<any>(null);

  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

  /* ─ PyWebView Mini-Mode Bridge ─ */
  const toggleMiniMode = useCallback((enabled: boolean) => {
    setIsMiniMode(enabled);
    try {
      (window as any).pywebview?.api?.set_mini_mode?.(enabled);
    } catch { /* offline / not in pywebview */ }
  }, []);

  /* ─ WebSocket Connection ─ */
  const connectWs = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = () => {
        console.log('[HUD] WebSocket connected');
      };

      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const { type, payload } = JSON.parse(event.data);
          switch (type) {
            case 'SYSTEM_TELEMETRY':
              setTelemetry(payload);
              break;
            case 'AUDIO_STATE':
              setAudioState(payload);
              break;
            case 'TOKEN_TELEMETRY':
              setTokenData((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                ...payload,
                total_session_tokens: payload.total_session_tokens ?? ((prev.total_session_tokens || 0) + (payload.prompt_tokens || 0) + (payload.completion_tokens || 0)),
              }));
              break;
            case 'CHAT_MESSAGE':
              setMessages((prev) => (prev.some((m) => m.id === payload.id) ? prev : [...prev.slice(-200), payload]));
              break;
            case 'AGENT_STEP':
              setAgentSteps((prev) => [...prev.slice(-50), payload]);
              break;
            case 'APPROVAL_REQUIRED':
              setApprovalRequest(payload);
              break;
            case 'MEETING_STATE':
              if (payload.captions) setCaptions(payload.captions);
              if (payload.current_meeting !== undefined) setCurrentMeeting(payload.current_meeting);
              break;
            default:
              break;
          }
        } catch { /* parse error */ }
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        console.log('[HUD] WebSocket disconnected, reconnecting in 3s...');
        reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connectWs, 3000);
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        ws.close();
      };
    } catch {
      reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connectWs, 3000);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    connectWs();
    return () => {
      if (wsRef.current) wsRef.current.close();
      if (reconnectTimer.current) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current);
    };
  }, [connectWs]);

  /* ─ Fetch meetings on tab switch ─ */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'meetings') {
      fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings`)
        .then((r) => r.json())
        .then((data) => {
          setMeetings(data.meetings || []);
          setSummaries(data.summaries || []);
          if (data.live_captions) setCaptions(data.live_captions);
          if (data.current_meeting) setCurrentMeeting(data.current_meeting);
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [activeTab]);

  /* ─ Command / Audio Handlers ─ */
  const sendCommand = useCallback((query: string) => {
    // Send over WebSocket if connected
    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'USER_COMMAND', payload: { query, source: 'text' } }));
    } else {
      // Fallback to REST and manually append user message only when WS is offline
      const userMsg: ChatMessageItem = {
        id: `user-${Date.now()}`,
        sender: 'user',
        source: 'text',
        text: query,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg]);

      fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/command`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query, source: 'text' }),
      })
        .then((r) => r.json())
        .then((data) => {
          setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { ...data, sender: 'jarvis', source: 'text', text: data.text || data.response }]);
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleMic = useCallback(async () => {
    const next = !audioState.mic_active;
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/audio/toggle`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ target: 'mic', enabled: next }),
      });
      setAudioState((prev) => ({ ...prev, mic_active: next }));
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [audioState.mic_active]);

  const toggleTts = useCallback(async () => {
    const next = !audioState.tts_active;
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/audio/toggle`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ target: 'tts', enabled: next }),
      });
      setAudioState((prev) => ({ ...prev, tts_active: next }));
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, [audioState.tts_active]);

  const abortAgent = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/agent/abort`, { method: 'POST' });
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, []);

  /* ─ Meeting Handlers ─ */
  const addMeeting = useCallback(async (meeting: any) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(meeting),
      });
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setMeetings(data.meetings || []);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, []);

  const deleteMeeting = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setMeetings((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id));
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, []);

  const joinMeeting = useCallback(async (url: string, title?: string, id?: string) => {
    try {
      if (id) {
        await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings/${id}/join`, {
          method: 'POST',
        });
      } else {
        await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings/join-url`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ url, title: title || 'Online Meeting' }),
        });
      }
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings`);
      const data = await res.json();
      setMeetings(data.meetings || []);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, []);

  const leaveMeeting = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/meetings/leave`, { method: 'POST' });
      setCurrentMeeting(null);
    } catch { /* offline */ }
  }, []);

  /* ── Ghost Mini-HUD Mode ────────────────────────────────────────── */
  if (isMiniMode) {
    const latestJarvisMsg = [...messages].reverse().find((m) => m.sender === 'jarvis');
    return (
      <div className="h-screen w-screen bg-void hud-scanlines flex flex-col justify-between p-2 select-none overflow-hidden">
        <TopTelemetryBar
          telemetry={telemetry}
          audioState={audioState}
          tokenData={tokenData}
          isMiniMode={true}
          onToggleMiniMode={() => toggleMiniMode(false)}
        />
        {latestJarvisMsg && (
          <div className="px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-mono text-cyan-arc truncate bg-void/60 border border-cyber/40 rounded my-1">
            <span className="text-steel font-bold mr-1">JARVIS:</span>
            {latestJarvisMsg.text}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 pt-1 border-t border-cyber/40">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Ask JARVIS (Enter to send)..."
            value={miniInput}
            onChange={(e) => setMiniInput(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter' && miniInput.trim()) {
                sendCommand(miniInput.trim());
                setMiniInput('');
              }
            }}
            className="flex-1 bg-void/80 border border-cyber text-ice text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded focus:border-cyan-arc focus:outline-none font-mono"
          />
          <button
            onClick={toggleMic}
            className={`p-1.5 rounded border transition-colors ${
              audioState.mic_active
                ? 'border-cyan-arc bg-cyan-arc/20 text-cyan-arc animate-pulse-glow'
                : 'border-cyber text-steel hover:text-ice'
            }`}
            title="Toggle Mic"
          >
            {audioState.mic_active ? <Mic size={14} /> : <MicOff size={14} />}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={toggleTts}
            className={`p-1.5 rounded border transition-colors ${
              audioState.tts_active
                ? 'border-cyan-arc bg-cyan-arc/10 text-cyan-arc'
                : 'border-cyber text-steel hover:text-ice'
            }`}
            title="Toggle TTS"
          >
            {audioState.tts_active ? <Volume2 size={14} /> : <VolumeX size={14} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* ── Full HUD Layout ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const renderActivePanel = () => {
    switch (activeTab) {
      case 'command':
        return (
          <CommandStream
            messages={messages}
            micActive={audioState.mic_active}
            ttsActive={audioState.tts_active}
            meetingAutoMuted={audioState.meeting_auto_muted}
            onSendCommand={sendCommand}
            onToggleMic={toggleMic}
            onToggleTts={toggleTts}
          />
        );
      case 'agent':
        return <TaskInspector steps={agentSteps} onAbortAgent={abortAgent} />;
      case 'meetings':
        return (
          <MeetingHub
            meetings={meetings}
            summaries={summaries}
            liveCaptions={captions}
            currentMeeting={currentMeeting}
            onAddMeeting={addMeeting}
            onDeleteMeeting={deleteMeeting}
            onJoinMeeting={joinMeeting}
            onLeaveMeeting={leaveMeeting}
            apiBase={API_BASE}
          />
        );
      case 'vault':
        return <ApiKeyVault apiBase={API_BASE} />;
      case 'skills':
        return <SkillsAndMcp apiBase={API_BASE} />;
      case 'system':
        return <SystemAndAlarms apiBase={API_BASE} />;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-screen w-screen bg-void hud-scanlines flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
      {/* Top Telemetry Bar */}
      <TopTelemetryBar
        telemetry={telemetry}
        audioState={audioState}
        tokenData={tokenData}
        isMiniMode={false}
        onToggleMiniMode={() => toggleMiniMode(true)}
      />

      {/* Main Body: Sidebar + Content */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex overflow-hidden">
        {/* ── Holographic Sidebar ─────────────────────────────────── */}
        <div
          className={`flex flex-col h-full bg-tactical border-r border-cyber transition-all duration-300 ${
            sidebarOpen ? 'w-48' : 'w-12'
          }`}
        >
          {/* Sidebar Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(!sidebarOpen)}
            className="flex items-center justify-center h-8 text-steel hover:text-cyan-arc transition-colors border-b border-cyber/50"
          >
            {sidebarOpen ? <ChevronLeft size={14} /> : <ChevronRight size={14} />}
          </button>

          {/* JARVIS Logo */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-3 border-b border-cyber/30">
            <Hexagon
              size={sidebarOpen ? 28 : 20}
              className="text-cyan-arc animate-pulse-glow"
              strokeWidth={1.5}
            />
            {sidebarOpen && (
              <span className="ml-2 font-hud text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.25em] text-cyan-arc glow-cyan">
                J.A.R.V.I.S.
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Navigation Tabs */}
          <nav className="flex-1 flex flex-col py-2 space-y-0.5">
            {TABS.map(({ id, label, icon }) => {
              const isActive = activeTab === id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={id}
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(id)}
                  className={`flex items-center px-3 py-2.5 transition-all ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-cyan-arc/10 border-l-2 border-cyan-arc text-cyan-arc'
                      : 'border-l-2 border-transparent text-steel hover:text-ice hover:bg-void/30'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className={isActive ? 'text-cyan-arc' : ''}>{icon}</span>
                  {sidebarOpen && (
                    <span className={`ml-3 text-[11px] font-hud tracking-wider ${isActive ? 'font-bold' : ''}`}>
                      {label}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          {/* Footer */}
          {sidebarOpen && (
            <div className="px-3 py-2 border-t border-cyber/30">
              <div className="text-[9px] text-steel text-center font-mono">JARVIS 2.0 HUD</div>
              <div className="text-[9px] text-steel/50 text-center font-mono">TACTICAL INTERFACE</div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ── Main Content Panel ──────────────────────────────────── */}
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
          {renderActivePanel()}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Approval Modal Overlay */}
      <ApprovalModal
        request={approvalRequest}
        apiBase={API_BASE}
        onDismiss={() => setApprovalRequest(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

export default App;
</file>

<file path="meeting_module_guide.md">
# 🤖 JARVIS Meeting Attendant Module — Setup & Usage Guide

## Architecture Overview

```mermaid
graph TD
    A["JARVIS Core (main.py)"] --> B["meeting_handler.py"]
    B --> C["modules/meeting_bot.py<br/>Playwright Automation"]
    B --> D["modules/meeting_scheduler.py<br/>Schedule + APScheduler"]
    B --> E["modules/schedule_interface.py<br/>FastAPI Dashboard"]
    D -->|"triggers 60s early"| C
    E -->|"REST API"| D
    E -->|"Join Now / Leave"| C
    D -->|"persists"| F["config/meetings.json"]
    C -->|"persistent session"| G[".browser_profile/"]
```

## Files Created / Modified

| File | Action | Purpose |
|------|--------|---------|
| [`modules/__init__.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/modules/__init__.py) | **New** | Package marker |
| [`modules/meeting_bot.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/modules/meeting_bot.py) | **New** | Headed Playwright browser automation for Google Meet & Teams |
| [`modules/meeting_scheduler.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/modules/meeting_scheduler.py) | **New** | Schedule persistence (JSON), APScheduler trigger engine, NLP parsing |
| [`modules/schedule_interface.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/modules/schedule_interface.py) | **New** | FastAPI web dashboard + REST API at `localhost:5050` |
| [`meeting_handler.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/meeting_handler.py) | **New** | Top-level handler bridging modules into JARVIS core |
| [`config/meetings.json`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/config/meetings.json) | **New** | Persistent meeting schedule storage |
| [`core.py`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/core.py) | **Modified** | Import + handler init + command dispatch + AI prompt |
| [`requirements.txt`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/requirements.txt) | **Modified** | Added `playwright`, `APScheduler`, `fastapi`, `uvicorn` |
| [`.env`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/.env) / [`.env.example`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/.env.example) | **Modified** | Added `MEETING_DASHBOARD_PORT/HOST` |
| [`.gitignore`](file:///d:/Projects,%20hackathons/JARVIS/.gitignore) | **Modified** | Excludes `.browser_profile/` and `config/meetings.json` |

---

## Setup Instructions

### 1. Install Dependencies

```bash
pip install -r requirements.txt
python -m playwright install chromium
```

### 2. One-Time Browser Login (Critical!)

> [!IMPORTANT]
> You must log into your Google and Microsoft accounts once in the persistent browser so the bot can join calls without re-authentication.

```bash
python -m modules.meeting_bot --setup
```

This opens a headed Edge browser with two tabs:
1. **Google Accounts** — log in to your Google account (for Google Meet)
2. **Microsoft Online** — log in to your Microsoft account (for Teams)

**Close the browser window** when done. Your session is saved to `.browser_profile/` and will be reused automatically.

### 3. Start JARVIS

```bash
python main.py
```

On startup you'll see:
```
[✓] Meeting attendant module ready
[ScheduleInterface] Dashboard running at http://127.0.0.1:5050
```

---

## Voice Commands

| Say this | What happens |
|----------|--------------|
| *"Schedule Team Standup at 2:30 PM with link https://meet.google.com/abc"* | Adds meeting, bot joins 60s early |
| *"What meetings do I have today?"* | Reads out today's schedule |
| *"Cancel my next meeting"* | Cancels the next pending meeting |
| *"Join the meeting"* / *"Join now"* | Immediately joins the next pending meeting |
| *"Leave the meeting"* | Force-exits the active Playwright session |
| *"Open schedule dashboard"* | Opens `http://localhost:5050` in your browser |

---

## Web Dashboard

Access at **http://127.0.0.1:5050** (or say *"Jarvis, open schedule dashboard"*).

### Features
- **Quick-Add Form** — Name, URL, start time, duration
- **Today's Timeline** — Live view of all meetings with status badges (pending/active/completed/cancelled)
- **Manual Overrides** — "Join Now" and "Cancel" buttons on each pending meeting
- **Bulk Importer** — Paste a JSON array to schedule multiple meetings at once

### REST API Endpoints

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| `GET` | `/api/meetings` | List all meetings |
| `GET` | `/api/meetings/today` | Today's meetings only |
| `GET` | `/api/meetings/pending` | Future pending meetings |
| `POST` | `/api/meetings` | Add a new meeting |
| `DELETE` | `/api/meetings/{id}` | Cancel a meeting |
| `POST` | `/api/meetings/{id}/join` | Force-join a meeting now |
| `POST` | `/api/meetings/leave` | Leave active meeting |
| `POST` | `/api/meetings/bulk` | Bulk import (JSON array) |

---

## How Auto-Join Works

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant User
    participant JARVIS
    participant Scheduler as APScheduler
    participant Bot as Playwright Bot
    participant Meet as Google Meet / Teams

    User->>JARVIS: "Schedule standup at 2 PM with link ..."
    JARVIS->>Scheduler: add_meeting() + schedule trigger at 1:59 PM
    Note over Scheduler: Waits until T-60 seconds...
    Scheduler->>Bot: trigger_join(meeting)
    Bot->>Meet: Navigate to URL (headed browser)
    Bot->>Meet: Toggle mic OFF, camera OFF
    Bot->>Meet: Click "Join now"
    loop Every 30 seconds
        Bot->>Meet: Check participant count
        alt Participants < 2 or end_time reached
            Bot->>Meet: Click Leave / close page
        end
    end
```

---

## Non-Blocking Architecture

> [!NOTE]
> All meeting subsystems run in independent daemon threads — they never block JARVIS's primary audio capture, TTS, or main event loop.

- **APScheduler** — Background daemon thread polling the schedule queue
- **FastAPI Dashboard** — Uvicorn runs in a daemon thread
- **Playwright Bot** — Each `join_meeting()` call runs in its own daemon thread
- **JARVIS Main Loop** — Continues listening for voice commands uninterrupted

---

## Bulk Import JSON Format

```json
[
  {
    "title": "Monday Standup",
    "meeting_url": "https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij",
    "start_time": "2026-10-13T09:00:00",
    "end_time": "2026-10-13T09:30:00",
    "auto_leave": true
  },
  {
    "title": "Sprint Planning",
    "meeting_url": "https://teams.microsoft.com/l/meetup-join/...",
    "start_time": "2026-10-13T14:00:00",
    "end_time": "2026-10-13T15:00:00"
  }
]
```
</file>

<file path="PROJECT.md">
# PROJECT: JARVIS 2.0 — Autonomous 3-Tier OS Agent, Multi-Provider API Vault & Holographic Desktop HUD

## Overview
JARVIS 2.0 is an autonomous desktop AI operating system and hybrid voice/text assistant. It combines local deterministic capabilities with dynamic multi-tier autonomy, resilient multi-key LLM routing, and a Marvel-inspired Holographic Desktop HUD (without copyrighted logos) packaged via PyWebView and FastAPI WebSockets.

## Architecture Highlights
- **Unified InputGateway**: Text + Voice command processing with independent microphone listening and TTS speech toggles.
- **Dynamic Multi-Key API Vault**: Multi-key rotation on 429/quota limits, custom OpenAI-compatible endpoints (Ollama, LM Studio, vLLM, DeepSeek, custom proxies), live token telemetry, and role-based model assignment.
- **3-Tier Dynamic OS Operation**:
  - *Tier 1*: Dynamic Python & Shell Code Synthesizer (`agent_os/code_executor.py`).
  - *Tier 2*: Model Context Protocol (MCP) Client Hub (`integrations/mcp_client.py`).
  - *Tier 3*: Set-of-Mark (SoM) Vision & Accessibility GUI Agent (`agent_os/state_machine.py`, `ui_distiller.py`, `som_vision.py`, `os_actuator.py`) with instant `[ESC]` hardware kill-switch.
- **High-Impact Assistant Modules**:
  - Safety Interceptor & Approval Modal (`agent_os/safety_interceptor.py`).
  - Smart Meeting Attendant 2.0 with DOM caption scraping & post-meeting LLM summaries (`automation/meeting_bot.py`).
  - Autonomous Web Researcher (`automation/web_agent.py`).
  - Universal Clipboard & Selection Copilot (`integrations/handlers/clipboard_copilot.py`).
  - Skills, Macros & Teach Mode (`integrations/skill_store.py`).
  - Contextual Workspace RAG (`integrations/workspace_rag.py`).
- **Frameless Holographic Desktop HUD**:
  - React + Vite + Tailwind CSS + Lucide-React frontend.
  - PyWebView frameless window with Ghost Mini-HUD mode and browser fallback.
  - Programmatic geometric Arc-Cyan HUD desktop icon generator (`scripts/create_desktop_icon.py`).
</file>

<file path="ROADMAP.md">
# ROADMAP: JARVIS 2.0 Autonomous Engineering Loops

- [ ] **Loop 0: GSD Initialization & Clean Directory Refactoring**
  - Project spec and state files (`PROJECT.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `LOOP_STATE.md`)
  - Target directory structure setup and legacy code migration
  - Module verification test `tests/test_loop0_structure.py`
- [ ] **Loop 1: Multi-Key API Vault, Custom OpenAI Adapter & Role Router**
  - Settings manager with `.env` seeding and `config/api_vault.json` persistence
  - Adapter architecture (`base.py`, `gemini_adapter.py`, `groq_adapter.py`, `anthropic_adapter.py`, `openai_compat.py`)
  - Multi-key rotation, failover, telemetry, and role dispatching in `core/llm_router.py`
  - Verification test `tests/test_loop1_vault.py`
- [ ] **Loop 2: Independent Audio State Machine, Meeting Interlock & Unified Input Gateway**
  - Unified TTS engine with `tts_active` toggle in `audio/tts_engine.py`
  - Thread-safe Voice listener with `mic_active` toggle, meeting auto-mute & manual override in `audio/voice_listener.py`
  - Unified `InputGateway` in `core/engine.py` with deterministic & LLM triage
  - Verification test `tests/test_loop2_audio_engine.py`
- [ ] **Loop 3: 3-Tier Dynamic OS Agent & Safety Interceptor**
  - Risk analyzer & Approval Modal gatekeeper in `agent_os/safety_interceptor.py`
  - Tier 1: Dynamic Python & Shell Code Synthesizer (`agent_os/code_executor.py`)
  - Tier 3: SoM Vision, UI Distiller, Actuator with `[ESC]` kill-switch, and State Machine
  - Verification test `tests/test_loop3_agent_os.py`
- [ ] **Loop 4: MCP Client, Skills/Teach Mode, Clipboard Copilot, Workspace RAG & Smart Meetings**
  - Tier 2 MCP Client Hub (`integrations/mcp_client.py`)
  - Skills store, macros, and Teach Mode (`integrations/skill_store.py`)
  - Clipboard Copilot (`integrations/handlers/clipboard_copilot.py`) and Workspace RAG (`integrations/workspace_rag.py`)
  - Smart Meeting Attendant 2.0 with caption scraping and AI summary (`automation/meeting_bot.py`)
  - Autonomous Web Researcher (`automation/web_agent.py`)
  - Verification test `tests/test_loop4_integrations.py`
- [ ] **Loop 5: FastAPI + WebSocket Server & Marvel JARVIS Holographic HUD (React/Tailwind)**
  - Backend server (`automation/server.py`) with REST + WebSocket event contract
  - Modern React + Vite + Tailwind frontend in `ui/` with Marvel HUD aesthetic and zero logos
  - All 6 panels + mini-HUD mode + Approval Modal + token telemetry
  - Build frontend bundle to `ui/dist`
- [ ] **Loop 6: Native Desktop App Wrapper (PyWebView), Clickable Desktop Icon & DevTools Audit**
  - Desktop wrapper in `main.py` with PyWebView and Chrome app fallback

<file path="automation/meeting_bot.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Meeting Bot Module
Headed Playwright browser automation for Google Meet and Microsoft Teams.

Uses a persistent browser profile so Google/Microsoft accounts stay logged in
across runs (bypassing 2FA/bot-detection). The browser launches in headed mode
so the user can verbally take over calls at any moment.

One-time setup:
    python -m modules.meeting_bot --setup
    (Opens a browser window for you to manually log in to Google & Microsoft.)
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import threading
import subprocess
import re
import urllib.parse
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

# Playwright
try:
    from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright, TimeoutError as PWTimeoutError
    PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE = False
    print("[!] Playwright not installed. Meeting bot disabled.")

# ─── Paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, ".browser_profile")

# Browser launch flags
CHROMIUM_FLAGS = [
    "--use-fake-ui-for-media-stream",   # auto-grant mic/camera permission dialogs
    "--no-sandbox",
    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
    "--disable-infobars",
    "--disable-notifications",
]


class MeetingBot:
    """
    Headed Playwright bot for joining Google Meet / Microsoft Teams calls.

    Key design decisions:
    - `headless=False` so the user can see and take over the call.
    - Persistent browser context so logins survive across restarts.
    - Mic & camera forced OFF before clicking "Join".
    - 30-second polling loop to auto-leave when participants < 2 or end_time arrives.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self._playwright = None
        self._browser = None
        self._context = None
        self._active_page = None
        self._monitor_thread = None
        self._should_leave = threading.Event()
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

        # Ensure profile directory exists
        os.makedirs(BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR, exist_ok=True)

        # Callbacks for meeting-aware audio interlocking & UI notifications
        self.on_meeting_joined = None
        self.on_meeting_left = None

        # Live Caption Feed & Meeting State
        self.live_captions: list = []
        self._seen_captions: set = set()
        self.current_meeting: dict = None

    # ─── Lifecycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _ensure_browser(self):
        """Lazily start the Playwright browser (persistent context)."""
        if self._context is not None:
            try:
                # Test if context is still alive
                _ = self._context.pages
                return
            except Exception:
                self._context = None

        if self._playwright is None:
            self._playwright = sync_playwright().start()

        try:
            self._context = self._playwright.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR,
                headless=False,
                args=CHROMIUM_FLAGS,
                ignore_default_args=["--enable-automation"],
                channel="chrome",  # use locally installed Chrome
                viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 720},
                permissions=["microphone", "camera"],
            )
            print("[MeetingBot] Browser launched (Chrome, persistent context)")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Chrome launch fallback to standard chromium ({e})")
            self._context = self._playwright.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR,
                headless=False,
                args=CHROMIUM_FLAGS,
                ignore_default_args=["--enable-automation"],
                viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 720},
                permissions=["microphone", "camera"],
            )

    def close(self):
        """Shut down the browser and Playwright cleanly."""
        self._should_leave.set()
        try:
            if self._active_page:
                try:
                    if not self._active_page.is_closed():
                        self._active_page.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._active_page = None
            if self._context:
                try:
                    self._context.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._context = None
            if self._playwright:
                try:
                    self._playwright.stop()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self._playwright = None
        except Exception:
            pass

    @classmethod
    def run_account_setup_async(cls) -> subprocess.Popen:
        """Launch the interactive setup in a separate non-blocking subprocess."""
        cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "automation.meeting_bot", "--setup"]
        print(f"[MeetingBot] Spawning browser account setup subprocess: {' '.join(cmd)}")
        return subprocess.Popen(cmd)

    # ─── Platform Detection ───────────────────────────────────────

    @staticmethod
    def detect_platform(url: str) -> str:
        if "meet.google.com" in url:
            return "google_meet"
        elif "teams.microsoft.com" in url or "teams.live.com" in url:
            return "teams"
        return "unknown"

    # ─── Join Meeting ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    def join_meeting(self, url: str, end_time: datetime = None,
                     auto_leave: bool = True, title: str = "Online Meeting"):
        """
        Navigate to the meeting URL, disable mic/camera, click Join,
        and start the monitoring loop with live caption scraping.
        """
        if not PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE:
            print("[MeetingBot] Playwright not installed — cannot join.")
            return

        self._should_leave.clear()
        self.live_captions.clear()
        self._seen_captions.clear()
        self.current_meeting = {"title": title, "url": url, "joined_at": datetime.now().isoformat()}

        # Audio interlock: auto-mute JARVIS listening so meeting audio doesn't trigger commands
        try:
            from audio.voice_listener import voice_listener
            voice_listener.on_meeting_state_change(True, meeting_title=title)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Voice interlock notice: {e}")

        self._ensure_browser()

        platform = self.detect_platform(url)
        try:
            page = self._context.new_page()
        except Exception:
            # Recreate browser context if previous one was closed
            self._context = None
            self._ensure_browser()
            page = self._context.new_page()

        self._active_page = page

        try:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Navigating to {platform} meeting...")
            page.goto(url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=30000)

            if platform == "google_meet":
                self._join_google_meet(page)
            elif platform == "teams":
                self._join_teams(page)
            else:
                print(f"[MeetingBot] Unknown platform for URL: {url}")
                page.close()
                return

            # Trigger joined hook
            if callable(self.on_meeting_joined):
                try:
                    self.on_meeting_joined(url)
                except Exception as cb_err:
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] on_meeting_joined callback error: {cb_err}")

            # Start monitoring loop with caption scraping
            if auto_leave:
                self._monitor_call(page, end_time, platform)
            else:
                print("[MeetingBot] Auto-leave is disabled. Staying in call until manually closed.")
                while not self._should_leave.is_set():
                    if page.is_closed():
                        break
                    self._scrape_live_captions(page, platform)
                    time.sleep(3)

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Error during meeting: {e}")
        finally:
            self.current_meeting = None
            try:
                if self._active_page and not self._active_page.is_closed():
                    self._active_page.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._active_page = None

            # Audio interlock: restore mic listening if auto-muted
            try:
                from audio.voice_listener import voice_listener
                voice_listener.on_meeting_state_change(False, meeting_title=title)
            except Exception:
                pass

            # Generate AI post-call summary
            try:
                self._generate_post_call_summary(title)
            except Exception as sum_err:
                print(f"[MeetingBot] Summary generation error: {sum_err}")

            # Trigger left hook
            if callable(self.on_meeting_left):
                try:
                    self.on_meeting_left(url)
                except Exception as cb_err:
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] on_meeting_left callback error: {cb_err}")

            # Always cleanly shut down the context and Playwright on this thread
            self.close()

    # ─── Google Meet ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _join_google_meet(self, page):
        """
        Handle the Google Meet pre-join lobby:
        1. Wait for the lobby to load
        2. Toggle mic and camera OFF
        3. Click "Join now" or "Ask to join"
        """
        if self._should_leave.is_set():
            return

        # Wait for the pre-join lobby to settle
        for _ in range(8):
            if self._should_leave.is_set():
                return
            page.wait_for_timeout(500)

        if self._should_leave.is_set():
            return

        # ── Mic OFF ──
        # Google Meet mic toggle: aria-label contains "microphone"
        try:
            mic_btn = page.locator('[aria-label*="microphone" i][data-is-muted]').first
            if mic_btn.count() > 0:
                is_muted = mic_btn.get_attribute("data-is-muted")
                if is_muted == "false":
                    mic_btn.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Mic toggled OFF")
            else:
                # Fallback: look for the mic button by tooltip text
                mic_fallback = page.locator('[aria-label*="Turn off microphone" i]').first
                if mic_fallback.count() > 0:
                    mic_fallback.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Mic toggled OFF (fallback)")
        except Exception:
            print("[MeetingBot] Could not detect mic state — proceeding")

        # ── Camera OFF ──
        try:
            cam_btn = page.locator('[aria-label*="camera" i][data-is-muted]').first
            if cam_btn.count() > 0:
                is_muted = cam_btn.get_attribute("data-is-muted")
                if is_muted == "false":
                    cam_btn.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Camera toggled OFF")
            else:
                cam_fallback = page.locator('[aria-label*="Turn off camera" i]').first
                if cam_fallback.count() > 0:
                    cam_fallback.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Camera toggled OFF (fallback)")
        except Exception:
            print("[MeetingBot] Could not detect camera state — proceeding")

        page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

        # ── Click "Join now" or "Ask to join" ──
        join_clicked = False
        for selector in [
            'button:has-text("Join now")',
            'button:has-text("Ask to join")',
            'button:has-text("Join")',
            '[data-idom-class*="join"]',
        ]:
            try:
                btn = page.locator(selector).first
                if btn.count() > 0 and btn.is_visible():
                    btn.click()
                    join_clicked = True
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] Clicked: {selector}")
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue

        if not join_clicked:
            print("[MeetingBot] Could not find Join button -- page may need manual intervention")

        # Allow time for the call to connect
        page.wait_for_timeout(5000)
        print("[MeetingBot] [OK] Joined Google Meet")

    # ─── Microsoft Teams ──────────────────────────────────────────

    def _join_teams(self, page):
        """
        Handle Microsoft Teams join flow:
        1. Handle launcher page redirect & fallback to direct web client URL
        2. Wait for pre-join lobby to load
        3. Fill guest display name if required
        4. Toggle mic and camera OFF
        5. Click Join now
        """
        if self._should_leave.is_set():
            return

        page.wait_for_timeout(3000)
        if self._should_leave.is_set():
            return

        # 1. Launcher page handling
        if "launcher" in page.url:
            print("[MeetingBot] Detected Teams launcher page...")
            try:
                cont_btn = page.locator('button[data-tid="joinOnWeb"], button:has-text("Continue on this browser"), a:has-text("Join on the web instead")').first
                if cont_btn.count() > 0 and cont_btn.is_visible():
                    cont_btn.click()
                    page.wait_for_timeout(2500)
                    print("[MeetingBot] Clicked 'Continue on this browser'")
            except Exception:
                pass

            # If still on launcher page, navigate directly using url query parameter
            if "launcher" in page.url:
                parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(page.url)
                qs = urllib.parse.parse_qs(parsed.query)
                target = qs.get("url", [None])[0]
                if target:
                    direct_url = f"https://teams.microsoft.com{target}"
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] Directing to web client: {direct_url}")
                    page.goto(direct_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=30000)

        # 2. Wait up to 20 seconds for pre-join lobby elements
        print("[MeetingBot] Waiting for Teams pre-join lobby...")
        for _ in range(20):
            if self._should_leave.is_set():
                return
            if page.locator('button[data-tid="prejoin-join-button"], #prejoin-join-button, button:has-text("Join now"), input[data-tid="toggle-mute"]').count() > 0:
                break
            page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

        if self._should_leave.is_set():
            return

        page.wait_for_timeout(1500)

        # 3. Check guest display name input
        try:
            name_input = page.locator('input[data-tid="prejoin-display-name-input"], input[placeholder*="name" i]').first
            if name_input.count() > 0 and name_input.is_visible() and not name_input.input_value().strip():
                name_input.fill("JARVIS")
                print("[MeetingBot] Entered guest name: JARVIS")
                page.wait_for_timeout(500)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 4. Toggle mic OFF
        try:
            mic_inp = page.locator('input[data-tid="toggle-mute"]').first
            if mic_inp.count() > 0 and mic_inp.is_checked():
                mic_inp.click()
                print("[MeetingBot] Teams mic toggled OFF (checkbox)")
            else:
                mic_btn = page.locator('button[data-tid="toggle-mute"], [aria-label*="Mic" i]').first
                if mic_btn.count() > 0 and (mic_btn.get_attribute("aria-checked") == "true" or mic_btn.get_attribute("aria-pressed") == "true"):
                    mic_btn.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Teams mic toggled OFF (button)")
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 5. Toggle camera OFF
        try:
            vid_inp = page.locator('input[data-tid="toggle-video"]').first
            if vid_inp.count() > 0 and vid_inp.is_checked():
                vid_inp.click()
                print("[MeetingBot] Teams camera toggled OFF (checkbox)")
            else:
                vid_btn = page.locator('button[data-tid="toggle-video"], [aria-label*="Camera" i]').first
                if vid_btn.count() > 0 and (vid_btn.get_attribute("aria-checked") == "true" or vid_btn.get_attribute("aria-pressed") == "true"):
                    vid_btn.click()
                    print("[MeetingBot] Teams camera toggled OFF (button)")
        except Exception:
            pass

        page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

        # 6. Click Join now
        join_clicked = False
        for selector in [
            'button[data-tid="prejoin-join-button"]',
            '#prejoin-join-button',
            'button:has-text("Join now")',
            'button:has-text("Join")',
            '#join-btn',
        ]:
            try:
                btn = page.locator(selector).first
                if btn.count() > 0 and btn.is_visible() and btn.is_enabled():
                    btn.click()
                    join_clicked = True
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] Clicked: {selector}")
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue

        if not join_clicked:
            try:
                page.locator('[data-tid="prejoin-join-button"]').first.click(timeout=3000)
                join_clicked = True
                print("[MeetingBot] Clicked fallback [data-tid='prejoin-join-button']")
            except Exception:
                pass

        page.wait_for_timeout(5000)
        print("[MeetingBot] [OK] Joined Microsoft Teams")

    # ─── In-Call Monitoring ───────────────────────────────────────

    def _monitor_call(self, page, end_time: datetime, platform: str):
        """
        Poll every 15 seconds. Leave when:
        - Participant count was >= 2 and drops below 2 (after 3 min grace period)
        - Scheduled end_time is reached (and not within first 5 minutes)
        - _should_leave event is set (external leave command)
        - Call end modal appears
        """
        join_time = datetime.now()
        # Defensive end_time: if end_time <= join_time + 5min, extend to join_time + 1h
        if end_time is None or end_time <= join_time + timedelta(minutes=5):
            end_time = join_time + timedelta(hours=1)
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Adjusted safe end_time to {end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}")

        max_participants_seen = 0
        grace_period_end = join_time + timedelta(minutes=3)

        print(f"[MeetingBot] Monitoring call (safe end_time: {end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')})...")
        while not self._should_leave.is_set():
            # Check for external leave in 1-second slices so leave_meeting is instantaneous
            for _ in range(15):
                if self._should_leave.is_set():
                    print("[MeetingBot] External leave requested -- leaving meeting")
                    self._leave_page(page, platform)
                    return
                # Check if page was closed externally
                try:
                    if page.is_closed():
                        print("[MeetingBot] Page closed externally -- exiting monitor")
                        return
                except Exception:
                    return
                # Scrape live captions periodically
                if _ % 3 == 0:
                    self._scrape_live_captions(page, platform)

                time.sleep(1)

            now = datetime.now()

            # Check end_time (only if at least 5 min after join)
            if now >= end_time and now >= join_time + timedelta(minutes=5):
                print("[MeetingBot] Scheduled end time reached -- leaving meeting")
                self._leave_page(page, platform)
                return

            # Check participant count
            count = self._get_participant_count(page, platform)
            if count is not None:
                max_participants_seen = max(max_participants_seen, count)
                # Only leave if call actually had participants (max >= 2) and dropped below 2,
                # and we're past initial 3-minute joining grace period
                if now >= grace_period_end and max_participants_seen >= 2 and count < 2:
                    print(f"[MeetingBot] Participants dropped from {max_participants_seen} to {count} -- leaving meeting")
                    self._leave_page(page, platform)
                    return

            # Check for "call ended" overlay
            if self._is_call_ended(page, platform):
                if now >= join_time + timedelta(seconds=30):
                    print("[MeetingBot] Call ended detected -- closing page")
                    return

    def _get_participant_count(self, page, platform: str) -> int | None:
        """Try to read the participant counter from the DOM."""
        try:
            if platform == "google_meet":
                # Google Meet shows participant count in various locations
                for selector in [
                    '[data-participant-id]',
                    '[aria-label*="participant" i]',
                    '.uGOf1d',  # participant counter chip
                ]:
                    elements = page.locator(selector)
                    count = elements.count()
                    if count > 0:
                        # If it's a counter chip, read the text
                        text = elements.first.inner_text()
                        nums = re.findall(r'\d+', text)
                        if nums:
                            return int(nums[0])
                        return count

            elif platform == "teams":
                # Teams participant roster count
                for selector in [
                    '[data-tid="roster-header-count"]',
                    '[aria-label*="participant" i]',
                    '#roster-title-section',
                    '.roster-count',
                ]:
                    el = page.locator(selector).first
                    if el.count() > 0:
                        text = el.inner_text()
                        nums = re.findall(r'\d+', text)
                        if nums:
                            return int(nums[0])
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    def _is_call_ended(self, page, platform: str) -> bool:
        """Detect if the call has ended (overlay / redirect)."""
        try:
            if platform == "google_meet":
                # Google Meet shows "You left the meeting" or "The meeting has ended"
                ended = page.locator('text="You left the meeting"').first
                if ended.count() > 0:
                    return True
                ended2 = page.locator('text="The meeting has ended"').first
                if ended2.count() > 0:
                    return True

            elif platform == "teams":
                ended = page.locator('text="You left the meeting"').first
                if ended.count() > 0:
                    return True
                ended2 = page.locator('text="This meeting has ended"').first
                if ended2.count() > 0:
                    return True
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False

    def _leave_page(self, page, platform: str):
        """Click the leave/hang-up button, then close the page."""
        try:
            if platform == "google_meet":
                # Red hang-up button
                leave_btn = page.locator('[aria-label*="Leave call" i]').first
                if leave_btn.count() > 0:
                    leave_btn.click()
                    page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

            elif platform == "teams":
                leave_btn = page.locator('button[data-tid="hangup-main-btn"], #hangup-button, [data-tid="hangup-button"], [aria-label*="Leave" i], button:has-text("Leave")').first
                if leave_btn.count() > 0:
                    leave_btn.click()
                    page.wait_for_timeout(1000)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Leave button click failed: {e}")

        try:
            if not page.is_closed():
                page.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ─── External Controls ────────────────────────────────────────

    def leave_meeting(self):
        """Force-leave the currently active meeting (called by voice command)."""
        self._should_leave.set()
        try:
            if self._active_page and not self._active_page.is_closed():
                self._active_page.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        print("[MeetingBot] Force-leave requested")

    def is_in_meeting(self) -> bool:
        return self._active_page is not None and not self._active_page.is_closed()

    def get_live_captions(self) -> list:
        """Return list of captured real-time captions."""
        return list(self.live_captions)

    def get_summaries(self) -> list:
        """Load stored post-call summaries."""
        summaries_file = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "config", "meeting_summaries.json")
        if os.path.exists(summaries_file):
            try:
                import json
                with open(summaries_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return []

    def _scrape_live_captions(self, page, platform: str) -> None:
        """Extract live captions from the meeting DOM nodes."""
        if not page:
            return
        try:
            if page.is_closed():
                return
            raw_texts = []
            if platform == "google_meet":
                # Google Meet live caption selectors
                for sel in ["div[jsname='r4nke']", ".iTTPOb", "div[jscontroller='D1tHje']"]:
                    try:
                        loc = page.locator(sel)
                        cnt = loc.count()
                        for i in range(min(5, cnt)):
                            t = loc.nth(i).inner_text().strip()
                            if t:
                                raw_texts.append(t)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            elif platform == "teams":
                # Microsoft Teams closed caption selectors (avoiding historical chat messages)
                for sel in ["[data-tid='closed-captions-renderer']", "[data-tid='closed-caption-text']", "[data-tid*='caption']"]:
                    try:
                        loc = page.locator(sel)
                        cnt = loc.count()
                        for i in range(min(5, cnt)):
                            t = loc.nth(i).inner_text().strip()
                            if t:
                                raw_texts.append(t)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            for line in raw_texts:
                if line and line not in self._seen_captions and len(line) > 3:
                    self._seen_captions.add(line)
                    cap_item = {
                        "text": line,
                        "timestamp": datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
                        "platform": platform,
                    }
                    self.live_captions.append(cap_item)
                    print(f"[MeetingBot Caption] {line}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _generate_post_call_summary(self, title: str) -> Optional[dict]:
        """Summarizes meeting captions using LLM and stores in config/meeting_summaries.json."""
        if not self.live_captions:
            print("[MeetingBot] No captions captured during call.")
            return None

        transcript = "\n".join(f"[{c['timestamp']}] {c['text']}" for c in self.live_captions)
        system_prompt = (
            "You are JARVIS. Summarize the following meeting transcript. "
            "Structure your output cleanly with:\n"
            "1. Executive Summary\n"
            "2. Key Decisions Made\n"
            "3. Action Items & Next Steps\n"
        )
        prompt = f"Meeting: {title}\nTranscript:\n{transcript}"

        try:
            from core.llm_router import router
            summary_text = router.query_text("qa_chat", prompt, system_prompt=system_prompt)
        except Exception as e:
            summary_text = f"Summary generation unavailable: {e}"

        summary_record = {
            "id": f"sum-{int(time.time())}",
            "title": title,
            "date": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
            "captions_count": len(self.live_captions),
            "summary": summary_text,
        }

        # Save to config/meeting_summaries.json
        summaries_file = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "config", "meeting_summaries.json")
        existing_summaries = []
        try:
            import json
            if os.path.exists(summaries_file):
                with open(summaries_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    existing_summaries = json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            existing_summaries = []

        existing_summaries.insert(0, summary_record)
        try:
            import json
            with open(summaries_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(existing_summaries, f, indent=2)
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Saved post-call summary for '{title}'")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingBot] Error saving summary: {e}")

        return summary_record

    # ─── Interactive Setup ────────────────────────────────────────

    @staticmethod
    def interactive_setup():
        """
        Open a headed browser session for the user to manually log in
        to Google and Microsoft accounts. The session is saved to the
        persistent profile so future bot runs don't need re-authentication.
        """
        if not PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE:
            print("ERROR: Playwright is not installed.")
            print("Run: pip install playwright && python -m playwright install chromium")
            return

        print("=" * 60)
        print("Meeting Bot — One-Time Account Setup")
        print("=" * 60)
        print()
        print("A browser window will open. Please log in to:")
        print("  1. Your Google account (for Google Meet)")
        print("  2. Your Microsoft account (for Teams)")
        print()
        print("Once logged in, close the browser window to save the session.")
        print()

        os.makedirs(BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR, exist_ok=True)

        pw = sync_playwright().start()
        try:
            ctx = pw.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                user_data_dir=BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR,
                headless=False,
                args=[
                    "--no-sandbox",
                    "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
                    "--disable-infobars",
                ],
                ignore_default_args=["--enable-automation"],
                channel="chrome",  # use locally installed Chrome
                viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 900},
            )
            try:
                # Open helpful tabs
                page = ctx.pages[0] if ctx.pages else ctx.new_page()
                page.goto("https://accounts.google.com")
                teams_page = ctx.new_page()
                teams_page.goto("https://login.microsoftonline.com")

                print("Waiting for you to finish logging in... (close the browser when done)")

                try:
                    # Block until the user closes the browser
                    page.wait_for_event("close", timeout=0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            finally:
                try:
                    ctx.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        finally:
            try:
                pw.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass

        print()
        print("✓ Session saved! The meeting bot will use these credentials automatically.")
        print()


# ─── CLI Entry Point ──────────────────────────────────────────────

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--setup" in sys.argv:
        MeetingBot.interactive_setup()
    else:
        print("Usage:")
        print("  python -m automation.meeting_bot --setup   # One-time login setup")

<file path="automation/meeting_handler.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Meeting Handler
Top-level JARVIS handler that bridges the meeting bot, scheduler,
and dashboard interface into the core command dispatch system.

Follows the same pattern as AlarmHandler, WeatherHandler, etc.:
  - __init__(self, jarvis) stores the reference
  - Public methods called from core.py's process_command / _execute_ai_command
"""

import re
import threading
import webbrowser
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# Import meeting sub-modules
try:
    from automation.meeting_scheduler import MeetingScheduler
    from automation.meeting_bot import MeetingBot, PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE
    from automation.schedule_interface import ScheduleInterface, FASTAPI_AVAILABLE
except ImportError:
    from modules.meeting_scheduler import MeetingScheduler
    from modules.meeting_bot import MeetingBot, PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE
    from modules.schedule_interface import ScheduleInterface, FASTAPI_AVAILABLE


class MeetingHandler:
    """Handles meeting-related voice commands and manages all meeting subsystems."""

    def __init__(self, jarvis):
        self.jarvis = jarvis

        # ── Subsystems ────────────────────────────
        self.bot = MeetingBot() if PLAYWRIGHT_AVAILABLE else None
        self.scheduler = MeetingScheduler(meeting_bot=self.bot)
        self.dashboard = ScheduleInterface(
            scheduler=self.scheduler,
            meeting_bot=self.bot,
        )

        # Start the web dashboard only if explicitly requested by legacy caller
        if FASTAPI_AVAILABLE and getattr(self.jarvis, "start_legacy_dashboard", False):
            self.dashboard.start()

        print("[OK] Meeting attendant module ready")

    # ─── Voice Commands ───────────────────────────────────────────

    def schedule_meeting(self, command: str):
        """
        Parse a voice/chat scheduling command and add the meeting.
        Supports recurrence (daily till <date>), duration, and AI fallback parsing.
        e.g. "schedule Team Standup at 2:30 PM with link https://meet.google.com/abc"
        """
        result = self.scheduler.parse_schedule_command(command)
        if not result:
            # AI Fallback via core.llm_router.router
            try:
                from core.llm_router import router
                sys_prompt = (
                    "Extract meeting schedule details from the user command as JSON with keys:\n"
                    "- title: string\n"
                    "- meeting_url: string\n"
                    "- start_time_iso: string (ISO 8601 local timestamp YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS)\n"
                    "- duration_minutes: int (default 60)\n"
                    "- recurrence_days: int (default 1)\n"
                    f"Current time is {datetime.now().isoformat()}."
                )
                ai_data = router.query_json("intent_router", command, system_prompt=sys_prompt)
                if ai_data and ai_data.get("meeting_url") and ai_data.get("start_time_iso"):
                    st = datetime.fromisoformat(ai_data["start_time_iso"])
                    dur = int(ai_data.get("duration_minutes", 60))
                    et = st + timedelta(minutes=dur)
                    rec = int(ai_data.get("recurrence_days", 1))
                    result = {
                        "title": ai_data.get("title", "Online Meeting"),
                        "meeting_url": ai_data["meeting_url"],
                        "start_time": st,
                        "end_time": et,
                        "duration_minutes": dur,
                        "recurrence_days": rec,
                    }
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[MeetingHandler] AI fallback schedule parsing error: {e}")

        if result:
            rec_days = result.get("recurrence_days", 1)
            start_base = result["start_time"]
            end_base = result.get("end_time") or (start_base + timedelta(hours=1))
            time_str = start_base.strftime("%I:%M %p")

            if rec_days > 1:
                for d in range(rec_days):
                    st = start_base + timedelta(days=d)
                    et = end_base + timedelta(days=d)
                    self.scheduler.add_meeting(
                        title=result["title"],
                        meeting_url=result["meeting_url"],
                        start_time=st,
                        end_time=et,
                    )
                self.jarvis.speak(
                    f"Scheduled '{result['title']}' daily for {rec_days} days starting at {time_str}, sir."
                )
            else:
                meeting = self.scheduler.add_meeting(
                    title=result["title"],
                    meeting_url=result["meeting_url"],
                    start_time=start_base,
                    end_time=end_base,
                )
                self.jarvis.speak(
                    f"Meeting '{meeting['title']}' scheduled for {time_str}, sir. "
                    f"I'll join automatically 60 seconds beforehand."
                )
        else:
            self.jarvis.speak(
                "I couldn't parse that schedule command, sir. "
                "Please include a meeting URL and a time. For example: "
                "'Schedule standup at 2:30 PM with link https://meet.google.com/abc'."
            )

    def list_today_meetings(self):
        """Read out today's meetings."""
        meetings = self.scheduler.get_today()
        if not meetings:
            self.jarvis.speak("You have no meetings scheduled for today, sir.")
            return

        pending = [m for m in meetings if m["status"] == "pending"]
        active = [m for m in meetings if m["status"] == "active"]

        parts = []
        if active:
            parts.append(f"You are currently in {len(active)} active meeting{'s' if len(active) > 1 else ''}.")
        if pending:
            parts.append(f"You have {len(pending)} upcoming meeting{'s' if len(pending) > 1 else ''}:")
            for m in sorted(pending, key=lambda x: x["start_time"]):
                t = datetime.fromisoformat(m["start_time"]).strftime("%I:%M %p")
                parts.append(f"  {m['title']} at {t}.")
        if not pending and not active:
            completed = len([m for m in meetings if m["status"] == "completed"])
            parts.append(f"All {completed} meeting{'s' if completed > 1 else ''} for today are completed, sir.")

        self.jarvis.speak(" ".join(parts))

    def cancel_next_meeting(self):
        """Cancel the next pending meeting."""
        cancelled = self.scheduler.cancel_next_pending()
        if cancelled:
            t = datetime.fromisoformat(cancelled["start_time"]).strftime("%I:%M %p")
            self.jarvis.speak(f"Cancelled '{cancelled['title']}' at {t}, sir.")
        else:
            self.jarvis.speak("There are no upcoming meetings to cancel, sir.")

    def leave_meeting(self):
        """Force-leave the currently active meeting."""
        if self.bot and self.bot.is_in_meeting():
            self.bot.leave_meeting()
            # Mark active meetings as completed
            for m in self.scheduler.get_active():
                self.scheduler.mark_completed(m["id"])
            self.jarvis.speak("Leaving the meeting, sir.")
        else:
            self.jarvis.speak("You're not in any active meeting, sir.")

    def join_next_meeting(self):
        """Immediately join the next pending meeting."""
        pending = self.scheduler.get_pending()
        if not pending:
            self.jarvis.speak("No pending meetings to join, sir.")
            return

        meeting = pending[0]
        self.jarvis.speak(f"Joining '{meeting['title']}' now, sir.")
        self.scheduler.mark_active(meeting["id"])

        if self.bot:
            end_time = datetime.fromisoformat(meeting["end_time"])
            threading.Thread(
                target=self._run_join,
                args=(meeting, end_time),
                daemon=True,
            ).start()
        else:
            # Fallback: open in default browser
            webbrowser.open(meeting["meeting_url"])
            self.jarvis.speak("Opened the meeting link in your browser, sir.")

    def _run_join(self, meeting, end_time):
        """Thread target for joining a meeting via the bot."""
        try:
            self.bot.join_meeting(
                url=meeting["meeting_url"],
                end_time=end_time,
                auto_leave=meeting.get("auto_leave", True),
                title=meeting.get("title", "Online Meeting"),
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MeetingHandler] Join error: {e}")
        finally:
            self.scheduler.mark_completed(meeting["id"])

    def open_dashboard(self):
        """Open the schedule dashboard in the default browser."""
        if FASTAPI_AVAILABLE:
            import os
            port = int(os.getenv("MEETING_DASHBOARD_PORT", "5050"))
            url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
            webbrowser.open(url)
            self.jarvis.speak("Opening the meeting dashboard, sir.")
        else:
            self.jarvis.speak("The meeting dashboard is not available, sir. Please install FastAPI and uvicorn.")

    # ─── Command Router ───────────────────────────────────────────

    def process_meeting_command(self, command: str):
        """
        Route a meeting-related voice command to the appropriate handler.
        Called from core.py when a meeting intent is detected.
        """
        cmd = command.lower()

        # Leave meeting
        if any(phrase in cmd for phrase in ['leave the meeting', 'leave meeting', 'exit meeting', 'hang up', 'end the call']):
            self.leave_meeting()
            return

        # Cancel meeting
        if any(phrase in cmd for phrase in ['cancel my next meeting', 'cancel next meeting', 'cancel meeting']):
            self.cancel_next_meeting()
            return

        # List meetings
        if any(phrase in cmd for phrase in [
            'what meetings', 'my meetings', 'today\'s meetings',
            'meetings today', 'meeting schedule', 'show meetings',
            'list meetings', 'upcoming meetings',
        ]):
            self.list_today_meetings()
            return

        # Open dashboard
        if any(phrase in cmd for phrase in ['open schedule', 'open dashboard', 'meeting dashboard', 'schedule dashboard']):
            self.open_dashboard()
            return

        # Join next meeting
        if any(phrase in cmd for phrase in ['join meeting', 'join the meeting', 'join next meeting', 'join now']):
            self.join_next_meeting()
            return

        # Schedule a meeting (contains a URL)
        if re.search(r'https?://(?:meet\.google\.com|teams\.microsoft\.com|teams\.live\.com)', cmd):
            self.schedule_meeting(command)
            return

        # Fallback: try to parse as schedule command
        if any(word in cmd for word in ['schedule', 'add meeting', 'book meeting', 'create meeting']):
            self.schedule_meeting(command)
            return

        self.jarvis.speak("I didn't understand that meeting command, sir. You can ask me to schedule, list, join, or cancel meetings.")

    # ─── Shutdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def shutdown(self):
        """Clean up all meeting subsystems."""
        self.scheduler.shutdown()
        if self.bot:
            self.bot.close()
</file>

__all__ = ["engine", "tts_engine", "voice_listener", "llm_router", "context_manager"]
