<file path="core/engine.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
JARVIS Core Engine & Unified Input Gateway
Orchestrates hybrid voice + text command routing across fast deterministic local handlers,
skill macros, meeting automation, and 3-tier autonomous LLM dispatch.
"""

import json
import os
import random
import re
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

# Settings & Vault
from config.settings import (
    BASE_DIR,
    SKILLS_FILE,
    get_active_key,
    load_vault,
    is_internet_connected,
)

# Audio State Machine
from audio.tts_engine import TTSEngine, tts_engine as default_tts
from audio.voice_listener import VoiceListener, voice_listener as default_listener

# Core Adapters & Router
from core.adapters.base import ChatMessage
from core.context_manager import ContextManager, context_mgr
from core.llm_router import LLMRouter, router as default_router

# Local Handlers
from integrations.handlers.date_time import DateTimeHandler
from integrations.handlers.applications import ApplicationHandler
from integrations.handlers.music import MusicHandler
from integrations.handlers.web_search import WebSearchHandler
from integrations.handlers.alarm import AlarmHandler
from integrations.handlers.weather import WeatherHandler
from integrations.handlers.system_controls import SystemControls
from integrations.handlers.system_info import SystemInfoHandler
from automation.meeting_handler import MeetingHandler
from integrations.handlers.qa_handler import QAHandler
from integrations.skill_store import skill_store

VALID_INTENTS = {
    "general_qa",
    "workspace_rag",
    "tier1_code",
    "tier2_mcp",
    "tier3_vision_gui",
    "web_research",
}


class JARVISEngine:
    """
    Central JARVIS Assistant Orchestrator.
    Implements the Unified InputGateway pattern processing typed text and voice identically.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        tts: Optional[TTSEngine] = None,
        listener: Optional[VoiceListener] = None,
        llm_router: Optional[LLMRouter] = None,
        context_manager: Optional[ContextManager] = None,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self.tts = tts or kwargs.get("tts_engine") or default_tts
        self.listener = listener or kwargs.get("voice_listener") or default_listener
        self.router = llm_router or default_router
        self.context = context_manager or context_mgr

        # Backward compatibility aliases & state flags
        self.running = True
        self.is_thinking: bool = False
        self.tts_engine = self.tts
        self.voice_listener = self.listener

        # Thread synchronization
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._chat_listeners: List[Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]] = []

        # Handlers
        self.datetime_handler = DateTimeHandler(self)
        self.app_handler = ApplicationHandler(self)
        self.music_handler = MusicHandler(self)
        self.web_handler = WebSearchHandler(self)
        self.alarm_handler = AlarmHandler(self)
        self.weather_handler = WeatherHandler(self)
        self.system_controls = SystemControls(self)
        self.system_info = SystemInfoHandler(self)
        self.meeting_handler = MeetingHandler(self)
        self.qa_handler = QAHandler(self)

        # Temporary response capture buffer during handler execution
        self._captured_speech: List[str] = []

        # Wire voice listener callback into unified InputGateway
        if self.listener:
            self.listener.on_command = self._on_voice_input

        # Iron Man flair acknowledgments
        self.ack_phrases = [
            "Right away, sir.",
            "Consider it done, sir.",
            "Working on it, sir.",
            "As you wish, sir.",
            "At once, sir.",
            "Right on it, sir.",
        ]

    # ── Listener Registration for WebSockets ──────────────────────────
    def register_chat_listener(self, callback: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], None]) -> None:
        """Register a callback for CHAT_MESSAGE events."""
        with self._lock:
            if callback not in self._chat_listeners:
                self._chat_listeners.append(callback)

    def _broadcast_chat_message(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Broadcast chat events to all registered frontend listeners."""
        with self._lock:
            listeners = list(self._chat_listeners)
        for cb in listeners:
            try:
                cb(message)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Engine] Chat broadcast error: {e}")

    # ── Audio State Convenience Methods ───────────────────────────────
    def set_mic_active(self, active: bool) -> None:
        if self.listener:
            self.listener.set_mic_active(active)

    def get_mic_active(self) -> bool:
        return self.listener.get_mic_active() if self.listener else False

    def toggle_mic(self) -> bool:
        return self.listener.toggle_mic() if self.listener else False

    def set_tts_active(self, active: bool) -> None:
        if self.tts:
            self.tts.set_tts_active(active)

    def get_tts_active(self) -> bool:
        return self.tts.get_tts_active() if self.tts else False

    def toggle_tts(self) -> bool:
        return self.tts.toggle_tts() if self.tts else False

    # ── Speech Output Capture ─────────────────────────────────────────
    def speak(self, text: str, wait: bool = False) -> None:
        """
        Synthesizes text through TTSEngine and logs to response buffer.
        Respects tts_active toggle (silent mode if False).
        """
        if not text or not text.strip():
            return
        cleaned = text.strip()
        self._captured_speech.append(cleaned)
        if self.tts:
            self.tts.speak(cleaned, wait=wait)

    def _on_voice_input(self, spoken_text: str) -> None:
        """Internal callback invoked when voice_listener transcribes a command."""
        self.process_command(query=spoken_text, source="voice")

    # ── Unified Input Gateway ─────────────────────────────────────────
    def _broadcast_audio_state(self) -> None:
        """Emit an immediate AUDIO_STATE broadcast so the HUD reacts instantly."""
        try:
            status = (
                "thinking" if self.is_thinking
                else ("speaking" if self.tts.is_speaking()
                      else ("listening" if self.get_mic_active() else "idle"))
            )
            payload = {
                "mic_active": self.get_mic_active(),
                "tts_active": self.get_tts_active(),
                "status": status,
                "meeting_auto_muted": getattr(self.listener, "meeting_auto_muted", False),
            }
            for cb in list(self._chat_listeners):
                try:
                    # Re-use chat listeners is not right — use the server broadcast directly
                    pass
                except Exception:
                    pass
            # Import lazily to avoid circular deps; server.broadcast_ws_sync handles it
            try:
                from automation.server import broadcast_ws_sync
                broadcast_ws_sync("AUDIO_STATE", payload)
            except Exception:
                pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    def process_command(self, query: str, source: str = "text") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Unified InputGateway method processing both typed text and spoken voice commands identically.
        Triage:
        1. Fast deterministic local handlers (time, date, volume, brightness, battery, alarms, apps, skills, etc.)
        2. LLM Intent Router (general_qa, workspace_rag, tier1_code, tier2_mcp, tier3_vision_gui, web_research)
        """
        if not query or not query.strip():
            return self._build_response("", source=source, action="none")

        clean_query = query.strip()
        timestamp = datetime.now().isoformat()
        msg_id = f"msg-{int(time.time() * 1000)}"

        # Broadcast incoming user message
        user_event = {
            "id": f"user-{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
            "sender": "user",
            "source": source,
            "text": clean_query,
            "timestamp": timestamp,
            "action": "input",
            "data": {},
        }
        self._broadcast_chat_message(user_event)

        # Set thinking state
        self.is_thinking = True
        self._broadcast_audio_state()

        try:
            return self._process_command_inner(clean_query, source)
        finally:
            self.is_thinking = False
            self._broadcast_audio_state()

    def _process_command_inner(self, clean_query: str, source: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Inner command processing logic, wrapped by process_command for is_thinking management."""
        # Clear captured speech buffer
        self._captured_speech.clear()

        # Teach Mode recording
        if skill_store.is_teaching() and source != "macro":
            stop_phrases = ["stop teaching", "stop recording", "end teach", "save routine", "cancel teach"]
            if not any(sp in clean_query.lower() for sp in stop_phrases):
                skill_store.record_step(clean_query)

        # Step 1: Check saved skill macros in skills.json
        skill_response = self._check_skills_macro(clean_query)
        if skill_response:
            return self._finalize_response(skill_response, source, action="skill_macro")

        # Step 2: Try fast deterministic handlers
        handled, response_text, action_type, extra_data = self._try_deterministic_handlers(clean_query)
        if handled:
            # If handler invoked self.speak, captured speech has priority
            if self._captured_speech:
                response_text = " ".join(self._captured_speech)
            return self._finalize_response(response_text, source, action=action_type, data=extra_data)

        # Step 3: LLM Intent Classification & Dynamic Routing
        llm_response = self._triage_llm(clean_query)
        return self._finalize_response(
            llm_response.get("text", ""),
            source,
            action=llm_response.get("action", "general_qa"),
            data=llm_response.get("data", {}),
        )

    def _finalize_response(
        self,
        text: str,
        source: str,
        action: str = "none",
        data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Formats and broadcasts final assistant response."""
        # Sanitize action if malformed or arbitrary string
        clean_action = action
        valid_deterministic = (
            action.startswith("get_")
            or action.startswith("set_")
            or action in (
                "skill_macro",
                "greet",
                "clear_memory",
                "system_info",
                "open_app",
                "play_music",
                "clipboard_copilot",
                "meeting_command",
                "none",
            )
        )
        if clean_action not in VALID_INTENTS and not valid_deterministic:
            clean_action = "general_qa"

        resp = self._build_response(text, source=source, action=clean_action, data=data or {})
        # Speak text if not already spoken during handler
        if not self._captured_speech and text:
            self.speak(text)
        self._broadcast_chat_message(resp)
        return resp

    def _build_response(
        self,
        text: str,
        source: str = "text",
        action: str = "none",
        data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Construct standard response dictionary contract."""
        return {
            "id": f"jarvis-{int(time.time() * 1000)}",
            "sender": "jarvis",
            "source": source,
            "text": text,
            "response": text,  # Backward compatibility alias
            "status": "ok",    # Backward compatibility alias
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "action": action,
            "data": data or {},
        }

    def is_online(self) -> bool:
        """Check if internet connectivity is available."""
        return is_internet_connected()

    # Backward compatibility alias
    execute_command = process_command

    # ── Fast Deterministic Local Handlers ─────────────────────────────
    def _try_deterministic_handlers(self, query: str) -> Tuple[bool, str, str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Evaluates regex rules for local zero-latency handlers.
        Returns: (handled: bool, response: str, action: str, data: dict)
        """
        q = query.lower().strip()

        # 0. Clear conversation memory
        if q in ("clear memory", "reset conversation", "clear chat", "clear context"):
            self.context.clear()
            return True, "Conversation memory cleared, sir.", "clear_memory", {}

        # 1. Time
        if re.search(r"\b(what time|time is it|current time|tell me the time)\b", q):
            now_str = datetime.now().strftime("%I:%M %p")
            return True, f"The current time is {now_str}, sir.", "get_time", {"time": now_str}

        # 2. Date, Year & Month
        if re.search(r"\b(what year|current year|which year is it|year is it now)\b", q):
            return True, f"The current year is {datetime.now().year}, sir.", "get_date", {"year": datetime.now().year}

        if re.search(r"\b(what month|current month|which month is it)\b", q):
            return True, f"It is currently {datetime.now().strftime('%B %Y')}, sir.", "get_date", {}

        if re.search(r"\b(what('s| is)? (the )?date|what date|what day|today's date|current date|date today)\b", q):
            date_str = datetime.now().strftime("%A, %B %d, %Y")
            return True, f"Today is {date_str}, sir.", "get_date", {"date": date_str}

        # 2.5 Greeting
        if re.search(r"\b(hello|hey jarvis|hi jarvis|greetings|good morning|good afternoon|good evening)\b", q) or q in ("hi", "hello"):
            return True, "Greetings, sir. How may I assist you today?", "greet", {}

        # 3. System Diagnostics (Battery / CPU / RAM / Status)
        if re.search(r"\b(battery|cpu|system status|system diagnostics|diagnostics|specs|ram usage|how is the system)\b", q):
            try:
                import psutil
                cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=None)
                ram = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
                battery = psutil.sensors_battery()
                batt_info = f"Battery at {int(battery.percent)}%." if battery else "No battery detected."
                status_text = f"Systems nominal, sir. CPU load is at {cpu}%, RAM utilization is {ram}%. {batt_info}"
                return True, status_text, "system_info", {
                    "cpu": cpu,
                    "ram": ram,
                    "battery": int(battery.percent) if battery else None,
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return True, "All primary system monitors active.", "system_info", {"error": str(e)}

        # 4. Volume Controls
        if re.search(r"\b(mute volume|mute sound|mute audio)\b", q):
            try:
                self.system_controls.mute()
                return True, "Audio muted, sir.", "set_volume", {"volume": 0, "muted": True}
            except Exception:
                return True, "Audio muted.", "set_volume", {}

        if re.search(r"\b(unmute volume|unmute sound|unmute audio)\b", q):
            try:
                self.system_controls.unmute()
                return True, "Audio restored, sir.", "set_volume", {"muted": False}
            except Exception:
                return True, "Audio restored.", "set_volume", {}

        vol_match = re.search(r"\b(set volume to|volume to|set volume)\s+(\d+)\b", q)
        if vol_match:
            val = int(vol_match.group(2))
            try:
                self.system_controls.set_volume(str(val))
                return True, f"Volume adjusted to {val} percent, sir.", "set_volume", {"volume": val}
            except Exception:
                return True, f"Volume set to {val}%.", "set_volume", {"volume": val}

        # 5. Brightness Controls
        bright_match = re.search(r"\b(set brightness to|brightness to|set brightness)\s+(\d+)\b", q)
        if bright_match:
            val = int(bright_match.group(2))
            try:
                self.system_controls.set_brightness(str(val))
                return True, f"Display brightness set to {val} percent, sir.", "set_brightness", {"brightness": val}
            except Exception:
                return True, f"Brightness adjusted to {val}%.", "set_brightness", {"brightness": val}

        # 6. Alarms & Countdown Timers
        alarm_match = re.search(r"\bset alarm for\s+(.+)", q)
        if alarm_match:
            target = alarm_match.group(1).strip()
            res = self.alarm_handler.set_alarm(f"set alarm for {target}")
            return True, res or f"Alarm set for {target}, sir.", "set_alarm", {"time": target}

        timer_match = re.search(r"\bset timer for\s+(.+)", q)
        if timer_match:
            target = timer_match.group(1).strip()
            self.system_controls.set_timer(target)
            return True, f"Countdown timer initiated for {target}, sir.", "set_timer", {"duration": target}

        # 7. Weather
        if re.search(r"\b(weather|temperature|forecast|is it raining|how hot is it)\b", q):
            try:
                res = self.weather_handler.process_weather_command(query)
                return True, res or "Weather telemetry retrieved.", "get_weather", {}
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 8. Open Applications
        app_match = re.search(r"\b(open|launch|start)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\s]+)\b", q)
        if app_match and not any(k in q for k in ["mode", "meeting", "timer", "alarm", "file"]):
            app_name = app_match.group(2).strip()
            try:
                self.app_handler.open_application(f"open {app_name}")
                return True, f"Launching {app_name}, sir.", "open_app", {"app": app_name}
            except Exception:
                return True, f"Attempting to launch {app_name}.", "open_app", {"app": app_name}

        # 9. Music / Media
        if q.startswith("play ") or "play music" in q or "pause music" in q or "stop music" in q:
            try:
                self.music_handler.play_music(query)
                return True, f"Media playback updated, sir.", "play_music", {}
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 10. Clipboard Copilot Voice/Text Triggers
        if re.search(r"\b(summarize clipboard|summarize this|fix this code|explain this|explain selection|translate selection)\b", q):
            try:
                from integrations.handlers.clipboard_copilot import clipboard_copilot
                res = clipboard_copilot.act_on_clipboard(q)
                return True, res, "clipboard_copilot", {}
            except Exception as e:
                return True, f"Clipboard processing error: {e}", "clipboard_copilot", {}

        # 11. Meeting Commands & URLs
        meeting_phrases = [
            "schedule meeting", "add meeting", "what meetings", "my meetings",
            "list meetings", "upcoming meetings", "cancel meeting", "cancel next meeting",
            "join meeting", "leave meeting", "end the call",
        ]
        meeting_domains = ["meet.google.com", "teams.microsoft.com", "teams.live.com"]
        if any(p in q for p in meeting_phrases) or any(d in q for d in meeting_domains):
            self.meeting_handler.process_meeting_command(query)
            return True, "Meeting command processed, sir.", "meeting_command", {}

        return False, "", "none", {}

    # ── Check Skill Macros ────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _check_skills_macro(self, query: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Checks if the query matches a saved routine in config/skills.json."""
        q = query.lower().strip()
        if not SKILLS_FILE.exists():
            return None

        try:
            skills = json.loads(SKILLS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            for s in skills:
                triggers = s.get("trigger_phrases", [])
                if any(t.lower() == q or t.lower() in q for t in triggers):
                    steps = s.get("steps", [])
                    print(f"[SkillMacro] Triggered skill: '{s.get('name')}' ({len(steps)} steps)")
                    results = []
                    for step in steps:
                        res = self.process_command(step, source="macro")
                        if res.get("text"):
                            results.append(res["text"])
                    summary = f"Routine '{s.get('name')}' executed. " + " ".join(results)
                    return summary
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[SkillMacro] Error reading skills: {e}")

        return None

    # ── LLM Dynamic Triage & Classification ───────────────────────────
    def _triage_llm(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Fast intent triage and direct single-call LLM response gateway.
        - Fast keyword/pattern routing for live web, code, vision, and RAG.
        - Direct single-call generation for general QA with automatic web search fallback on cutoff/refusal.
        - NEVER allows invalid strings to leak into action.
        """
        q_lower = query.lower().strip()

        # 1. Live Web / Current Events Detection
        web_pattern = r"\b(2025|2026|this year|latest|most heard|most streamed|top song|who won|current price|news about|search web|look up online|on spotify)\b"
        if re.search(web_pattern, q_lower):
            try:
                from automation.web_agent import web_agent
                result = web_agent.research(query)
                summary = result.get("summary") or "Web research completed, sir."
                self.context.add_user_message(query)
                self.context.add_assistant_message(summary)
                return {
                    "text": summary,
                    "action": "web_research",
                    "data": result,
                }
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Engine] Fast web research error: {e}")

        # 2. Explicit OS / Code / GUI / RAG Triggers
        code_pattern = r"\b(write a python script|run a script|create a file|zip folder|in my downloads|in my desktop)\b"
        if re.search(code_pattern, q_lower):
            try:
                from agent_os.code_executor import code_executor
                result = code_executor.execute_task(query)
                return {
                    "text": result.get("summary", "Code execution complete, sir."),
                    "action": "tier1_code",
                    "data": result,
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return {"text": f"Code execution notice: {e}", "action": "tier1_code", "data": {}}

        gui_pattern = r"\b(click on|type into|on my screen|visually operate)\b"
        if re.search(gui_pattern, q_lower):
            try:
                from agent_os.state_machine import os_agent
                result = os_agent.run_task(query)
                return {
                    "text": result.get("summary", "GUI operation finished, sir."),
                    "action": "tier3_vision_gui",
                    "data": result,
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return {"text": f"Vision agent notice: {e}", "action": "tier3_vision_gui", "data": {}}

        rag_pattern = r"\b(in my workspace|in my project files|search my documents)\b"
        if re.search(rag_pattern, q_lower):
            try:
                from integrations.workspace_rag import workspace_rag
                result = workspace_rag.query(query)
                return {
                    "text": result.get("answer", "Workspace search completed, sir."),
                    "action": "workspace_rag",
                    "data": result,
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return {"text": f"Workspace RAG notice: {e}", "action": "workspace_rag", "data": {}}

        # Check MCP trigger
        mcp_pattern = r"\b(mcp tool|use tool|call tool|list tools)\b"
        if re.search(mcp_pattern, q_lower):
            try:
                from integrations.mcp_client import mcp_client
                tools = mcp_client.list_tools()
                if tools:
                    tool_prompt = (
                        "You are the MCP tool selector for JARVIS 2.0. Analyze the user request and select the "
                        "appropriate server_name, tool_name, and arguments from the available tools list.\n"
                        f"Available tools: {json.dumps(tools)}\n"
                        "Return valid JSON:\n"
                        "{\n"
                        '  "server_name": "<server name>",\n'
                        '  "tool_name": "<tool name>",\n'
                        '  "arguments": {<extracted tool arguments>}\n'
                        "}"
                    )
                    decision = self.router.query_json("intent_router", query, system_prompt=tool_prompt)
                    if decision and isinstance(decision, dict):
                        s_name = decision.get("server_name")
                        t_name = decision.get("tool_name")
                        t_args = decision.get("arguments", {})
                        if s_name and t_name:
                            res = mcp_client.call_tool(s_name, t_name, t_args)
                            res_summary = res.get("output") or res.get("result") or ("Success" if res.get("ok") else res.get("error"))
                            return {
                                "text": f"MCP tool '{t_name}' executed on server '{s_name}'. Result: {res_summary}",
                                "action": "tier2_mcp",
                                "data": res,
                            }
            except Exception as e:
                return {"text": f"MCP execution notice: {e}", "action": "tier2_mcp", "data": {}}

        # 3. Direct single LLM call for all general questions & chat
        self.context.add_user_message(query)
        ctx_messages = self.context.get_messages()
        resp = self.router.dispatch_role("qa_chat", ctx_messages)
        text_out = (resp.text or "").strip() if resp else ""

        refusal_signatures = [
            "cannot predict the future",
            "can't predict the future",
            "not yet known",
            "cutoff",
            "knowledge cutoff",
            "training data ends",
            "as an ai",
            "i cannot provide real-time",
            "do not have access to real-time",
            "don't have access to real-time",
            "i do not have real-time",
            "i don't have real-time",
        ]
        placeholder_signatures = [
            "let me check that for you",
            "let me check",
            "i can help with that",
            "right away, sir",
            "working on it, sir",
            "as you wish, sir",
            "at once, sir",
        ]

        needs_web_fallback = False
        if not text_out:
            needs_web_fallback = True
        elif any(sig in text_out.lower() for sig in refusal_signatures):
            needs_web_fallback = True
        elif len(text_out) < 40 and any(ps in text_out.lower() for ps in placeholder_signatures):
            needs_web_fallback = True

        if needs_web_fallback:
            try:
                from automation.web_agent import web_agent
                result = web_agent.research(query)
                web_summary = result.get("summary")
                if web_summary and web_summary.strip():
                    self.context.add_assistant_message(web_summary)
                    return {
                        "text": web_summary,
                        "action": "web_research",
                        "data": result,
                    }
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Engine] Web agent fallback notice: {e}")

        final_text = text_out or "I am listening, sir. How may I assist you?"
        self.context.add_assistant_message(final_text)
        return {
            "text": final_text,
            "action": "general_qa",
            "data": {},
        }


# Singleton engine instance
engine = JARVISEngine()

<file path="scripts/create_desktop_icon.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Desktop Shortcut & Geometric Arc-Cyan HUD Icon Generator for JARVIS 2.0
Programmatically generates the Marvel HUD icon (jarvis.png and jarvis.ico)
using Pillow supersampling, and creates native clickable desktop launchers.
"""

import math
import os
import sys
import platform
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

# Paths
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
MAIN_PY = BASE_DIR / "main.py"
ASSETS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "ui" / "assets"
ICON_ICO = ASSETS_DIR / "jarvis.ico"
ICON_PNG = ASSETS_DIR / "jarvis.png"


def generate_geometric_hud_icon(ico_path: Path, png_path: Path) -> None:
    """
    Programmatically creates an Arc-Cyan Holographic Reactor HUD icon
    with concentric segmented arcs, tick-marks, and central energy core.
    Draws on a 1024x1024 supersampled canvas and downsamples for crisp edges.
    """
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

    os.makedirs(ico_path.parent, exist_ok=True)
    canvas_size = 1024
    center = canvas_size // 2
    img = Image.new("RGBA", (canvas_size, canvas_size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # 1. Dark Chamfered Background Hexagon / Circle Badge
    badge_radius = 490
    bg_color = (6, 10, 18, 250)  # #060A12 deep space dark
    draw.ellipse(
        [center - badge_radius, center - badge_radius, center + badge_radius, center + badge_radius],
        fill=bg_color,
        outline=(0, 229, 255, 120),  # Subtle outer cyan border
        width=4,
    )

    # 2. Outer Segmented Arc Ring (Cyan #00E5FF)
    arc_radius = 430
    arc_width = 16
    cyan = (0, 229, 255, 255)
    cyan_dim = (0, 229, 255, 100)
    gold = (255, 179, 0, 255)  # Reactor Gold #FFB300

    # Draw 4 segmented arcs
    for start_deg, end_deg in [(15, 75), (105, 165), (195, 255), (285, 345)]:
        draw.arc(
            [center - arc_radius, center - arc_radius, center + arc_radius, center + arc_radius],
            start=start_deg,
            end=end_deg,
            fill=cyan,
            width=arc_width,
        )

    # 3. Intermediate Dashed Sensor Ring
    mid_radius = 360
    draw.ellipse(
        [center - mid_radius, center - mid_radius, center + mid_radius, center + mid_radius],
        outline=cyan_dim,
        width=3,
    )

    # 12 Radial telemetry tick marks
    for i in range(12):
        angle = math.radians(i * 30)
        r_inner = mid_radius - (22 if i % 3 == 0 else 12)
        r_outer = mid_radius + (22 if i % 3 == 0 else 12)
        x1 = center + r_inner * math.cos(angle)
        y1 = center + r_inner * math.sin(angle)
        x2 = center + r_outer * math.cos(angle)
        y2 = center + r_outer * math.sin(angle)
        tick_color = gold if i % 3 == 0 else cyan
        draw.line([(x1, y1), (x2, y2)], fill=tick_color, width=5 if i % 3 == 0 else 3)

    # 4. Concentric Inner HUD Ring
    inner_radius = 270
    draw.ellipse(
        [center - inner_radius, center - inner_radius, center + inner_radius, center + inner_radius],
        outline=cyan,
        width=8,
    )

    # 5. Hexagonal Reactor Frame
    hex_radius = 210
    hex_points = []
    for i in range(6):
        angle = math.radians(i * 60 - 30)
        hx = center + hex_radius * math.cos(angle)
        hy = center + hex_radius * math.sin(angle)
        hex_points.append((hx, hy))
    draw.polygon(hex_points, outline=cyan, fill=(0, 229, 255, 30))

    # 6. Central Arc Reactor Core (Glowing Central Node)
    core_radius = 110
    draw.ellipse(
        [center - core_radius, center - core_radius, center + core_radius, center + core_radius],
        fill=(0, 229, 255, 180),
        outline=gold,
        width=6,
    )

    # Inner bright hot-spot
    hot_radius = 55
    draw.ellipse(
        [center - hot_radius, center - hot_radius, center + hot_radius, center + hot_radius],
        fill=(255, 255, 255, 240),
        outline=cyan,
        width=4,
    )

    # 3 Inverted Reactor Blades inside core
    for i in range(3):
        angle = math.radians(i * 120 - 90)
        bx = center + (core_radius - 10) * math.cos(angle)
        by = center + (core_radius - 10) * math.sin(angle)
        draw.line([(center, center), (bx, by)], fill=gold, width=5)

    # 7. Supersampled High-Quality Export
    png_256 = img.resize((256, 256), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
    png_256.save(png_path, format="PNG")
    print(f"[OK] Generated Arc-Cyan PNG Icon: {png_path}")

    # Generate Multi-Resolution ICO
    ico_sizes = [(16, 16), (32, 32), (48, 48), (64, 64), (128, 128), (256, 256)]
    img.save(ico_path, format="ICO", sizes=ico_sizes)
    print(f"[OK] Generated Arc-Cyan Windows ICO: {ico_path}")


def get_desktop_dir() -> Path:
    """Resolve the user's Desktop directory cross-platform."""
    home = Path(os.path.expanduser("~"))
    desktop = home / "Desktop"

    # Handle OneDrive redirected desktop on Windows if present
    onedrive_desktop = home / "OneDrive" / "Desktop"
    if onedrive_desktop.exists():
        return onedrive_desktop

    if desktop.exists():
        return desktop

    return home


def create_windows_shortcut(desktop_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Create Windows .lnk shortcut and fallback .bat launcher."""
    shortcut_path = desktop_dir / "JARVIS Assistant.lnk"
    bat_path = desktop_dir / "Launch JARVIS.bat"

    python_exe = sys.executable
    pythonw_exe = Path(python_exe).parent / "pythonw.exe"
    target_exe = str(pythonw_exe if pythonw_exe.exists() else python_exe)

    icon_path = str(ICON_ICO if ICON_ICO.exists() else ICON_PNG)

    # 1. Native Windows .lnk shortcut via PowerShell WScript.Shell
    ps_command = f"""
$ws = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
$s = $ws.CreateShortcut('{str(shortcut_path)}')
$s.TargetPath = '{target_exe}'
$s.Arguments = '"{str(MAIN_PY)}"'
$s.WorkingDirectory = '{str(BASE_DIR)}'
$s.IconLocation = '{icon_path}'
$s.Description = 'J.A.R.V.I.S. 2.0 Autonomous OS Agent & Holographic HUD'
$s.Save()
"""
    try:
        subprocess.run(
            ["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command", ps_command],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )
        print(f"[OK] Created Windows Shortcut: {shortcut_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[!] PowerShell shortcut creation notice: {e}")

    # 2. Reliable .bat launcher in case LNK creation is restricted
    bat_content = f"""@echo off
title JARVIS 2.0 Assistant
cd /d "{str(BASE_DIR)}"
start "" "{target_exe}" "{str(MAIN_PY)}"
"""
    try:
        with open(bat_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(bat_content)
        print(f"[OK] Created Batch Launcher: {bat_path}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[!] Batch launcher creation notice: {e}")

    return shortcut_path


def create_linux_desktop_file(desktop_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Create Linux .desktop application launcher."""
    desktop_file = desktop_dir / "jarvis.desktop"
    icon_path = str(ICON_PNG if ICON_PNG.exists() else ICON_ICO)

    content = f"""[Desktop Entry]
Version=2.0
Type=Application
Name=J.A.R.V.I.S. 2.0
Comment=Autonomous OS Agent & Holographic HUD
Exec={sys.executable} "{str(MAIN_PY)}"
Path={str(BASE_DIR)}
Icon={icon_path}
Terminal=false
Categories=Utility;Development;
"""
    with open(desktop_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(content)

    os.chmod(desktop_file, 0o755)
    print(f"[OK] Created Linux Desktop Entry: {desktop_file}")

    apps_dir = Path(os.path.expanduser("~")) / ".local" / "share" / "applications"
    if apps_dir.exists():
        app_file = apps_dir / "jarvis.desktop"
        with open(app_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(content)
        os.chmod(app_file, 0o755)
        print(f"[OK] Added to Applications menu: {app_file}")

    return desktop_file


def create_macos_command_file(desktop_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Create macOS clickable .command launcher."""
    cmd_file = desktop_dir / "JARVIS.command"
    content = f"""#!/bin/bash
cd "{str(BASE_DIR)}"
"{sys.executable}" "{str(MAIN_PY)}"
"""
    with open(cmd_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(content)

    os.chmod(cmd_file, 0o755)
    print(f"[OK] Created macOS Command Launcher: {cmd_file}")
    return cmd_file


def main():
    os_name = platform.system()
    desktop = get_desktop_dir()

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"JARVIS 2.0 Desktop Launcher Generator (OS: {os_name})")
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"Target Project: {BASE_DIR}")
    print(f"Main Script:    {MAIN_PY}")
    print(f"Python Binary:  {sys.executable}")
    print(f"Desktop Folder: {desktop}\n")

    if not MAIN_PY.exists():
        print(f"[Error] main.py not found at {MAIN_PY}")
        sys.exit(1)

    # 1. Programmatically Generate Arc-Cyan HUD Icon
    print("[1/2] Generating Geometric Arc-Cyan HUD Icons...")
    try:
        generate_geometric_hud_icon(ICON_ICO, ICON_PNG)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[!] Icon generation fallback notice: {e}")

    # 2. Generate OS-Specific Desktop Launcher
    print("\n[2/2] Generating Desktop Shortcuts...")
    if os_name == "Windows":
        shortcut = create_windows_shortcut(desktop)
    elif os_name == "Linux":
        shortcut = create_linux_desktop_file(desktop)
    elif os_name == "Darwin":
        shortcut = create_macos_command_file(desktop)
    else:
        shortcut = create_macos_command_file(desktop)

    print("\n[SUCCESS] Desktop application launcher and HUD icons complete!")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

<file path="config/settings.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Configuration and Environment Settings for JARVIS 2.0
Loads environment variables, manages persistent API Vault, and seeds credentials.
"""

import json
import os
import socket
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from urllib.parse import urlparse
from dotenv import load_dotenv

# Root Directory of the Project
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

# Load .env file from root
ENV_FILE = BASE_DIR / ".env"
load_dotenv(dotenv_path=ENV_FILE)

# Directory & File Paths
CONFIG_DIR = BASE_DIR / "config"
API_VAULT_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "api_vault.json"
MEETINGS_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "meetings.json"
ALARMS_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "alarms.json"
SKILLS_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "skills.json"
MCP_SERVERS_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "mcp_servers.json"
UI_DIR = BASE_DIR / "ui"
UI_DIST_DIR = UI_DIR / "dist"
STYLES_FILE = UI_DIR / "styles.qss"
ASSETS_DIR = UI_DIR / "assets"
BROWSER_PROFILE_DIR = BASE_DIR / ".browser_profile"
QA_CACHE_FILE = BASE_DIR / "qa_cache.json"

# Server Settings
MEETING_DASHBOARD_PORT = int(os.getenv("MEETING_DASHBOARD_PORT", "5050"))
MEETING_DASHBOARD_HOST = os.getenv("MEETING_DASHBOARD_HOST", "127.0.0.1")

# Speech & TTS Settings
TTS_NEURAL_VOICE = os.getenv("JARVIS_VOICE", "en-GB-RyanNeural")
TTS_FALLBACK_RATE = int(os.getenv("JARVIS_TTS_RATE", "175"))
MIC_PHRASE_TIME_LIMIT = 5
MIC_TIMEOUT = 5

# Legacy / Environment Fallbacks
WEATHER_API_KEY = os.getenv("WEATHER_API_KEY", "").strip()
GEMINI_API_KEY = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "").strip()
GROQ_API_KEY = os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY", "").strip()
GROQ_MODEL = os.getenv("GROQ_MODEL", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile").strip()
GEMINI_MODEL = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-2.5-flash").strip()
JARVIS_FORCE_ONLINE = os.getenv("JARVIS_FORCE_ONLINE", "false").lower() == "true"

# Global reentrant lock for vault file operations
_vault_lock = threading.RLock()

DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE = {
    "autonomous_trust_mode": False,
    "workspace_path": str(BASE_DIR),
    "roles": {
        "intent_router": {"provider": "gemini", "model": "gemini-2.5-flash"},
        "qa_chat": {"provider": "groq", "model": "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"},
        "vision_agent": {"provider": "gemini", "model": "gemini-2.5-flash"},
        "offline_fallback": {"provider": "ollama", "model": "qwen2.5:7b"},
    },
    "providers": {
        "gemini": {
            "keys": [],
            "active_index": 0,
            "models": ["gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.5-pro"],
        },
        "groq": {
            "keys": [],
            "active_index": 0,
            "models": ["llama-3.3-70b-versatile", "llama-3.1-8b-instant"],
        },
        "anthropic": {
            "keys": [],
            "active_index": 0,
            "models": ["claude-3-5-sonnet-latest"],
        },
        "openrouter": {
            "base_url": "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
            "keys": [],
            "active_index": 0,
            "models": ["openai/gpt-4o-mini", "deepseek/deepseek-chat"],
        },
        "ollama": {
            "base_url": "http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1",
            "keys": ["ollama"],
            "active_index": 0,
            "models": ["qwen2.5:7b", "llama3.2:latest"],
        },
        "custom_endpoints": [
            {
                "id": "custom-default",
                "name": "Custom OpenAI Endpoint",
                "base_url": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
                "api_key": "",
                "model": "gpt-4o-mini",
                "available_models": ["gpt-4o-mini", "gpt-4o"],
            }
        ],
    },
    "services": {
        "openweathermap": "",
    },
}


def mask_key(key: str) -> str:
    """Masks key for secure UI presentation."""
    if not key:
        return ""
    key = key.strip()
    if len(key) <= 8:
        return "•" * len(key)
    return f"{key[:4]}{'•' * (len(key) - 8)}{key[-4:]}"


def load_vault() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Loads api_vault.json, seeding from .env if needed."""
    with _vault_lock:
        if not API_VAULT_FILE.exists():
            data = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE))
            API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        else:
            try:
                data = json.loads(API_VAULT_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            except Exception:
                data = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE))

        # Ensure all baseline keys exist
        for key, val in DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE.items():
            if key not in data:
                data[key] = val

        # Ensure providers exist
        if "providers" not in data:
            data["providers"] = {}
        for prov, pconf in DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE["providers"].items():
            if prov not in data["providers"]:
                data["providers"][prov] = pconf
            else:
                if isinstance(pconf, dict):
                    for subk, subv in pconf.items():
                        if subk not in data["providers"][prov]:
                            data["providers"][prov][subk] = subv

        # Seed from .env
        modified = False
        gemini_env = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "").strip()
        if gemini_env:
            gem_keys = data["providers"].setdefault("gemini", {}).setdefault("keys", [])
            if gemini_env not in gem_keys:
                gem_keys.append(gemini_env)
                modified = True

        groq_env = os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY", "").strip()
        if groq_env:
            groq_keys = data["providers"].setdefault("groq", {}).setdefault("keys", [])
            if groq_env not in groq_keys:
                groq_keys.append(groq_env)
                modified = True

        weather_env = os.getenv("WEATHER_API_KEY", "").strip()
        if weather_env:
            current_weather = data.setdefault("services", {}).get("openweathermap", "")
            if not current_weather:
                data["services"]["openweathermap"] = weather_env
                modified = True

        weather_val = data.get("services", {}).get("openweathermap", "")
        data.setdefault("providers", {})["openweathermap"] = {
            "keys": [weather_val] if weather_val else [],
            "keys_masked": [mask_key(weather_val)] if weather_val else [],
        }

        if modified:
            try:
                API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Settings] Error saving seeded vault: {e}")

        return data


def save_vault(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Saves updated vault dictionary to disk."""
    with _vault_lock:
        API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def get_vault() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Retrieve the current vault structure."""
    return load_vault()


def get_masked_vault() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Returns a deep-copy of the vault with sensitive API keys masked."""
    raw = load_vault()
    masked = json.loads(json.dumps(raw))
    providers = masked.get("providers", {})
    for prov_name, prov_data in providers.items():
        if isinstance(prov_data, dict) and "keys" in prov_data:
            prov_data["keys_masked"] = [mask_key(k) for k in prov_data["keys"]]
        elif prov_name == "custom_endpoints" and isinstance(prov_data, list):
            for ep in prov_data:
                if "api_key" in ep:
                    ep["api_key_masked"] = mask_key(ep["api_key"])

    weather_val = masked.get("services", {}).get("openweathermap", "")
    masked.setdefault("providers", {})["openweathermap"] = {
        "keys": [weather_val] if weather_val else [],
        "keys_masked": [mask_key(weather_val)] if weather_val else [],
    }

    if "services" in masked and "openweathermap" in masked["services"]:
        masked["services"]["openweathermap_masked"] = mask_key(masked["services"]["openweathermap"])

    return masked


def get_active_key(provider: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """Returns the current active key for a given provider."""
    vault = load_vault()
    prov_data = vault.get("providers", {}).get(provider, {})
    keys = prov_data.get("keys", [])
    if not keys:
        return None
    idx = prov_data.get("active_index", 0)
    if idx >= len(keys) or idx < 0:
        idx = 0
    return keys[idx]


def rotate_provider_key(provider: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Rotates to the next key in the pool for the specified provider.
    Returns the new active key, or None if no keys exist.
    """
    with _vault_lock:
        vault = load_vault()
        prov_data = vault.get("providers", {}).get(provider, {})
        keys = prov_data.get("keys", [])
        if not keys:
            return None
        current_idx = prov_data.get("active_index", 0)
        new_idx = (current_idx + 1) % len(keys)
        prov_data["active_index"] = new_idx
        API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return keys[new_idx]


def add_provider_key(provider: str, key: str) -> bool:
    """Adds a key to the specified provider's pool."""
    key = key.strip()
    if not key:
        return False
    with _vault_lock:
        vault = load_vault()
        if provider == "openweathermap":
            vault.setdefault("services", {})["openweathermap"] = key
            API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            return True

        prov_data = vault.setdefault("providers", {}).setdefault(provider, {"keys": [], "active_index": 0})
        keys = prov_data.setdefault("keys", [])
        if key not in keys:
            keys.append(key)
            API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return True


def remove_provider_key(provider: str, key_or_index: Any = None, key_index: Optional[int] = None) -> bool:
    """Removes a key from the specified provider's pool by key string, masked string, or index."""
    with _vault_lock:
        vault = load_vault()
        if provider == "openweathermap":
            vault.setdefault("services", {})["openweathermap"] = ""
            API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            return True

        prov_data = vault.get("providers", {}).get(provider, {})
        keys = prov_data.get("keys", [])

        # Check explicit key_index or integer key_or_index
        idx = key_index if key_index is not None else (key_or_index if isinstance(key_or_index, int) else None)
        if idx is not None and 0 <= idx < len(keys):
            keys.pop(idx)
            prov_data["active_index"] = 0
            API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            return True

        # String match raw or masked
        target_str = str(key_or_index).strip() if key_or_index is not None else ""
        if target_str:
            match_idx = None
            for i, k in enumerate(keys):
                if k == target_str or mask_key(k) == target_str:
                    match_idx = i
                    break
            if match_idx is not None:
                keys.pop(match_idx)
                prov_data["active_index"] = 0
                API_VAULT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(vault, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
                return True

        return False


def get_role_target(role: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Returns (provider, model) assigned to a given role.
    Fallback to defaults if not specified.
    """
    vault = load_vault()
    roles = vault.get("roles", {})
    if role in roles:
        return roles[role].get("provider", "gemini"), roles[role].get("model", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    default_role = DEFAULT_VAULT_STRUCTURE["roles"].get(role, {"provider": "gemini", "model": "gemini-2.5-flash"})
    return default_role["provider"], default_role["model"]


# ── Background Connectivity Monitor (Non-Blocking 0ms Cached Check) ───
_online_state: bool = True
_last_check_time: float = 0.0
_consecutive_failures: int = 0
_connectivity_lock = threading.Lock()
_connectivity_thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None


def _check_connectivity_once() -> bool:
    """
    Checks if any configured custom endpoint or local server (127.0.0.1 / localhost)
    is reachable, OR checks multiple reliable hosts (1.1.1.1:443, 8.8.8.8:53, api.groq.com:443)
    with a 1.5s timeout.
    """
    # 1. First, check if any configured custom endpoint or local server (127.0.0.1 / localhost) is reachable
    try:
        vault = load_vault()
        endpoints = vault.get("providers", {}).get("custom_endpoints", [])
        for ep in endpoints:
            base_url = ep.get("base_url", "")
            if "127.0.0.1" in base_url or "localhost" in base_url:
                parsed = urlparse(base_url)
                host = parsed.hostname or "127.0.0.1"
                port = parsed.port or (443 if parsed.scheme == "https" else 80)
                try:
                    s = socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=1.5)
                    s.close()
                    return True
                except Exception:
                    pass

        # Also check ollama if configured with localhost / 127.0.0.1
        ollama_url = vault.get("providers", {}).get("ollama", {}).get("base_url", "")
        if "127.0.0.1" in ollama_url or "localhost" in ollama_url:
            parsed = urlparse(ollama_url)
            host = parsed.hostname or "127.0.0.1"
            port = parsed.port or 11434
            try:
                s = socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=1.5)
                s.close()
                return True
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2. Check multiple reliable hosts with a 1.5s timeout
    reliable_hosts = [
        ("1.1.1.1", 443),
        ("8.8.8.8", 53),
        ("api.groq.com", 443),
    ]
    for host, port in reliable_hosts:
        try:
            s = socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=1.5)
            s.close()
            return True
        except Exception:
            continue

    return False


def _connectivity_worker() -> None:
    global _online_state, _last_check_time, _consecutive_failures
    while True:
        try:
            success = _check_connectivity_once()
            with _connectivity_lock:
                _last_check_time = time.time()
                if success:
                    _consecutive_failures = 0
                    _online_state = True
                else:
                    _consecutive_failures += 1
                    # 3-strike hysteresis: only flip _online_state to False if 3 consecutive checks fail completely!
                    if _consecutive_failures >= 3:
                        _online_state = False
        except Exception:
            pass
        time.sleep(20)


def start_connectivity_monitor() -> None:
    global _connectivity_thread
    with _connectivity_lock:
        if _connectivity_thread is None or not _connectivity_thread.is_alive():
            _connectivity_thread = threading.Thread(
                target=_connectivity_worker,
                daemon=True,
                name="Connectivity-Monitor",
            )
            _connectivity_thread.start()


def is_internet_connected(timeout: float = 1.0) -> bool:
    """
    Returns cached online state instantly in 0ms without performing network I/O
    on the calling thread.
    """
    if JARVIS_FORCE_ONLINE:
        return True
    return _online_state


# Initialize vault on module import
_initial_vault = load_vault()

# Start background connectivity monitor
start_connectivity_monitor()

<file path="main.py">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
JARVIS 2.0 Main Entry Point
Launches the FastAPI backend server, background APScheduler, and VoiceListener,
then opens the Marvel Holographic HUD inside a native PyWebView frameless window.
Supports --cli for headless terminal-only mode.
"""

import sys
import os
import socket
import sys
import threading
import time
import uvicorn

# Ensure project root is in sys.path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

from automation.server import app as fastapi_app
from audio.voice_listener import voice_listener
from audio.tts_engine import tts_engine
from core.engine import engine

class JarvisWindowApi:
    def __init__(self):
        self.window = None

    def set_mini_mode(self, enabled: bool):
        if not self.window:
            return False
        try:
            if enabled:
                self.window.resize(520, 140)
                self.window.on_top = True
            else:
                self.window.resize(1360, 860)
                self.window.on_top = False
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[JarvisWindowApi] set_mini_mode error: {e}")
            return False


def wait_for_server(host: str = "127.0.0.1", port: int = 5050, timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
    """Polls until the FastAPI Uvicorn server is accepting TCP connections."""
    start = time.time()
    while time.time() - start < timeout:
        try:
            with socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=0.2):
                return True
        except (OSError, ConnectionRefusedError):
            time.sleep(0.1)
    return False


def start_backend():
    """Runs the FastAPI server in a background thread."""
    uvicorn.run(fastapi_app, host="127.0.0.1", port=5050, log_level="warning")

def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print(" J.A.R.V.I.S. 2.0 — Autonomous OS Agent & Holographic HUD")
    print("=" * 60)

    # 1. Start FastAPI Backend in background thread
    server_thread = threading.Thread(target=start_backend, daemon=True, name="FastAPI-Server")
    server_thread.start()

    # Wait for Uvicorn server to actually start listening on 127.0.0.1:5050
    if wait_for_server("127.0.0.1", 5050, timeout=10.0):
        print("[Main] Backend server operational at http://127.0.0.1:5050")
    else:
        print("[Main] Backend server connection timeout; proceeding with caution.")

    # 2. Wire Meeting Handler (single source of truth on engine — no duplicate instances)
    meeting_handler = getattr(engine, "meeting_handler", None)
    print("[Main] Meeting scheduler & Playwright attendant online")

    # Check CLI mode flag
    if "--cli" in sys.argv:
        voice_listener.start()
        print("[Main] Voice listener active")
        print("[Main] Running in CLI terminal mode. Type commands below (Ctrl+C to exit):")
        try:
            while True:
                q = input("\nOperator > ")
                if q.lower() in ("exit", "quit", "shutdown"):
                    break
                res = engine.process_command(q, source="text")
                print(f"JARVIS > {res.get('text', '')}")
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            pass
        finally:
            voice_listener.stop()
            if meeting_handler:
                meeting_handler.shutdown()
            print("\n[Main] JARVIS shutdown complete.")
        return

    # 4. Launch Native PyWebView Desktop HUD Window
    try:
        import webview
        print("[Main] Launching native PyWebView Desktop HUD...")
        
        js_api = JarvisWindowApi()
        # Create frameless/styled native window pointing to FastAPI server
        window = webview.create_window(
            title="J.A.R.V.I.S. 2.0",
            url="http://127.0.0.1:5050",
            width=1360,
            height=860,
            min_size=(400, 120),
            background_color="#060A12",
            resizable=True,
            confirm_close=False,
            js_api=js_api,
        )
        js_api.window = window

        # Delay starting voice_listener until 1.5 seconds after GUI window creation
        threading.Timer(1.5, voice_listener.start).start()

        webview.start(debug=False)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Main] PyWebView launch notice ({e}). Falling back to browser app mode...")
        import webbrowser
        webbrowser.open("http://127.0.0.1:5050")
        try:
            while server_thread.is_alive():
                time.sleep(1)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            pass
    finally:
        voice_listener.stop()
        if meeting_handler:
            meeting_handler.shutdown()
        print("[Main] JARVIS shutdown complete.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# Environment variables for JARVIS
# Copy this file to .env and add your actual API keys

# OpenWeatherMap API Key (for weather feature)
# Get yours free at: https://openweathermap.org/api
WEATHER_API_KEY=your_weather_api_key_here

# Google Gemini API Key (for AI-powered responses)
# Get yours free at: https://aistudio.google.com/app/apikey
# Free tier: 1,500 requests/day, 15 requests/minute
GEMINI_API_KEY=your_gemini_api_key_here

<file path="README.md">
# JARVIS 2.0 — Autonomous OS Agent & Holographic HUD

> **Just A Rather Very Intelligent System**  
> Autonomous AI Agent with Marvel Arc-Cyan Holographic HUD, FastAPI + WebSocket Engine, Multi-Key Dynamic Vault, 3-Tier OS Actuator, and Playwright Meeting Attendant.

JARVIS 2.0 is an autonomous desktop OS agent and voice/text companion inspired by Tony Stark's AI assistant from Iron Man. Featuring a React + Vite + Tailwind CSS tactical HUD served inside a native PyWebView desktop container, sub-second local deterministic triage, autonomous 3-tier OS action engine, Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool integration, live meeting captions with AI summaries, and multi-provider LLM failover routing.

---

## Interface Overview

```
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| [J.A.R.V.I.S. 2.0] HOLOGRAPHIC TACTICAL HUD            [ONLINE]  [CORE STATUS: OPTIMAL] |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| [MIC: MUTED / ACTIVE]  [TTS: ACTIVE / SILENT]  [TRUST MODE: ON/OFF]   [MINI-HUD GHOST]  |
+-------------------------------------------------------------+---------------------------+
| TABS: [CHAT STREAM] [OS AGENT] [MEETINGS] [API VAULT]       |    ARC TELEMETRY GAUGES   |
|       [SKILLS & MCP] [SYSTEM & ALARMS]                      |                           |
|                                                             |    ( CPU LOAD: 14.2% )    |
|  YOU [TEXT / VOICE]                                         |    ( RAM USAGE: 42.1% )   |
|  "Open Chrome and navigate to GitHub"                       |    ( BATTERY: 95% AC )    |
|                                                             |                           |
|  J.A.R.V.I.S. [TIER 1/3 ACTUATOR]                           |   NEXT SCHEDULED MEETING  |
|  "Launching Chrome and navigating to repository, sir."      |   "Team Sprint Sync"      |
|                                                             |   Today at 3:00 PM        |
|  [SET-OF-MARK VISION PREVIEW: Active Arc Cyan Tags]         |   [ Join Now Button ]     |
|                                                             |                           |
+-------------------------------------------------------------+---------------------------+
| [Type a command, query, or script...                                 ]  [ EXECUTE ]     |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

---

## Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Marvel Holographic HUD** | Pure geometric Arc Cyan (`#00E5FF`) and Reactor Gold HUD built with React, Vite, and Tailwind CSS |
| **Native PyWebView Container** | Frameless, modern native desktop window with browser fallback at `http://127.0.0.1:5050` |
| **FastAPI + WebSocket Engine** | Real-time bi-directional telemetry and command streaming on port 5050 |
| **Multi-Key API Vault** | Hot-swappable key rotation across Gemini, Groq, Anthropic, OpenRouter, and Ollama with custom OpenAI endpoints |
| **3-Tier Dynamic OS Agent** | Tier 1 Safe Sandboxed Python, Tier 2 MCP Tools, and Tier 3 Set-of-Mark (SoM) Vision Actuator |
| **Safety Interceptor** | Cryptographic confirmation prompts with countdown timer for potentially destructive OS actions |
| **Automated Meeting Attendant** | Headed Playwright browser attendant with live caption feed, auto-mute interlock, and AI post-meeting summaries |
| **Workspace RAG & Copilot** | Local codebase indexing, semantic search, and clipboard summarization/bug fixing |
| **Custom Voice Skills & MCP** | Interactive "Teach Mode" voice macro recorder and dynamic MCP server registry |
| **Cross-Platform Shortcut** | One-click desktop launcher generator for Windows (.lnk & .bat), Linux (.desktop), and macOS |

---

## Quick Start

### 1. Prerequisites

- **Python 3.10+**
- **Node.js 18+** & npm (for UI compilation)
- **Google Chrome** installed (for meeting automation)
- Microphone and speakers/headphones

### 2. Installation

```bash
git clone https://github.com/yourusername/JARVIS.git
cd JARVIS

# Python dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt
python -m playwright install chromium

# Frontend HUD build
cd ui
npm install
npm run build
cd ..
```

### 3. Launch Modes

#### Default: Native Desktop Holographic HUD
```bash
python main.py
```
Launches the FastAPI backend and opens the native PyWebView frameless window (1360x860) with auto-fallback to `http://127.0.0.1:5050`.

#### Headless / Terminal CLI Mode
```bash
python main.py --cli
```
Runs in terminal-only mode for command-line operators (type commands, `exit` to quit).

---

## Generating Desktop Application Icon & Shortcut

To programmatically generate the geometric Arc-Cyan HUD icon (`jarvis.png` / `jarvis.ico`) and install native desktop launchers:

```bash
python scripts/create_desktop_icon.py
```

- **Arc-Cyan HUD Icon:** Programmatically rendered via Pillow at 1024x1024 supersampling with concentric segmented arcs, tick-marks, and central reactor core (`ui/assets/jarvis.ico` and `ui/assets/jarvis.png`).
- **Windows:** Creates `JARVIS Assistant.lnk` and `Launch JARVIS.bat` on your Desktop with the custom icon.
- **Linux:** Creates `jarvis.desktop` on your Desktop and installs it into `~/.local/share/applications/`.
- **macOS:** Creates `JARVIS.command` on your Desktop with executable permissions.

---

## Dual Audio State Machine & Interlocking

JARVIS separates audio ingestion from audio synthesis:

1. **Microphone Toggle (`MIC`)**:
   - `MIC: MUTED` (Gray): Background speech listener is paused. No microphone data is processed.
   - `MIC: LISTENING` (Cyan): Background listener active, processing speech through Groq Whisper / Google STT.
2. **TTS Output Toggle (`VOICE`)**:
   - `VOICE: ACTIVE` (Cyan): Responses are spoken aloud via `edge-tts` (neural British voice) or `pyttsx3`.
   - `VOICE: MUTED` (Gray): Silent mode. JARVIS responds exclusively via text bubbles in the Chat Feed.
3. **Meeting-Aware Audio Interlocking**:
   - When a Google Meet or Teams call is joined, the microphone is **automatically muted** to prevent room audio from triggering commands.
   - **Manual Override**: The user can click the `MIC` button at any time during an active call to unmute.
   - When the meeting ends, the microphone automatically restores to its previous active state unless overridden.

---

## GUI Shortcuts & Controls

- **Enter**: Submit query from input field.
- **Up Arrow**: Recall previous command from history.
- **Down Arrow**: Navigate forward in command history.
- **Clear Chat**: Clears message history from the screen.
- **Meeting Dashboard**: Opens `http://localhost:5050` in your default browser.
- **Join Now**: Joins the next scheduled meeting immediately from the Telemetry dock.

---

## Commands Reference

Both text queries and voice commands are accepted:

### Meeting Commands
- `schedule meeting Project Sync at 3 PM https://meet.google.com/abc-defg-hij`
- `what meetings do i have today` / `list meetings`
- `cancel meeting Project Sync`
- `join meeting https://teams.microsoft.com/...`
- `leave meeting`
- `open meeting dashboard`

### System Controls & Hardware
- `set volume to 50%` / `mute volume` / `volume up`
- `set brightness to 80%`
- `system status` / `check battery` / `cpu usage`
- `set timer for 5 minutes` / `stopwatch`

### Daily Utilities & General Knowledge
- `what time is it` / `what is the date`
- `open Chrome` / `open Spotify` / `open Notepad`
- `play lo-fi hip hop on youtube` / `pause music` / `skip song`
- `weather in New York` / `forecast for London`
- `search for quantum computing`
- `who directed Interstellar?` (answered via Groq Llama 3.1)

---

## Project Structure

```
jarvis-assistant/
├── main.py                     # Entry point (launches PySide6 GUI by default; --cli fallback)
├── config/
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── settings.py             # Config, environment loader, and path definitions
│   └── meetings.json           # Persistent meeting schedules storage
├── core/
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── engine.py               # Core orchestrator & InputGateway
│   ├── voice_listener.py       # Thread-safe SpeechRecognition worker & audio interlock
│   └── tts_engine.py           # Unified edge-tts neural voice & pyttsx3 synthesis engine
├── handlers/                   # Consolidated command execution modules
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── system_controls.py      # Volume and brightness adjustments
│   ├── system_info.py          # Battery and CPU load monitoring
│   ├── applications.py         # System application launcher
│   ├── music.py                # YouTube and Spotify playback controller
│   ├── web_search.py           # Google search handler
│   ├── weather.py              # OpenWeatherMap API integration
│   ├── date_time.py            # Clock and calendar handler
│   ├── alarm.py                # Alarms and reminders
│   └── qa_handler.py           # Groq (Llama 3.1) and Gemini knowledge engine
├── automation/
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── meeting_bot.py          # Playwright browser driver (Google Meet & Teams)
│   ├── meeting_scheduler.py    # APScheduler background runner & JSON storage
│   ├── schedule_interface.py  # FastAPI meeting dashboard at :5050
│   └── meeting_handler.py      # High-level meeting management adapter
├── ui/
│   ├── __init__.py
│   ├── app_window.py           # PySide6 MainWindow layout, signals, & QThreadPool
│   ├── styles.qss              # Dark JARVIS HUD theme (slate/cyan palette)
│   ├── components/             # Reusable UI widgets
│   │   ├── __init__.py
│   │   ├── chat_feed.py        # Responsive message bubbles with auto-scroll
│   │   ├── stat_cards.py       # Live telemetry mini-cards and meeting banner
│   │   └── input_bar.py        # Text input field with command history recall
│   └── assets/                 # Custom Arc Reactor icons (PNG, ICO)
├── scripts/
│   └── create_desktop_icon.py  # Cross-platform desktop shortcut generator
├── tests/
│   ├── test_gateway.py         # Loop 1 verification (InputGateway offline routing)
│   ├── test_audio_state.py     # Loop 2 verification (Audio state machine & interlocking)
│   └── test_ui_window.py       # Loop 3 & 4 verification (PySide6 GUI & async signals)
├── requirements.txt            # Python dependencies
├── .env.example                # Environment variables template
├── .gitignore                  # Git ignore rules (protects credentials & browser profile)
└── README.md                   # Project documentation
```

---

## Verification Test Suites

Run the automated verification test suites across all loops:

```bash
# Verify Loop 1: InputGateway & Modular Handlers
python tests/test_gateway.py

# Verify Loop 2: Concurrency & Audio State Machine
python tests/test_audio_state.py

# Verify Loop 3 & 4: PySide6 GUI & Async Wiring
python tests/test_ui_window.py

# Verify Loop 5: Desktop Icon Generation
python scripts/create_desktop_icon.py
```
